+"""gcloud-only cloud operations. Dry-run never invokes gcloud or reads credentials."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+import re
+import shlex
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import tempfile
+from pathlib import Path
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+BILLING = os.environ.get("HILL_CLIMB_BILLING_ACCOUNT", "")  # set it before setup
+
+
+def action(message):
+    raise SystemExit("USER ACTION NEEDED: " + message)
+
+
+class Gcloud:
+    def __init__(self, args):
+        self.args = args
+        self.exe = shutil.which("gcloud") or shutil.which("gcloud.cmd")
+        if args.execute and not self.exe:
+            action("Install Google Cloud CLI and run gcloud auth login with your personal Gmail.")
+
+    def call(self, *argv, optional=False, data=None):
+        command = [self.exe or "gcloud", *argv, "--project", self.args.project, "--quiet"]
+        if not self.args.execute:
+            print("DRY RUN: " + shlex.join(["gcloud", *command[1:]]))
+            return ""
+        result = subprocess.run(command, input=data, capture_output=True)
+        if result.returncode:
+            err = result.stderr.decode(errors="replace")
+            if optional and any(x in err.lower() for x in ("not_found", "not found", "does not exist", "404")):
+                return None
+            # Do not forward upstream stderr, especially on credential-upload failures.
+            action(f"gcloud {' '.join(argv[:3])} failed. Check login, billing link to {BILLING}, "
+                   "API terms, IAM permissions, regional Spot CPU quota and capacity in your own gcloud console.")
+        return result.stdout.decode().strip()
+
+    def ensure(self, describe, create):
+        if not self.args.execute:
+            print("If missing:")
+            self.call(*create)
+        elif self.call(*describe, optional=True) is None:
+            self.call(*create)
+
+
+def bindings(gc, resource_args, account, roles):
+    for role in roles:
+        gc.call(*resource_args, "--member", "serviceAccount:" + account, "--role", role)
+
+
+def setup(args, gc):
+    p, r, bucket = args.project, args.region, "gs://" + args.bucket
+    worker = f"hill-climb-worker@{p}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
+    builder = f"hill-climb-builder@{p}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"
+    print(f"Will configure project {p}, region {r}, repository hill-climb, bucket {bucket}, "
+          f"worker/builder accounts, Dream/Claude secret containers and a {args.budget} USD budget alert.")
+    print("Project creation and billing linking are manual. Existing secret versions are never rotated.")
+    if args.execute:
+        account = gc.call("auth", "list", "--filter=status:ACTIVE", "--format=value(account)")
+        if not account:
+            action("Run gcloud auth login with the Gmail owning the billing account.")
+        gc.call("projects", "describe", p)
+        billing = json.loads(gc.call("billing", "projects", "describe", p, "--format=json"))
+        if not billing.get("billingEnabled") or not billing.get("billingAccountName", "").endswith(BILLING):
+            action(f"Link project {p} to billing account {BILLING} in the console, then rerun setup.")
+    gc.call("services", "enable", "batch.googleapis.com", "compute.googleapis.com",
+            "artifactregistry.googleapis.com", "cloudbuild.googleapis.com", "secretmanager.googleapis.com",
+            "storage.googleapis.com", "billingbudgets.googleapis.com", "logging.googleapis.com",
+            "iam.googleapis.com")
+    gc.ensure(["artifacts", "repositories", "describe", "hill-climb", "--location", r],
+              ["artifacts", "repositories", "create", "hill-climb", "--location", r, "--repository-format=docker"])
+    gc.ensure(["storage", "buckets", "describe", bucket],
+              ["storage", "buckets", "create", bucket, "--location", r,
+               "--uniform-bucket-level-access", "--public-access-prevention"])
+    for name, email in [("hill-climb-worker", worker), ("hill-climb-builder", builder)]:
+        gc.ensure(["iam", "service-accounts", "describe", email],
+                  ["iam", "service-accounts", "create", name])
+    bindings(gc, ["projects", "add-iam-policy-binding", p], worker,
+             ["roles/batch.agentReporter", "roles/logging.logWriter"])
+    bindings(gc, ["projects", "add-iam-policy-binding", p], builder, ["roles/logging.logWriter"])
+    bindings(gc, ["artifacts", "repositories", "add-iam-policy-binding", "hill-climb", "--location", r],
+             worker, ["roles/artifactregistry.reader"])
+    bindings(gc, ["artifacts", "repositories", "add-iam-policy-binding", "hill-climb", "--location", r],
+             builder, ["roles/artifactregistry.writer"])
+    bindings(gc, ["storage", "buckets", "add-iam-policy-binding", bucket], worker,
+             ["roles/storage.objectCreator", "roles/storage.objectViewer"])   # gcloud storage cp needs objects.get
+    bindings(gc, ["storage", "buckets", "add-iam-policy-binding", bucket], builder,
+             ["roles/storage.objectViewer"])
+    for secret in ("dream-api-key", "anthropic-api-key", "openrouter-api-key"):
+        gc.ensure(["secrets", "describe", secret],
+                  ["secrets", "create", secret, "--replication-policy=automatic"])
+        bindings(gc, ["secrets", "add-iam-policy-binding", secret], worker, ["roles/secretmanager.secretAccessor"])
+    budget_name = "hill-climb-sims-monthly"
+    existing = gc.call("billing", "budgets", "list", "--billing-account", BILLING,
+                       "--filter=displayName=" + budget_name, "--format=value(name)")
+    if not existing:
+        gc.call("billing", "budgets", "create", "--billing-account", BILLING,
+                "--display-name", budget_name, "--budget-amount", f"{args.budget}USD",
+                "--filter-projects", "projects/" + p,
+                "--threshold-rule=percent=0.5", "--threshold-rule=percent=0.9",
+                "--threshold-rule=percent=1.0")
+    # Upload only through the user's authenticated gcloud. Values never enter argv or a temp file.
+    if args.execute:
+        versions = gc.call("secrets", "versions", "list", "dream-api-key", "--filter=state:ENABLED",
+                           "--format=value(name)", "--limit=1")
+        if not versions:
+            credentials = Path.home() / ".dreamlabs/credentials"
+            if not credentials.is_file():
+                action("Save the Dream setup block in ~/.dreamlabs/credentials, then rerun setup.")
+            key = None
+            for line in credentials.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+                name, sep, value = line.removeprefix("export ").partition("=")
+                if sep and name.strip() == "DREAM_API_KEY":
+                    try:
+                        key = " ".join(shlex.split(value, comments=True))
+                    except ValueError:
+                        action("Fix DREAM_API_KEY quoting in ~/.dreamlabs/credentials.")
+            if not key:
+                action("Add DREAM_API_KEY to ~/.dreamlabs/credentials, then rerun setup.")
+            gc.call("secrets", "versions", "add", "dream-api-key", "--data-file=-", data=key.encode())
+            key = None
+        for email in (worker, builder):
+            gc.call("iam", "service-accounts", "add-iam-policy-binding", email,
+                    "--member", "user:" + account, "--role", "roles/iam.serviceAccountUser")
+    else:
+        print("DRY RUN: upload Dream key via stdin only if no enabled version; grant current user actAs on both accounts.")
+    print("Budget alerts notify billing IAM recipients; they do not cap spending.")
+
+
+def tasks(matrix, pilot=False):
+    import yaml
+    raw = yaml.safe_load(Path(matrix).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    if raw.get("scene_randomisation") != "none":
+        raise ValueError("the current scene supports only scene_randomisation: none")
+    groups = []
+    for config in raw["configs"]:
+        if not re.fullmatch(r"[a-z0-9_-]+", config["name"]):
+            raise ValueError("invalid config name")
+        if not {"--match", "--detect", "--live-guard"} <= set(config["args"]):
+            raise ValueError("matrix config must enable match, detect and live guard")
+        seeds = config["seeds"]
+        if seeds["count"] < 1:
+            raise ValueError("seed count must be positive")
+        groups.append([{**config, "seed": s} for s in range(seeds["start"], seeds["start"] + seeds["count"])])
+    # Interleave configs so a ten-match pilot covers both plans.
+    result = [g[i] for i in range(max(map(len, groups))) for g in groups if i < len(g)]
+    if pilot and len(result) < 10:
+        raise ValueError("pilot requires at least ten matrix entries")
+    return result[:10] if pilot else result
+
+
+def arm_commands(args, entries):
+    """Matrix mode (task i -> matrix entries), or one arm: --config C seeds from --seed-start, --run-args for cloud.run."""
+    if getattr(args, "config", None):
+        return ["--config", args.config, "--seed-start", str(args.seed_start), "--matches", str(args.matches_per_task),
+                *shlex.split(getattr(args, "run_args", "") or "")]
+    return ["--matches-per-task", str(args.matches_per_task), "--limit", str(len(entries))]
+
+
+def job_config(args, entries):
+    identity = hashlib.sha256(json.dumps({"tasks": entries, "judge": args.judge, "image": args.image,
+                                         "group": args.matches_per_task, "judge_models": args.judge_models,
+                                         "judge_mode": args.judge_mode, "judge_hedge": args.judge_hedge,
+                                         "dream_hedge": args.dream_hedge, "machine": args.machine_type,
+                                         "arm": [getattr(args, "config", None), getattr(args, "run_args", ""),
+                                                 getattr(args, "seed_start", 0)],
+                                         "reasoning": getattr(args, "judge_reasoning", "low")},
+                                        sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest()[:32]
+    count = (len(entries) + args.matches_per_task - 1) // args.matches_per_task
+    secret = {"DREAM_API_KEY": f"projects/{args.project}/secrets/dream-api-key/versions/latest"}
+    if args.judge == "api":
+        secret["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"] = f"projects/{args.project}/secrets/anthropic-api-key/versions/latest"
+    if args.judge == "openrouter":
+        secret["OPENROUTER_API_KEY"] = f"projects/{args.project}/secrets/openrouter-api-key/versions/latest"
+    network = ({"networkInterfaces": [{"network": "global/networks/default",
+                                       "subnetwork": f"regions/{args.region}/subnetworks/default",
+                                       "noExternalIpAddress": True}]} if args.private else None)
+    policy = {"instances": [{"policy": {"machineType": args.machine_type,
+              "provisioningModel": "SPOT", "bootDisk": {"sizeGb": 30, "type": "pd-standard"}}}],
+              "location": {"allowedLocations": ["regions/" + args.region]},
+              "serviceAccount": {"email": f"hill-climb-worker@{args.project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com"}}
+    if network:
+        policy["network"] = network
+    return {"taskGroups": [{"taskCount": count, "parallelism": min(args.parallelism, count),
+                            "taskCountPerNode": 1,
+                            "taskSpec": {"runnables": [{"container": {"imageUri": args.image,
+                                "commands": ["--matrix", "/app/cloud/matrix.yaml", "--judge", args.judge,
+                                             "--judge-models", args.judge_models, "--judge-mode", args.judge_mode,
+                                             "--judge-hedge", str(args.judge_hedge),
+                                             "--dream-hedge", str(args.dream_hedge),
+                                             *arm_commands(args, entries)]}}],
+                                "environment": {"variables": {"OUTPUT_PREFIX": f"gs://{args.bucket}/runs/{args.job}",
+                                                                "RUN_ID": args.job,
+                                                                "MATRIX_SHA256": hashlib.sha256((ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml").read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                                                                "LP_NUM_THREADS": "2", "OMP_NUM_THREADS": "2",
+                                                                "HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING":
+                                                                    getattr(args, "judge_reasoning", "low")},
+                                                "secretVariables": secret},
+                                "computeResource": {"cpuMilli": 2000, "memoryMib": 1280},
+                                "maxRetryCount": 2,
+                                # Retry Spot preemptions only. A failed match (exit 1) or failed upload (75)
+                                # is never re-run: each retry would be another paid Dream match.
+                                "lifecyclePolicies": [{"action": "FAIL_TASK",
+                                                       "actionCondition": {"exitCodes": [1, 75]}}],
+                                "maxRunDuration": str(900 * args.matches_per_task) + "s"}}],
+            "allocationPolicy": policy,
+            "logsPolicy": {"destination": "CLOUD_LOGGING"}, "labels": {"manifest": identity}}
+
+
+def build(args, gc):
+    print(f"Will submit Cloud Build from the allowlisted checkout and push {args.image}. No local Docker.")
+    if args.execute:
+        if gc.call("artifacts", "docker", "images", "describe", args.image, optional=True):
+            print("Image tag already exists; use a new --tag after changing code.")
+            return
+    digest = hashlib.sha256()
+    source = [ROOT / "sim_station.py", ROOT / "experiments/sim_match.py"]
+    for directory in ("cloud", "harness", "sim_scenes", "plans", "lessons"):
+        source.extend(path for path in (ROOT / directory).rglob("*") if path.is_file()
+                      and not {"evidence", "generated", "__pycache__"}.intersection(path.relative_to(ROOT).parts)
+                      and path.name not in ("credentials", ".env"))
+    for path in sorted(source):
+        digest.update(path.relative_to(ROOT).as_posix().encode() + b"\0" + path.read_bytes())
+    config = {"steps": [{"name": "gcr.io/cloud-builders/docker", "args": ["build", "-f", "cloud/Dockerfile",
+                       "--build-arg", "SOURCE_REVISION=" + digest.hexdigest(), "-t", args.image, "."]}],
+              "images": [args.image], "serviceAccount": f"projects/{args.project}/serviceAccounts/"
+                         f"hill-climb-builder@{args.project}.iam.gserviceaccount.com",
+              "options": {"logging": "CLOUD_LOGGING_ONLY"}, "timeout": "1800s"}
+    with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory(prefix="hill-climb-build-") as folder:
+        path = Path(folder) / "build.json"
+        path.write_text(json.dumps(config), encoding="utf-8")
+        gc.call("builds", "submit", str(ROOT), "--config", str(path),
+                "--ignore-file", str(ROOT / "cloud/gcloudignore"),
+                "--gcs-source-staging-dir", f"gs://{args.bucket}/build-source")
+
+
+def submit(args, gc):
+    entries = tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml", args.pilot)
+    if args.config:
+        config = next((x for x in tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml") if x["name"] == args.config), None)
+        if config is None:
+            raise ValueError(f"--config {args.config} is not in cloud/matrix.yaml")
+        entries = [{**config, "seed": args.seed_start + i} for i in range(args.seeds)]
+    config = job_config(args, entries)
+    print(f"Will submit Spot job {args.job}: {len(entries)} matches, "
+          f"{config['taskGroups'][0]['taskCount']} tasks, parallelism {args.parallelism}, judge {args.judge}.")
+    print(f"Each task runs {args.matches_per_task} sequential matches on {args.machine_type}; "
+          "Spot retries may repeat paid Dream inference.")
+    if args.execute:
+        old = gc.call("batch", "jobs", "describe", args.job, "--location", args.region,
+                      "--format=json", optional=True)
+        if old:
+            if json.loads(old).get("labels", {}).get("manifest") != config["labels"]["manifest"]:
+                action("Job name exists with a different matrix/image/judge. Choose another --job name.")
+            print("Job already exists with this manifest; nothing submitted.")
+            return
+        for key in ["dream-api-key"] + (["anthropic-api-key"] if args.judge == "api" else []) + (
+                ["openrouter-api-key"] if args.judge == "openrouter" else []):
+            if not gc.call("secrets", "versions", "list", key, "--filter=state:ENABLED", "--format=value(name)", "--limit=1"):
+                action(f"Upload {key} through Secret Manager in your own console before submitting.")
+        gc.call("artifacts", "docker", "images", "describe", args.image)
+    target = Path(args.output)
+    target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    target.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    print("Job config: " + str(target))
+    gc.call("batch", "jobs", "submit", args.job, "--location", args.region, "--config", str(target))
+
+
+def main():
+    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    p.add_argument("operation", choices=["setup", "build", "submit"])
+    p.add_argument("--execute", action="store_true", help="perform cloud changes; default is offline dry-run")
+    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
+    p.add_argument("--project", default="hill-climb-sims")
+    p.add_argument("--region", default="us-central1")
+    p.add_argument("--bucket", default="hill-climb-sims-results")
+    p.add_argument("--budget", type=int, default=50)
+    p.add_argument("--tag", default="cloud-v1")
+    p.add_argument("--job", default="hill-climb-pilot-v1")
+    p.add_argument("--pilot", action="store_true")
+    p.add_argument("--judge", choices=["oracle", "api", "openrouter"], default="oracle")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-models", default="google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-mode", choices=["race", "alternate"], default="race")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-hedge", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--judge-reasoning", choices=["none", "low", "medium", "high"], default="low",
+                   help="OpenRouter reasoning level for every judge model in the job")
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--private", action="store_true", help="no external IPs (needs Cloud NAT; lifts the IP quota)")
+    p.add_argument("--parallelism", type=int, default=8)
+    p.add_argument("--matches-per-task", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--config", help="one experiment arm: a matrix config name (with --seeds, --seed-start)")
+    p.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=8, help="matches in the arm, seeds seed-start .. +seeds-1")
+    p.add_argument("--seed-start", type=int, default=0)
+    p.add_argument("--run-args", default="", help="extra cloud.run flags for every match of the arm, one string")
+    p.add_argument("--machine-type", default="e2-highcpu-2")
+    p.add_argument("--output", default="cloud/generated/job.json")
+    args = p.parse_args()
+    if args.execute and args.dry_run:
+        p.error("choose --execute or --dry-run")
+    if min(args.parallelism, args.matches_per_task, args.budget) < 1:
+        p.error("parallelism, matches-per-task and budget must be positive")
+    args.image = f"{args.region}-docker.pkg.dev/{args.project}/hill-climb/sim:{args.tag}"
+    try:
+        globals()[args.operation](args, Gcloud(args))
+    except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
+        action(str(exc))
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""Run K sequential matches per Batch task, upload logs, then publish completion last."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import hashlib
+import json
+import os
+import shutil
+import signal
+import subprocess
+import sys
+import threading
+import urllib.request
+import uuid
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from cloud.control import tasks
+
+
+UPLOAD_FAILED = 75  # control.py marks this exit code FAIL_TASK, so Batch never re-runs a paid match for it
+
+
+def redact(text):
+    for name in ("DREAM_API_KEY", "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY", "OPENROUTER_API_KEY"):
+        value = os.environ.get(name)
+        if value:
+            text = text.replace(value, "[REDACTED]")
+    return text
+
+
+METADATA_TOKEN = ("http://metadata.google.internal/computeMetadata/v1/instance/service-accounts/default/token")
+TOKEN_FILE = Path("/tmp/hill-climb-gcloud-token")
+
+
+def inherited_cloudsdk():
+    return {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if k.startswith("CLOUDSDK_")}
+
+
+def vm_token():
+    """The VM service account's access token, straight from the metadata server, or None off GCE."""
+    request = urllib.request.Request(METADATA_TOKEN, headers={"Metadata-Flavor": "Google"})
+    try:
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(request, timeout=5) as reply:
+            return json.loads(reply.read())["access_token"]
+    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError):
+        return None
+
+
+def gcloud(*args):
+    # Batch's runtime hands the container its own CLOUDSDK_* settings (a CLOUDSDK_PYTHON of /usr/bin/python3 that
+    # does not exist here, and no usable account), so drop all of them, pin this interpreter, and authenticate
+    # with the VM's own service-account token. Fetched per call: tokens expire after about an hour.
+    env = {k: v for k, v in os.environ.items() if not k.startswith("CLOUDSDK_")}
+    env["CLOUDSDK_PYTHON"] = sys.executable
+    token = vm_token()
+    if token:
+        TOKEN_FILE.write_text(token, encoding="utf-8")
+        TOKEN_FILE.chmod(0o600)
+        env["CLOUDSDK_AUTH_ACCESS_TOKEN_FILE"] = str(TOKEN_FILE)
+    result = subprocess.run(["gcloud", *args, "--quiet"], capture_output=True, env=env)
+    if result.returncode:
+        print(redact(result.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace"))[-800:], flush=True)
+        if not token:
+            print("No metadata-server token: not on a GCE VM, or the metadata server is unreachable.", flush=True)
+    return result.returncode == 0
+
+
+def put(source: Path, destination: str, recursive=False):
+    """Copy a result file or folder to gs://... (gcloud) or to a local/NFS folder (the home cluster)."""
+    if destination.startswith("gs://"):
+        return gcloud("storage", "cp", *(["--recursive"] if recursive else []), str(source), destination)
+    try:
+        target = Path(destination)
+        if recursive:
+            shutil.copytree(source, target / source.name, dirs_exist_ok=True)
+        else:
+            target.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+            shutil.copy2(source, target)
+        return True
+    except OSError as exc:
+        print(f"local copy failed: {exc}", flush=True)
+        return False
+
+
+def preflight(prefix, attempt, out):
+    """Prove this VM can upload before spending a Dream match."""
+    probe = out / f"preflight-{attempt}.json"
+    probe.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    probe.write_text(json.dumps({"attempt": attempt, "inherited_cloudsdk": inherited_cloudsdk(),
+                                 "python": sys.executable}), encoding="utf-8")
+    print(f"Inherited gcloud settings: {sorted(inherited_cloudsdk().items())}", flush=True)
+    # Twice to the same object: live snapshots and the final upload REPLACE files, which needs delete permission
+    # as well as create (8 Nov: matches ran, then their upload failed on storage.objects.delete).
+    target = f"{prefix.rstrip('/')}/_preflight/{attempt}.json"
+    if not (put(probe, target) and put(probe, target)):
+        print("USER ACTION NEEDED: upload preflight failed before any match ran; check bucket IAM and network.",
+              flush=True)
+        raise SystemExit(UPLOAD_FAILED)
+
+
+def main():
+    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    p.add_argument("--matrix", default="/app/cloud/matrix.yaml")
+    p.add_argument("--judge", choices=["oracle", "api", "openrouter"], default="oracle")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-models", default="google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-mode", choices=["race", "alternate"], default="race")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-hedge", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--matches-per-task", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--limit", type=int)
+    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("/out"))
+    p.add_argument("--config", help="optional one-config sequential seed range, outside Batch")
+    p.add_argument("--seed-start", type=int, default=0)
+    p.add_argument("--matches", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
+    p.add_argument("--preflight-only", action="store_true", help="check uploads from this VM, run no match")
+    args, run_args = p.parse_known_args()    # anything else goes to every cloud.run (an experiment arm's settings)
+    if min(args.matches_per_task, args.matches) < 1:
+        p.error("match counts must be positive")
+    entries = tasks(args.matrix)
+    expected_matrix = os.environ.get("MATRIX_SHA256")
+    if expected_matrix and hashlib.sha256(Path(args.matrix).read_bytes()).hexdigest() != expected_matrix:
+        raise SystemExit("USER ACTION NEEDED: matrix differs from built image; build a new image tag.")
+    if args.config:
+        config = next((x for x in entries if x["name"] == args.config), None)
+        if config is None:
+            p.error("unknown --config")
+        # In Batch each task takes the next seed range: task i runs seeds start + i*matches ... (one arm per job).
+        start = args.seed_start + int(os.environ.get("BATCH_TASK_INDEX", "0")) * args.matches
+        entries = [{**config, "seed": seed} for seed in range(start, start + args.matches)]
+    else:
+        entries = entries[:args.limit]
+        index = int(os.environ.get("BATCH_TASK_INDEX", "0"))
+        entries = entries[index * args.matches_per_task:(index + 1) * args.matches_per_task]
+    if not entries:
+        p.error("task index outside matrix")
+    prefix = os.environ.get("OUTPUT_PREFIX", "")
+    attempt = uuid.uuid4().hex
+    print(f"Will run {len(entries)} Dream matches with {args.judge} judge, write {args.out}, "
+          f"upload to {prefix or '(missing OUTPUT_PREFIX)'}, attempt {attempt}.", flush=True)
+    if args.dry_run:
+        print(json.dumps([{k: x[k] for k in ("name", "seed", "plan", "args")} for x in entries]))
+        return
+    if not (prefix.startswith("gs://") or Path(prefix).is_absolute()) or not os.environ.get("DREAM_API_KEY"):
+        raise SystemExit("USER ACTION NEEDED: set OUTPUT_PREFIX (gs://... or an absolute folder) and DREAM_API_KEY.")
+    if args.judge == "api" and not os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
+        raise SystemExit("USER ACTION NEEDED: upload anthropic-api-key and submit with --judge api.")
+    if args.judge == "openrouter" and not os.environ.get("OPENROUTER_API_KEY"):
+        raise SystemExit("USER ACTION NEEDED: upload openrouter-api-key and submit with --judge openrouter.")
+    preflight(prefix, attempt, args.out)
+    if args.preflight_only:
+        print("Preflight upload OK; no match run.", flush=True)
+        return
+    failed = False
+    for entry in entries:
+        key = f"{entry['name']}-{entry['seed']}"
+        folder = args.out / attempt / key
+        folder.mkdir(parents=True)
+        command = [sys.executable, "-m", "cloud.run", "--policy", "dream", "--judge", args.judge,
+                   "--plan", entry["plan"], "--seed", str(entry["seed"]), "--label", entry["name"],
+                   "--out", str(folder), "--judge-models", args.judge_models, "--judge-mode", args.judge_mode,
+                   "--judge-hedge", str(args.judge_hedge), "--dream-hedge", str(args.dream_hedge), *entry["args"],
+                   *run_args]
+        child = subprocess.Popen(command, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
+        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, lambda *_: child.terminate())
+        # Live view of the running match (cloud.run writes live/): upload it every 30 s so jobs can be watched.
+        live_stop = threading.Event()
+
+        def push_live(folder=folder, key=key):
+            while not live_stop.wait(30):
+                if (folder / "live").is_dir():
+                    put(folder / "live", f"{prefix.rstrip('/')}/{attempt}/{key}/", recursive=True)
+        live_pusher = threading.Thread(target=push_live, name="live-upload", daemon=True)
+        live_pusher.start()
+        with (folder / "worker.log").open("w", encoding="utf-8") as log:
+            for line in child.stdout:
+                log.write(redact(line))
+                log.flush()
+        code = child.wait()
+        live_stop.set()
+        live_pusher.join(timeout=60)
+        # Scrub known secrets from structured logs too, before they leave this task.
+        for path in folder.rglob("*"):
+            if path.suffix in (".json", ".jsonl", ".txt", ".log"):
+                path.write_text(redact(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")), encoding="utf-8")
+        destination = f"{prefix.rstrip('/')}/{attempt}/{key}"
+        if not put(folder, f"{prefix.rstrip('/')}/{attempt}/", recursive=True):
+            print("USER ACTION NEEDED: output upload failed; check bucket IAM and network. Local logs remain in /out.",
+                  flush=True)
+            raise SystemExit(UPLOAD_FAILED)
+        result_file = folder / "match.json"
+        complete = result_file.exists() and json.loads(result_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")).get("complete", False)
+        if code or not complete:
+            failed = True
+            print(f"Match {key} failed with exit {code}; diagnostic logs uploaded.", flush=True)
+            continue
+        marker = folder / "complete.json"
+        marker.write_text(json.dumps({"match": key, "run_id": os.environ.get("RUN_ID", "manual"),
+                                      "attempt": attempt}), encoding="utf-8")
+        if not put(marker, destination + "/complete.json"):
+            print("USER ACTION NEEDED: completion upload failed; rerun collection after checking bucket IAM.",
+                  flush=True)
+            raise SystemExit(UPLOAD_FAILED)
+    if failed:
+        raise SystemExit(1)
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+"""Use the production monitor scheduler with ground-truth answers and measured latency."""
+import hashlib
+import random
+import time
+
+from experiments.sim_match import OracleVerifier, get
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier
+
+
+class DelayedOracle(MonitorVerifier):
+    def __init__(self, plan, station_url, seed, latency=(6.5, 13.0)):
+        super().__init__(plan, transport=lambda _: {})
+        self.oracle = OracleVerifier(station_url)
+        self.oracle._plan_guard = plan.guard
+        self.rng = random.Random(seed)
+        self.latency = latency
+        self.calls = 0
+
+    def _build(self, stage, frames, elapsed_s):
+        payload = super()._build(stage, frames, elapsed_s)
+        if payload is None:
+            return None
+        snap = get(self.oracle.url, "/objects")
+        # Capture at request time, so a delayed verdict cannot see the future.
+        self.oracle.start.setdefault(stage.name, snap["referee"])
+        return {"stage": stage, "snapshot": snap, "elapsed": elapsed_s,
+                "delay": self.rng.uniform(*self.latency),
+                "signature": hashlib.sha256(b"".join(frames[k] for k in sorted(frames))).hexdigest()}
+
+    def _call(self, payload, menu_len):
+        self.calls += 1
+        time.sleep(payload["delay"])
+        verdict = self.oracle.check(payload["stage"], {}, payload["elapsed"], snapshot=payload["snapshot"])
+        verdict.frames_sig = payload["signature"]
+        return verdict

+"""Run one isolated, real-time sim match. Scripted policy is for offline validation only."""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import dataclasses
+import hashlib
+import importlib.metadata
+import json
+import os
+import signal
+import threading
+import time
+import urllib.request
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import psutil
+
+from cloud.judge import DelayedOracle
+from harness.detect import TableDetector
+from harness.frames import FrameSource
+from harness.live_guard import LiveFrames, LiveGuard
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.runner import Runner
+from harness.start_pose import StartPoses
+from harness.station_client import StationClient
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, anthropic_transport
+from sim_station import SimStation
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+
+
+def sim_text(value):
+    """The sim's balls have the real table's colours (sim_scenes.hill_climb_table: green, yellow and orange golf
+    balls legal; a white golf ball and a black 8-ball illegal), so plan texts apply as written: no translation."""
+    return value
+
+
+def start_live_snapshots(sim, log, folder, stop, every_s=15.0, keep_every=4):
+    """For watching running jobs: every `every_s` write live/{scene,wrist_l,wrist_r}.jpg (latest), live/status.json
+    and a line in live/timeline.jsonl; every `keep_every`-th scene frame is also kept as live/scene_NNN.jpg.
+    The entrypoint uploads live/ while the match runs. Never raises into the match."""
+    folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    state = {"stage": None, "attempt": None, "last_verdict": None, "started": time.monotonic()}
+
+    def track(event, fields):
+        if event == "stage_start":
+            state["stage"] = fields.get("stage")
+        elif event == "attempt_start":
+            state["attempt"] = fields.get("attempt")
+        elif event == "verdict":
+            state["last_verdict"] = {k: fields.get(k) for k in ("done", "confidence", "reason")}
+            state["last_verdict"]["reason"] = str(state["last_verdict"]["reason"])[:200]
+    log.listeners.append(track)
+
+    def loop():
+        n = 0
+        while not stop.wait(every_s if n else 2.0):
+            try:
+                for cam in ("scene", "wrist_l", "wrist_r"):
+                    with urllib.request.urlopen(f"{sim.api_url}/frame/{cam}.jpg?w=480", timeout=5) as reply:
+                        (folder / f"{cam}.jpg").write_bytes(reply.read())
+                if n % keep_every == 0:
+                    (folder / f"scene_{n // keep_every:03d}.jpg").write_bytes((folder / "scene.jpg").read_bytes())
+                with sim._lock:
+                    ref = dict(sim._poses.get("referee") or {})
+                status = {"wall_s": round(time.monotonic() - state["started"], 1), "sim_t": ref.get("t"),
+                          "stage": state["stage"], "attempt": state["attempt"], "points": ref.get("points"),
+                          "unplugged": ref.get("unplugged"), "tower_in": ref.get("tower_in"),
+                          "balls": ref.get("golf_in_bin"), "eight_in_bin": ref.get("eight_in_bin"),
+                          "dream": sim.dream_summary(),
+                          "last_verdict": state["last_verdict"]}
+                (folder / "status.json").write_text(json.dumps(status), encoding="utf-8")
+                with (folder / "timeline.jsonl").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+                    fh.write(json.dumps(status) + "\n")
+            except Exception as exc:   # a missed snapshot must never touch the match
+                (folder / "snapshot_error.txt").write_text(f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", encoding="utf-8")
+            n += 1
+
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=loop, name="live-snapshots", daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    return thread
+
+
+def prepare_plan(path, skip=(), *, notes=True, technique=None, level=None):
+    """technique/level None = as the plan file says."""
+    plan = sim_text(load_plan(path, operator_notes=notes, prompt_technique=technique))
+    if level is not None:
+        plan.prompt_level = level
+    plan.stages = [s for s in plan.stages if not any(s.name.startswith(x) for x in skip)]
+    if not plan.stages or any(s.verifier != "monitor" for s in plan.stages):
+        raise ValueError("cloud matches require monitor-only stages and no human input")
+    if not plan.guard or plan.time_budget_s > 300:
+        raise ValueError("cloud matches require a guard and a budget no longer than 300s")
+    plan.claude_user["parallel"] = 2
+    # Read-only practice lessons, as in --match. Adapt their ball colours too.
+    lesson = ROOT / "lessons/operator.md"
+    if lesson.exists():
+        plan.system_prompt += "\n" + sim_text(lesson.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    plan.system_prompt += ("\nSIM SCENE: the robot is a pair of YAM arms. The green, yellow and orange golf balls are "
+                           "legal; the white golf ball and the black 8-ball are illegal. The hub is silver. The bin is "
+                           "white. Judge the rendered scene, not the physical station photographs.")
+    return plan
+
+
+def run_match(args):
+    print(f"Will create one {args.policy}/{args.judge} match and logs under {args.out}", flush=True)
+    os.environ["HARNESS_CLAUDE_BACKEND"] = "api"
+    if args.judge == "api" and not os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"):
+        raise ValueError("USER ACTION NEEDED: supply ANTHROPIC_API_KEY through Secret Manager")
+    plan_path = ROOT / args.plan
+    plan = prepare_plan(plan_path, args.skip, notes=args.operator_notes != "off",
+                        technique={"on": True, "off": False}.get(args.prompt_technique), level=args.prompt_level)
+    detector = TableDetector.from_file(ROOT / args.detect)
+    log = RunLog(args.out)
+    proc = psutil.Process()
+    started, cpu0 = time.monotonic(), time.process_time()
+    peak = [proc.memory_info().rss]
+    done = threading.Event()
+
+    def sample():
+        while not done.wait(0.2):
+            peak[0] = max(peak[0], proc.memory_info().rss)
+
+    thread = threading.Thread(target=sample, daemon=True)
+    thread.start()
+    sim = None
+    runner = None
+    summary = None
+    try:
+        sim = SimStation(policy=args.policy, scene="hill_climb", realtime=True,
+                         api_port=0, view_port=0, max_trips=4,
+                         execution_horizon=args.execution_horizon or None, sync_above_ms=args.sync_above_ms)
+        sim.hedge = args.dream_hedge     # Dream sessions raced per action chunk (sim_station.HedgedPolicy)
+        sim.hedge_cutoff_s, sim.hedge_max_in_flight, sim.hedge_max_calls = (
+            args.dream_hedge_cutoff, args.dream_hedge_max_in_flight, args.dream_hedge_max_calls)
+        sim.dream_pipeline = args.dream_pipeline   # Dream sessions used in turn for throughput (PipelinedPolicy)
+        sim = sim.start()
+        station = StationClient(sim.api_url)
+        live_thread = start_live_snapshots(sim, log, Path(args.out) / "live", done)
+        station.reset()
+        if args.judge == "oracle":
+            mon = DelayedOracle(plan, sim.api_url, args.seed)
+        elif args.judge == "openrouter":
+            from harness.verifiers import openrouter_transport
+            plan.claude_user["hedge"] = args.judge_hedge          # identical copies per call, first good reply wins
+            models = [m.strip() for m in args.judge_models.split(",") if m.strip()]
+            mon = MonitorVerifier(plan, model=models[0],
+                                  transport=openrouter_transport(models, mode=args.judge_mode))
+        else:
+            mon = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=anthropic_transport(os.environ["ANTHROPIC_API_KEY"]))
+        mon.annotator, mon.legend = detector.annotate_frames, detector.legend()
+        frames = FrameSource(sim.view_url, station_url=sim.api_url)
+        runner = Runner(plan, station, frames, {"monitor": mon}, log)
+        runner.fast_guard = detector.guard
+        # Infrastructure health, so a judge/Dream outage can't pass as a low-scoring "complete" match.
+        health = {"verdicts": 0, "monitor_errors": 0, "guard_blind": 0, "attempts_aborted": 0, "last_error": None}
+
+        def count_health(event, fields):
+            if event == "verdict":
+                health["verdicts"] += 1
+                if str(fields.get("reason", "")).startswith("monitor error"):
+                    health["monitor_errors"] += 1
+                    health["last_error"] = str(fields["reason"])[:300]
+            elif event == "guard_blind":
+                health["guard_blind"] += 1
+            elif event == "attempt_end" and str(fields.get("reason", "")).startswith(("RuntimeError", "OSError")):
+                health["attempts_aborted"] += 1
+                health["last_error"] = str(fields["reason"])[:300]
+        log.listeners.append(count_health)
+        if args.judge == "oracle":
+            def stage_baseline(event, fields):
+                if event == "stage_start":
+                    with sim._lock:
+                        mon.oracle.start[fields["stage"]] = dict(sim._poses["referee"])
+            log.listeners.append(stage_baseline)
+        from harness.joint_telemetry import JointTelemetry
+        runner.joints = JointTelemetry(sim.api_url, log=log.event,                # live joint torques -> monitor, log
+                                       record_to=str(Path(args.out) / "joints.jsonl")).start()   # observer training data
+        live = LiveFrames(sim.api_url)
+        if set(live.probe()) != {"wrist_l", "wrist_r"}:
+            raise RuntimeError("live guard wrist-camera probe failed")
+        runner.live_guard = LiveGuard(live.grab, detector.guard, station.stop,
+                                      rate_hz=10, confirm_frames=3, log=log.event)
+        if plan.start_pose:
+            runner.start_poses = StartPoses.load(ROOT / "plans" / plan.start_pose.reference)
+        plan_data = dataclasses.asdict(plan)
+        log.update_manifest(match=True, policy=args.policy, judge=args.judge, seed=args.seed,
+                            judge_models=args.judge_models, judge_mode=args.judge_mode,
+                            judge_hedge=args.judge_hedge, dream_hedge=args.dream_hedge,
+                            label=args.label, scene="hill_climb", scene_randomisation="none",
+                            detector=args.detect, live_guard=True, operator=False,
+                            plan_sha256=hashlib.sha256(plan_path.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                            effective_plan=plan_data, sim_colour_adaptation=True,
+                            versions={p: importlib.metadata.version(p) for p in ("mujoco", "dreamlabs")})
+        runner.live_guard.start()
+        signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, lambda *_: runner.request_stop())
+        match_start = time.monotonic()
+        result = runner.run()
+        # An early harness finish holds the arms through the rest of the scoring clock.
+        while time.monotonic() - match_start < 300 and not runner._stop_event.is_set():
+            time.sleep(min(0.25, 300 - (time.monotonic() - match_start)))
+        with sim._lock:
+            ref = dict(sim._poses["referee"])
+        wall = time.monotonic() - match_start
+        complete = not runner._stop_event.is_set() and wall >= 299
+        sim_t = ref.get("t")
+        error_rate = health["monitor_errors"] / max(1, health["verdicts"])
+        problems = [why for why, bad in [
+            ("monitor errors over 20% of verdicts", error_rate > 0.2),
+            ("guard went blind", health["guard_blind"] > 0),
+            ("station/Dream error aborted an attempt", health["attempts_aborted"] > 0),
+            ("harness stopped on an error", "error" in str(result.reason).lower()),
+            ("no judge verdicts", health["verdicts"] == 0)] if bad]
+        summary = {"schema_version": 1, "scenario": "hill_climb", "label": args.label,
+                   "seed": args.seed, "policy": args.policy, "judge": args.judge,
+                   "judge_models": args.judge_models, "judge_mode": args.judge_mode,
+                   "judge_reasoning": os.environ.get("HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING"),
+                   "operator_notes": bool(plan.operator_notes), "prompt_technique": plan.prompt_technique,
+                   "prompt_level": plan.prompt_level, "dream_hedge": args.dream_hedge, "dream_pipeline": args.dream_pipeline,
+                   "execution_horizon": args.execution_horizon,
+                   "dream": sim.dream_summary(),
+                   "harness": dataclasses.asdict(result), "referee": ref,
+                   "stages": [dataclasses.asdict(s) for s in result.stages],
+                   "match_wall_s": wall,
+                   "complete": complete,
+                   # valid = complete AND the infrastructure worked; compare judges on valid rows only.
+                   "valid": complete and not problems, "invalid_because": problems, "health": health,
+                   "sim_t": sim_t, "sim_rate": round(sim_t / wall, 3) if sim_t and wall else None,
+                   "monitor_calls": getattr(mon, "calls", mon._seq),
+                   "milestones": {"stage1_at": ref["stage1_at"], "stage2_at": ref["stage2_at"],
+                                  "first_touch": ref["first_touch"]},
+                   "effective_plan_sha256": hashlib.sha256(json.dumps(plan_data, sort_keys=True).encode()).hexdigest(),
+                   "versions": {p: importlib.metadata.version(p) for p in ("mujoco", "dreamlabs")},
+                   "source_revision": os.environ.get("SOURCE_REVISION", "local")}
+    finally:
+        if runner and runner.live_guard:
+            runner.live_guard.close()
+        if runner and getattr(runner, "joints", None):
+            runner.joints.close()
+        if sim:
+            sim.close()
+        done.set()
+        thread.join()
+    summary["metrics"] = {"wall_s": round(time.monotonic() - started, 3),
+                          "cpu_s": round(time.process_time() - cpu0, 3),
+                          "peak_rss_mib": round(peak[0] / 2**20, 2),
+                          "host_cpu_count": os.cpu_count(), "platform": os.name}
+    target = Path(args.out) / "match.json"
+    target.write_text(json.dumps(summary, indent=2), encoding="utf-8")
+    print(json.dumps({"result": str(target), "metrics": summary["metrics"], "referee": summary["referee"]}), flush=True)
+    return summary
+
+
+def parser():
+    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
+    p.add_argument("--policy", choices=["dream", "scripted"], default="dream")
+    p.add_argument("--judge", choices=["oracle", "api", "openrouter"], default="oracle")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-models", default="google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
+                   help="OpenRouter model ids, comma-separated (--judge openrouter)")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-mode", choices=["race", "alternate"], default="race")
+    p.add_argument("--judge-hedge", type=int, default=1)
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge", type=int, default=1, help="Dream sessions raced per chunk (N x Dream usage)")
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge-cutoff", type=float, default=None, help="seconds before a slow call is cut and resent")
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge-max-in-flight", type=int, default=None, help="most hedged calls running at once")
+    p.add_argument("--dream-hedge-max-calls", type=int, default=None, help="most hedged calls per action chunk")
+    p.add_argument("--plan", default="plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml")
+    p.add_argument("--skip", action="append", default=[])
+    p.add_argument("--operator-notes", choices=["plan", "off"], default="plan",
+                   help="off = ignore the plan's operator_notes (A/B control)")
+    p.add_argument("--prompt-technique", choices=["plan", "on", "off"], default="plan")
+    p.add_argument("--prompt-level", type=int, choices=range(0, 6), default=None)
+    p.add_argument("--execution-horizon", type=int, default=10,
+                   help="Dream async pacing: rows per chunk before the next takes over (10 at 20 Hz = 2 Hz calls); "
+                        "0 = Dream's example loop (whole chunk, physics holds during inference)")
+    p.add_argument("--dream-pipeline", type=int, default=1,
+                   help="Dream sessions used in turn, so a new chunk can land every 0.5 s even with a ~1 s round trip "
+                        "(N x Dream usage); needs --execution-horizon")
+    p.add_argument("--sync-above-ms", type=float, default=1900.0,
+                   help="round trip past which the pacing goes sync (10 rows + 1.9 s x 20 Hz = the 48-row chunk)")
+    p.add_argument("--detect", default="plans/sim_table_colors.yaml")
+    p.add_argument("--match", action="store_true", default=True)
+    p.add_argument("--live-guard", action="store_true", default=True)
+    p.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
+    p.add_argument("--label", default="hill_climb_v4")
+    p.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=Path("/out"))
+    return p
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    run_match(parser().parse_args())

+"""Offline container gate: original Dream example, relay scene, render, HTTP and detector."""
+import io
+import json
+import urllib.request
+
+import numpy as np
+from PIL import Image
+
+from harness.detect import TableDetector
+from sim_station import SimStation, load_example
+
+
+def main():
+    y, _ = load_example(None)
+    original = y.YamEnv()
+    try:
+        for _ in range(40):
+            original.step(y.HOME, y.HOME)
+        assert all(np.isfinite(v).all() for v in original.state().values())
+        assert all(rgb.std() > 10 for rgb in original.images().values())
+    finally:
+        original.close()
+    with SimStation(policy="scripted", scene="hill_climb", api_port=0, view_port=0) as sim:
+        for cam in ("scene", "wrist_l", "wrist_r"):
+            with urllib.request.urlopen(sim.api_url + f"/frame/{cam}.jpg?w=320") as response:
+                im = Image.open(io.BytesIO(response.read()))
+                assert im.size == (320, 180) and np.asarray(im).std() > 10
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(sim.api_url + "/arms") as response:
+            assert all(0 <= arm["gripper"] <= 1 for arm in json.load(response).values())
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(sim.api_url + "/frame/scene.jpg") as response:
+            boxes = TableDetector.from_file("plans/sim_table_colors.yaml").boxes({"scene": response.read()})["scene"]
+        assert sum(b.role == "target" for b in boxes) >= 2        # green, yellow, orange golf balls
+        assert sum(b.role == "avoid" for b in boxes) >= 1        # the white golf ball (+ black 8-balls if added)
+    # The match path must import inside the image, and the operator lessons must be there (run.py reads them).
+    import importlib
+    from pathlib import Path
+    for module in ("cloud.run", "cloud.judge", "cloud.entrypoint", "cloud.collect", "experiments.sim_match"):
+        importlib.import_module(module)
+    assert Path("lessons/operator.md").is_file(), "lessons/operator.md missing from the image"
+    print("Offline original-example and relay build/step/render/API/detector gates passed.")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()

+# Harness decision model: runtime, arms, balls, prompts, and the decision tree as tunable equations
+
+7 Nov 2026 (late). This builds on `HARNESS_SPEED_STATE_SPACE_2026-11-07.md`. It uses:
+- today's inference logs from the NUC (1,650 chunks over 52 runs);
+- the runner on `main` / `start-pose-live-guard`;
+- kaupau's `hybrid-harness` branch, which does prompt generation better (section 4);
+- the sequential vs combined two-arm benchmark (`codex/dual-arm-experiment`, `runs_dual_arm/`).
+
+## 0. Findings that drive the design
+
+| # | Finding | Evidence |
+|---|---|---|
+| F1 | **The arms sit still about 30% of the time.** Dream's pacer runs `sync` whenever the median round trip is over 900 ms: it waits for each 2.4 s chunk, and chunks arrive every 3.54 s (p50). In `async` mode chunks arrive every 1.55 s with no gap. 65% of today's chunks were sync. | `robots-inference.jsonl`; station.log `dream.pacing: pacing sync ... over the 900 ms threshold` |
+| F2 | The round trip is about 950 ms even with a fast upload (≈650 ms of gateway overhead), and upload size makes it worse. Each request is ~1.6 MB. With a 5 Mbps uplink (p10) the round trip is ~1.9 s, and only 4% of those chunks could run async. | same log, `request_bytes`, `up_mbps` |
+| F3 | A fresh `/start` costs 2.9 s (p50) before motion begins; `/prompt` on a running session costs nothing extra. | run_start → chunk 1 |
+| F4 | The harness costs about 5-8 s per stage boundary: 3 s poll, ~4 s monitor call, then confirm 2 means one more look. | runner constants, stress sim |
+| F5 | **Dream-1 does not reliably obey arm naming.** "with the left arm, …" succeeded 0/4 and the right arm still moved 10-19 s. "Left does X while right does Y" succeeded 0/4. Two goals without naming arms finished one goal in 2/4 trials. (YAM sim, n = 12.) | `runs_dual_arm/20261107T230412Z/results.jsonl` |
+| F6 | Base Dream-1 follows short, specific prompts that name objects by colour and position much better than the generic task texts. It refuses cable work without the "no power" sentence (4 × DA-4000 today). | `hybrid-harness` plan notes, inference log errors |
+| F7 | The harness's own 2 MB debug-view frame fetches slowed Dream to 2-6 s per chunk. The add-on's `/frame/<cam>.jpg?w=640` frames are ~35 KB. | `hybrid-harness` HARNESS_GUIDE 6g |
+
+## 1. Runtime: where the seconds go, ranked by expected gain
+
+Model of one stage: `T_stage = T_start + T_robot / u + T_detect + T_decide`, where `u` is the share of time the arms actually move (F1).
+
+| Lever | Saves | Cost / risk | How to measure |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **L1. Get Dream into async.** Wired ethernet on the NUC. Stop the harness pulling debug-view trips (use hybrid's light frames, F7). Ask Dream whether the 900 ms pacing threshold or the image size per request can be configured. | u ≈ 0.70 → ~0.95, about 25% less robot time on every stage | network work only; any station config change is the station owner's decision | share of `async` chunks and chunk-gap p50 in `robots-inference.jsonl`, before vs after |
+| **L2. Event-driven stage done.** A local estimator (gripper sensors from `/arms`, detector boxes, cable-end boxes) advances when its posterior is high. The monitor confirms in parallel and can veto. | 4-7 s per boundary, 20-35 s per match | a false done on a pass bar is a DQ, so it is gated by the bound in section 5.3 | `stage_start` − true-done time in replayed logs |
+| **L3. Never `/start` again mid-match.** Recoveries and retries use `/prompt`, not stop + start. | 2.9 s each time | none; it's already mostly the case | count `start` calls per match |
+| **L4. Skip checks the sensors can answer.** "Are both grippers empty?" (release_cable, arms_clear, put-downs) comes from `/arms` gripper readings in milliseconds, not a 4 s synchronous VLM look. Hybrid already lets the sensors overrule the monitor's `holding`. | ~4 s per skippable stage, 3-4 per match | sensor calibration (hybrid has this station's readings) | `entry_check` latency |
+| **L5. Faster judge.** Use hybrid's stitched grid, two staggered calls in flight, and 4 history frames. | a verdict every ~5 s instead of ~10 s | merge from hybrid | `latency_s` in verdict events |
+
+L1 and L2 dominate. L1 is a network and ops change, not code.
+
+## 2. Both arms at once
+
+The station API has **one prompt for one bimanual policy**. There is no per-arm command and no way to run two sessions. So "both arms at the same time" has to come from one of these:
+
+| Mode | How | Works today? | Use for |
+|---|---|---|---|
+| **A. One worker, the other parked (current).** | The right arm does everything; the left is parked by the reset prompt. | yes | default |
+| **B. Watcher.** | The stage's `watch:` puts one arm's wrist camera on the work, and the other arm works. The harness re-sends the park prompt if the watcher drifts (`watch_lost`). | partly: depends on Dream holding the watcher still (F5 says naming the arm is unreliable) | close views of the port, the ball in the gripper, the tower top |
+| **C. Combined prompt, two goals.** | "put the X … and the Y …" without naming arms; Dream assigns them. | 2/4 single-goal successes in the sim, both arms moving at once | parallel independent goals, e.g. ball + ball, once tested on Metal |
+| **D. Trained bimanual skill.** | Record teleop demos where the left arm watches or helps while the right works, under one fixed task text. | needs data | the only route to dependable two-arm behaviour |
+
+The harness can't make the two arms independent tasks. It **can** decide which mode each stage uses, which arm is the worker (section 3), and when a combined prompt is worth the risk. That decision gets a weight like the others (section 5).
+
+Experiment E1 (sim first, then station): 3 modes × 20 trials each on the same seeded scenes. Measure success, time to done, both-arms-moving seconds, and collisions or knocks. 20 per mode separates 30% from 70% success. A 0.5 vs 0.8 gap needs about 40 per mode for 80% power.
+
+## 3. Picking the arm from the layout
+
+What the harness has: the overhead camera, colour detector boxes (balls, cubes, sticks), cable-end boxes (new), gripper state, and joints (`/arms`, after a station restart).
+
+1. **Table frame.** One homography H from overhead pixels to table metres. Click the four table corners once per station (or mark them with tags) and store it in the plan. The arm bases `b_L` and `b_R` and the bin, hub and tower positions all go in that frame.
+2. **Cost of arm a for object o:**
+
+       c_a(o) = w_reach · ‖p_o − b_a‖ / R_a             reach, normalised by the arm's reach
+              + w_cross · 1[o is past the midline for a]   crossing the body
+              + w_clear · Σ_k exp(−d(path_a,o, k)² / σ²)   obstacles near the straight path: tower, hub, illegal balls
+              + w_busy  · 1[a holds something or is the watcher]
+              + w_skill · (1 − s_a(task))                  arm-specific skill prior: demos exist only for the right arm
+
+   Choose `a* = argmin_a c_a(o)`. If `c_a*(o) > c_max` for every arm, the object is unreachable: skip it or pick another object.
+3. **Turning the choice into an action.** F5 means naming the arm in the prompt is not enough on its own. In order of dependability:
+   - (i) choose the **object** the chosen arm is best placed for, and name it by colour and position ("the orange ball nearest the right arm"). Dream tends to use the nearer arm;
+   - (ii) for a fine-tune, record per-arm task texts ("… nearest the left arm" as well as "… nearest the right arm");
+   - (iii) the arm phrase as a hint only, and the monitor checks which arm moved. Count a wrong-arm move as a failed attempt.
+4. Experiment E2: does base Dream-1 on Metal pick the nearer arm? Place objects at 5 positions from left to right, 4 trials each, with and without an arm phrase. Log which arm moved first. The result sets `w_skill` and tells us whether (iii) is worth anything.
+
+## 4. Prompting Dream
+
+**Build on kaupau's `hybrid-harness` branch.** What it does that main doesn't:
+- **Templates with slots:** `tower_prompts` with `<piece>` / `<top>`, cable prompts as plan variables, and a `prompt_prefix` that is put back on every rewrite.
+- **Closed-loop rewriting:** the monitor returns `next_prompt` when it sees a wrong object or step, a plain stall, or a failure about to happen. The runner sends it, with a cooldown of 15 s and at most 3 per attempt. Every rewrite is logged as `prompt_rewrite`.
+- **Station lessons in the monitor's system prompt:** plugs are pulled straight up; carry a block high and lower it straight down; Dream ignores fine adjustments like "1 cm higher". There is also a running operator-lessons file, for testing only.
+- **`tools/label_rollout.py`:** labels Dream's own run records with stage, attempt, outcome and the clean task text. This is the bridge to fine-tune data.
+
+Formalised: the prompt is a policy `π_P(x) = template_s(slots(x))`, with `rewrite(x)` as a gated fallback. Slots come from the state estimate: object colour and position, arm (section 3), next tower piece, and which plug is next. A rewrite fires only when
+
+    g_rw = w_wrong·1[wrong object/step] + w_stall·(t − t_rise)/stall_s + w_risk·P(knock) − w_churn·n_rewrites  >  θ_rw
+    and  t − t_last_prompt ≥ τ_cool
+
+Hybrid's fixed rules are one point in this space: `θ_rw` = "the monitor chose to write one", τ_cool = 15, and a hard cap of 3.
+
+**Prompt experiments (E3).** The unit is one stage from a seeded scene, scored on success within T, time to first contact, and refusals.
+- Factors: specificity (generic task text vs colour + position), motion adverbs ("lift high, lower straight down"), arm phrase (none / hint), preamble (on / off, cables only), and length.
+- Plan: a fractional factorial screen in the YAM sim with real Dream-1, which is cheap. Then confirm the best 2-3 on Metal at 10-20 trials each.
+- Prompts already sent today, from the inference log, cover about 40 variants across 52 runs, but the log has no outcomes. Join them to harness `events.jsonl` with `label_rollout.py` to get a free first ranking.
+
+The fine-tune rule still holds. A fine-tune follows only its recorded texts, so whatever the base-model experiments settle on is what the teleop labels must say. Hybrid runs a fine-tune with `--no-rewrite` plus the recorded texts.
+
+## 5. The decision tree as weighted equations
+
+### 5.1 What the runner does today, as rules
+
+At each check (every `check_every_s` = 3 s), per stage attempt:
+- **done** if the verifier says done `confirm` (2) times in a row, or twice within `DONE_WINDOW_S` (15 s), or with `immediate` set;
+- **fail** if `failing` comes `confirm` times in a row and elapsed ≥ `FAIL_MIN_FRACTION` (0.5) × `timeout_s`; or progress drops by `REGRESSION_DROP` (0.3) below the attempt's max; or progress hasn't risen by `STALL_RISE` (0.05) for `stall_s`; or `timeout_s` passes, with one extension of `EXTEND_S` (20 s) while the gripper is on the target and `CONFIRM_GRACE_S` (10 s) while a done is pending;
+- **on fail:** abort, skip, or the recovery menu in order (`max_uses` each), then `home_and_retry` if allowed, up to `max_attempts`, never with less than `HOME_RESERVE_S` (30 s) left;
+- **guard trips** after `guard.confirm` illegal-ball answers (live: 3 distinct frames at 10 Hz), or when blind for `max_blind_s`.
+
+### 5.2 The same tree as equations
+
+State `x_t`: stage s, elapsed τ, time left `L = T − t`, the evidence features `φ(t)`, attempt count n, recovery uses, and the arm and object state.
+
+**Done evidence (log-odds, recursive):**
+
+    ℓ_t = γ · ℓ_{t−1} + Σ_i w_i · φ_i(t)
+
+| φ_i | Source | Today's implicit weight |
+|---|---|---|
+| monitor done (+1) / not (−1), × confidence | VLM | +1 / reset to 0 |
+| progress p_t (0..1) | VLM | used only for stall and regression |
+| gripper went from closed on an object to open, near the target | `/arms` | none (new) |
+| object at goal (detector box inside the bin or on the tower) | detector | none (new) |
+| plug count in the hub fell (cable-end boxes) | classifier | none (new) |
+| same frames as last time | sha1 | φ = 0 (not counted) |
+
+Done when `ℓ_t ≥ θ_done`. Today's rule is the special case γ = 1 on agreement and 0 on disagreement, `w_monitor = 1`, `θ_done = confirm = 2`.
+
+**Fail hazard:**
+
+    h_t = σ( b + w_f·n_fail + w_r·max(0, p_max − p_t − δ) + w_s·(τ − τ_rise)/stall_s + w_T·τ/timeout_s − w_on·1[on target] )
+
+The attempt ends when `h_t > θ_fail`. The hard rules above are the corners of this function: they fire when any one term is large.
+
+**Action choice when an attempt ends (or at each tick, for `continue`):** a ∈ {continue, re-prompt / rewrite, reset prompt, home + retry, skip, abort}.
+
+    Q(a | x) = P_a(succ within c_a) · V_s  +  W(L − c_a)  −  λ_risk · R_a
+
+- `V_s` = the stage's points (rubric v4: first cable +10, extra cable +5, tower pass +20, +3 per inch, ball +5), plus the value it unlocks (pass bars gate later stages).
+- `W(L)` = expected points from the stages after s with L seconds left. Compute it backwards over the stage list once per plan, using each stage's success-vs-time curve `F_s(τ)`.
+- `c_a` = time the action costs (re-prompt ~1 s, reset 5-20 s, home ~10 s plus `/start` 2.9 s).
+- `R_a` = risk: a DQ from a false done or a touch out of order, a knocked tower, an illegal ball.
+
+Pick `a* = argmax Q`. `on_fail: skip` and `max_attempts` become consequences of Q, not hand-set values.
+
+**Optimal timeout (replaces a fixed `timeout_s`):** keep going on stage s while the success rate per second, times its value, beats what the remaining time is worth elsewhere:
+
+    f_s(τ) / (1 − F_s(τ)) · V_s   ≥   dW/dL
+
+That gives each stage a timeout that depends on its own success curve and on how much match is left. Optional stages get short timeouts late in the match and longer ones early.
+
+### 5.3 The pass-bar constraint
+
+A false done on a pass bar (unplug, stack_2, stack_3, tower_pass) is a DQ. With per-look false-positive rate α for the evidence and k independent looks required, `P(false done) ≈ α^k`. Choose `θ_done` per stage so that `P(false done) ≤ ε` (e.g. 1%), then minimise the expected detection delay. This is the one hard constraint in the tuning. Everything else trades off in `J` below.
+
+### 5.4 Parameters and tuning
+
+θ = {w_i, γ, θ_done(s), w_f, w_r, w_s, w_T, w_on, θ_fail, λ_risk, θ_rw, w_wrong, w_churn, τ_cool, c_a weights (section 3), check period, live-guard Hz and frames, start-pose tolerance}. Today's constants (`confirm`, `STALL_RISE`, `REGRESSION_DROP`, `FAIL_MIN_FRACTION`, `DONE_WINDOW_S`, `EXTEND_S`, `CONFIRM_GRACE_S`, `HOME_RESERVE_S`, `SKIP_MIN_CONFIDENCE`, `REWRITE_*`) are its starting point.
+
+Objective, estimated in the stress sim and the rubric-v4 referee:
+
+    J(θ) = E[score] − μ · P(DQ) − ν · P(illegal ball)
+
+1. **Calibrate the sim's evidence model from real logs.** Per stage, how often the monitor says done when it's not, and the reverse (label saved frames by hand). Also the detector's miss and false-alarm rates, `F_s(τ)` from the runs, and refusal and IK-error rates. This is the STEP / SIMPLER warning: tuning against a sim with the wrong noise picks the wrong θ.
+2. **Search:** CMA-ES or coordinate hill climbing over θ. Use common random numbers (the same seeded scenes for every candidate), about 200 sim matches per candidate, run on the cluster.
+3. **Confirm on Metal:** the best 2 θ vs today's, 5 supervised matches each. Promote only if J is better and P(DQ) didn't rise.
+
+## 6. Ball handling
+
+Golf ball: 42.7 mm, rolls when nudged. The bin is white, the same colour as the illegal white ball. Dream does the grasp. The harness controls **which ball, from where, with what prompt, and whether the grasp worked**:
+
+1. **Choose the ball** (overhead detector + homography): legal colour; reachable by the chosen arm (section 3); clearance from the tower `d_tower ≥ 2·D_ball` and from any illegal ball `d_illegal ≥ 1.5·D_ball` so the gripper can't take both; not against an edge it can roll off. Score `s(b) = −c_a*(b) − w_tw·exp(−d_tower²/σ²) − w_il·exp(−d_illegal²/σ²)` and send the best: "pick up the orange ball nearest the right arm, straight down from above, and drop it into the white bin".
+2. **Grasp check, local and fast.** After the gripper closes, its opening g from `/arms` should sit in a band around the ball's width, `g ∈ [g_ball − δ, g_ball + δ]`, for N ticks. Fully closed means a miss; wider means it's on something else. This is the same idea as hybrid's "closed fully on nothing" sensor rule. Calibrate `g_ball` once by closing on a ball.
+3. **Rolled away:** if the target's overhead position jumps by more than D_ball between frames without being held, re-target with a new prompt naming the ball's new position. Don't retry blind.
+4. **The illegal ball:** the live guard (10 Hz, `/stop` in under 0.5 s) stays as the hard stop. The ball choice in step 1 lowers the chance it's ever needed.
+5. **Dream's own ball skill:** there are no ball demos yet; balls rely on base Dream-1. To make it dependable, record ball teleop (top-down grasp, carry high, release over the bin) under one fixed text, and add one demo set starting next to the white ball so the policy learns to go around it.
+
+## 7. What to build, in order
+
+1. Merge `hybrid-harness` (prompting, light frames, faster judge, sensor overrides) with `start-pose-live-guard` (start pose, live guard, cable-end classifier).
+2. **L1 network check:** ethernet, and light frames only. Re-measure the async share. Ops only; the biggest single gain.
+3. Overhead homography + arm/ball choice (sections 3 and 6) as a `layout.py` that feeds prompt slots.
+4. A `decide.py` with the equations in 5.2. Default weights reproduce today's rules exactly, with a test proving it. Then calibrate and tune in the sim (5.4).
+5. Experiments E1 (dual-arm modes), E2 (nearer-arm choice), E3 (prompt factors), sim first, then Metal.

+## Dashboard contents
+
+The dashboard has six widgets:
+
+1. Running Batch tasks, with links to authoritative Batch task counts and the
+   status command. The project has no native Batch task-count metric. This
+   widget explicitly explains that limitation.
+2. Reporting simulation VMs, using reserved-core metric series counted in
+   60-second intervals.
+3. CPU utilization per simulation VM.
+4. hc-nat allocation failures, using the BOOL allocation-failure metric aligned
+   as true-sample counts per minute.
+5. Batch agent task-state reports.
+6. Task logs and errors.
+
+VM charts select names starting with `pilot2-` or `hill-climb-`. Use that naming
+convention for future jobs or revise the dashboard filter. The queries were
+tested against real Monitoring data: the VM series showed 12 reporting VMs,
+CPU series contained pilot history, and hc-nat returned zero allocation failure
+samples. Metrics can lag several minutes. Historical series can outlive VMs.
+A blank chart is not an authoritative zero task count.
+
+A live numeric RUNNING-task chart would require a task-state metric exporter
+or other additional data source. None was created under the brief's resource
+restrictions. Use the linked Batch Tasks tab or status.sh for exact task counts.
+
+## Healthy runs and stalled runs
+
+Run this in Git Bash from this checkout:
+
+```bash
+bash cloud/status.sh
+# Or select a specific existing job:
+bash cloud/status.sh pilot2-oracle
+```
+
+The script pins the Gmail account, project and us-central1 on every gcloud call.
+It discovers the existing Windows SDK path if gcloud is absent from PATH; an
+explicit executable can also be supplied through `GCLOUD`. It performs no cloud
+writes. Read failures print UNKNOWN and return exit code 1 instead of reporting
+false zero counts. A healthy read returns 0 even if a job itself failed.
+
+The script reports the job state, task-state counts, current VM counts, uploaded
+match.json and complete.json counts, and the latest five task error entries per
+job from the last seven days. QUEUED and SCHEDULED are job states. Batch tasks
+normally use PENDING, ASSIGNED, RUNNING, SUCCEEDED, FAILED or UNEXECUTED, so the
+requested QUEUED/SCHEDULED columns are retained alongside the actual API states.
+
+Object counts cover every attempt, including retries, and are not deduplicated
+match counts. Only complete.json marks a fully uploaded, complete match. A
+match.json without that marker is not ready for collection. The entrypoint
+writes worker.log locally while a match runs, then uploads the output and the
+marker last. Quiet task logs during the roughly 300-second match can be normal.
+
+A healthy job moves through QUEUED, SCHEDULED and RUNNING, gains successful tasks,
+and uploads completion markers. Compare successive snapshots. CPU activity,
+recent Batch agent reports and increasing completed uploads support health.
+Check a job that remains queued without VMs for quota or capacity errors. Check
+repeated failures, upload errors, stale agent reports, or absent completion
+markers after expected match/retry durations. A RUNNING VM alone does not prove
+the simulation is healthy. Use the configured 900-second task timeout and status
+events when judging whether a task is stuck; do not automatically cancel it.
+
+## What happened to these pilots
+
+Initially all three protected pilots were RUNNING, each with four VMs. During
+this audit the service reported transitions to CANCELLATION_IN_PROGRESS:
+
+| Job | Transition time UTC |
+| --- | --- |
+| pilot2-oracle | 2026-11-08 10:27:55.461665405 |
+| pilot2-gemini-h2 | 2026-11-08 10:27:58.367976028 |
+| pilot2-alt-gemini-haiku | 2026-11-08 10:28:01.508344286 |
+
+Subsequent reads showed CANCELLED and zero remaining VMs. This session issued
+no cancel/delete/edit/resubmit call. Read-only Batch audit-log queries returned
+no entries, so this audit cannot identify the caller responsible for the
+cancellation. Do not infer the caller from the active account alone.
+
+All three jobs reported `output upload failed; check bucket IAM and network`.
+The final reads found zero match.json and complete.json objects for every pilot.
+The IAM and network configuration matched the brief, so these messages alone do
+not establish the root cause. No corrective change or rerun was attempted.
+
+**USER ACTION NEEDED:** review the cancellation and upload failures before
+authorizing any later run. Keep the existing job history, logs and bucket
+objects. Do not interpret leftover task RUNNING counts on a CANCELLED job as
+live work; task states can remain recorded after cancellation. The current job
+state and VM inventory take precedence for whether compute is still running.
+
+## Cancel a future job safely
+
+The three pilot2 jobs remain protected by the brief. This section describes a
+manual procedure for a different job that you have chosen to stop.
+
+1. Verify Gmail, project, region and the exact job name. Capture its status and
+   uploaded-marker counts first. A cancellation can lose the current task's
+   local, not-yet-uploaded output and does not refund completed API inference.
+2. Use Batch cancellation for that job, rather than deleting individual managed
+   VMs. In Git Bash, replace FUTURE-JOB with the intended non-protected job:
+
+   ```bash
+   gcloud batch jobs cancel FUTURE-JOB --location=us-central1 \
+     --project=hill-climb-sims --account=<your-gmail>
+   ```
+
+3. Read the job until it reaches CANCELLED and verify its VMs disappear. Leave
+   bucket results, logs, NAT, secrets and job history in place. Cancellation
+   retains Batch job information for up to 60 days, subject to Google's normal
+   retention; deleting the job removes that history sooner.
+
+This procedure was not executed during the audit.
+
+## Reading costs
+
+| Service or SKU | What causes the charge |
+| --- | --- |
+| Batch | Orchestrates jobs; underlying compute, storage and network are billed separately. |
+| Compute Engine E2 Spot CPU and RAM | Allocated VM runtime, including startup, idle periods and retries. Low measured CPU use does not reduce allocated-VM billing. |
+| Persistent Disk | Each pilot VM requested a 30 GB pd-standard boot disk. Disk provisioned capacity is billed while it exists. |
+| Cloud NAT gateway and data processing | Private VMs' outbound internet traffic uses hc-nat. Existing NAT and its auto-allocated IP can continue to incur charges after Batch VMs finish. |
+| External IPv4 | NAT's allocated public IP can be billed separately. These pilot VMs had no individual external IP. |
+| Network transfer | Internet and applicable inter-region traffic. A second region can add transfer charges to the central results bucket and registry. |
+| Artifact Registry | Stored image bytes and applicable data transfer. Both version tags remain stored. |
+| Cloud Build | Container build machine time, subject to billing allowances. Source staging objects also occupy storage. No build was run during this audit. |
+| Cloud Storage | Result/source bytes, operations, applicable retrieval/transfer, and retained soft-deleted data. Bucket soft-delete retention is seven days. |
+| Cloud Logging and Monitoring | Log ingestion/retention and any chargeable telemetry beyond allowances. This dashboard reads existing service metrics; it creates no exporter or custom metric. |
+| Secret Manager | Stored active versions and access operations, subject to allowances. No secret version was read during this audit. |
+| Dream and OpenRouter/Anthropic | Separate providers' inference/session/token charges. They are not covered by the GCP $50 budget. |
+
+The $50 budget covers the selected GCP project, includes credits, and sends
+actual-spend alerts at $25, $45 and $50. It is an alerts-only budget, not a hard
+spending cap. Billing and alert delivery can lag usage. The recipient is already
+the Gmail billing administrator; no additional role or notification channel is
+needed. Do not change the budget amount or payment settings.
