+arms at y < 0.2. The strip and cables are in front of the RIGHT arm and the basket by the left one (with them in front
+of the left arm, Dream-1 reached across the blocks and balls: 3 DQs in 3 sim matches). HillClimbReferee reads contacts
+and poses each physics step and reports the score, the pass bars and any DQ (a gripper touching a later stage's
+object before the current pass bar).

+class HedgedPolicy:
+    """N Dream sessions fed the same observations; each `get_action` races them and returns the first reply.
+
+    The Dream API is input -> output: the same observation sent to N sessions gives N samples of the policy, and the
+    one that comes back first is used (harness.hedge's idea applied to the action chunks). This cuts the latency
+    tail of a round trip, not its floor, and costs N x the Dream usage. A session that errors is skipped while any
+    other is still running; only if every session fails does the error reach the control loop.
+
+    When a reply is taken the other calls are cancelled. Dream has no cancel route (only /v1/get_action and
+    /v1/end_session), so cancelling means cutting the HTTP connection: it frees the session here at once, but the
+    server may finish the work and bill it. With `cutoff_s`, a call older than that is cut and a fresh one sent, if
+    `max_calls` and `max_in_flight` allow; the last call running is never cut without a replacement to send."""
+
+    def __init__(self, sessions: list[Any], *, cutoff_s: float | None = None, max_in_flight: int | None = None,
+                 max_calls: int | None = None, log: Any = None):
+        self.sessions = sessions
+        self._pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(sessions), thread_name_prefix="dream-hedge")
+        self.wins = [0] * len(sessions)
+        self.last_winner: int | None = None
+        self.cutoff_s = cutoff_s
+        self.max_in_flight = max(1, min(max_in_flight or len(sessions), len(sessions)))
+        self.max_calls = max(1, max_calls or len(sessions) + 1)     # per get_action
+        self._log = log
+        self.calls: deque[dict[str, Any]] = deque(maxlen=5000)      # started, finished, cancelled, stopped, dropped
+        self.counts = {"started": 0, "finished": 0, "cancelled": 0, "stopped": 0, "dropped": 0, "applied": 0, "error": 0}
+        self._note_lock = threading.Lock()
+        self._busy: dict[int, Any] = {}
+        self._live: dict[int, dict[str, Any]] = {}
+        self._next_id = 0
+        self._cut = [threading.Event() for _ in sessions]
+        self._guard_resend()
+
+    def set_observation(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
+        for s in self.sessions:
+            s.set_observation(*args, **kwargs)
+
+    def _running(self) -> int:
+        """Calls in flight that can still be used; a cut call no longer counts against the cap."""
+        return sum(not r["cancelled"] for r in list(self._live.values()))
+
+    def _note(self, rec: dict[str, Any], event: str, **more: Any) -> None:
+        row = {"event": event, "call": rec["id"], "session": rec["k"], "t": time.monotonic(),
+               "latency_s": round(time.monotonic() - rec["t0"], 4), **more}
+        with self._note_lock:
+            self.calls.append(row)
+            self.counts[event] = self.counts.get(event, 0) + 1
+        if self._log is not None:
+            self._log(row)
+
+    @staticmethod
+    def _transport(session: Any) -> Any:
+        """The SDK's Transport under a Dream session, or None for anything else (a scripted or fake session)."""
+        for owner in (session, getattr(session, "_policy", None), getattr(session, "_client", None)):
+            transport = getattr(owner, "_transport", None)
+            if transport is not None and hasattr(transport, "_connection"):
+                return transport
+        return None
+
+    def _guard_resend(self) -> None:
+        """The SDK resends once when a reused connection dies before the reply, and a cut looks just like that:
+        without this, a cancelled call would go out again as a new billed call."""
+        for k, session in enumerate(self.sessions):
+            transport = self._transport(session)
+            if transport is None:
+                continue
+            try:
+                from dream.errors import DreamProtocolError
+                from dream.transport import _StaleConnectionError
+            except ImportError:
+                return
+
+            def guarded(*args: Any, _send: Any = transport._exchange, _cut: Any = self._cut[k], **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
+                try:
+                    return _send(*args, **kwargs)
+                except _StaleConnectionError:
+                    if _cut.is_set():
+                        raise DreamProtocolError("cancelled by the hedge") from None
+                    raise
+            transport._exchange = guarded
+
+    def _abort(self, rec: dict[str, Any]) -> bool:
+        """Cut the call's connection, so the blocked read fails now. True if a live socket was cut. Only the socket
+        is touched from this thread: the call's own failure path drops the connection."""
+        k = rec["k"]
+        self._cut[k].set()
+        connection = getattr(self._transport(self.sessions[k]), "_connection", None)
+        sock = getattr(connection, "sock", None)
+        if sock is None:
+            return False
+        try:
+            sock.shutdown(socket.SHUT_RDWR)
+        except OSError:
+            return False
+        return True
+
+    def _cancel(self, rec: dict[str, Any], why: str) -> None:
+        if rec["cancelled"] or rec["future"].done():
+            return
+        rec["cancelled"] = True
+        rec["cut_at"] = time.monotonic()
+        self._note(rec, "cancelled", why=why, socket_cut=self._abort(rec))
+
+    def _done(self, rec: dict[str, Any], future: Any) -> None:
+        self._live.pop(rec["id"], None)
+        if rec["cancelled"]:
+            self._note(rec, "stopped", after_cut_s=round(time.monotonic() - rec["cut_at"], 4))
+
+    def _idle(self) -> list[int]:
+        return [k for k in range(len(self.sessions)) if k not in self._busy or self._busy[k].done()]
+
+    def _fire(self, k: int, commanded: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        s = self.sessions[k]
+        self._cut[k].clear()
+        rec: dict[str, Any] = {"id": self._next_id, "k": k, "t0": time.monotonic(), "cancelled": False}
+        self._next_id += 1
+        f = self._pool.submit(s.get_action, commanded=commanded) if commanded is not None else \
+            self._pool.submit(s.get_action)
+        rec["future"] = f
+        self._busy[k] = f
+        self._live[rec["id"]] = rec
+        self._note(rec, "started")
+        f.add_done_callback(lambda fut: self._done(rec, fut))
+        return rec
+
+    def get_action(self, commanded: Any = None) -> Any:
+        from concurrent.futures import FIRST_COMPLETED, wait
+
+        # A Dream client runs one get_action at a time: a session whose call is still unwinding sits this round out
+        # instead of raising "another get_action() is running".
+        idle = self._idle()
+        if not idle:                  # every session still busy: wait for the first to free up
+            wait(list(self._busy.values()), return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
+            idle = self._idle()
+        room = max(1, self.max_in_flight - self._running())
+        calls = [self._fire(k, commanded) for k in idle[:min(room, self.max_calls)]]
+        started, owed = len(calls), 0
+        first_error: BaseException | None = None
+        running = {r["future"]: r for r in calls}
+        while running or owed:
+            now = time.monotonic()
+            live = [r for r in running.values() if not r["cancelled"]]
+            timeout = 0.02 if owed else None
+            if not owed and self.cutoff_s is not None and live and started < self.max_calls:
+                timeout = max(0.02, min(r["t0"] + self.cutoff_s for r in live) - now)
+            done, _ = wait(set(running) or set(self._busy.values()), timeout=timeout, return_when=FIRST_COMPLETED)
+            for f in [f for f in done if f in running]:
+                rec = running.pop(f)
+                if rec["cancelled"]:
+                    self._note(rec, "dropped", why="reply or error of a cancelled call")
+                    continue
+                if f.exception() is not None:
+                    self._note(rec, "error", error=type(f.exception()).__name__)
+                    first_error = first_error or f.exception()
+                    continue
+                self._note(rec, "finished")
+                for other in running.values():
+                    self._cancel(other, "lost the race")
+                self.wins[rec["k"]] += 1
+                self.last_winner = rec["k"]
+                self.counts["applied"] += 1
+                return f.result()
+            if self.cutoff_s is not None:
+                now = time.monotonic()
+                for rec in [r for r in running.values() if not r["cancelled"] and now - r["t0"] >= self.cutoff_s]:
+                    if started + owed >= self.max_calls:
+                        break                     # nothing left to replace it with: let it finish
+                    if self._idle() or self._transport(self.sessions[rec["k"]]) is not None:
+                        self._cancel(rec, f"older than the {self.cutoff_s} s cutoff")
+                        owed += 1
+            while owed and self._idle() and self._running() < self.max_in_flight:
+                rec = self._fire(self._idle()[0], commanded)
+                running[rec["future"]] = rec
+                started += 1
+                owed -= 1
+        raise first_error if first_error is not None else RuntimeError("no Dream session answered")
+
+    def stats(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Counts for match.json. `started` is what the account may be billed for; `applied` is what was used."""
+        with self._note_lock:
+            return {**self.counts, "sessions": len(self.sessions), "cutoff_s": self.cutoff_s,
+                    "max_in_flight": self.max_in_flight, "max_calls": self.max_calls}
+
+    def close(self) -> None:
+        for rec in list(self._live.values()):
+            self._cancel(rec, "closing")
+        for s in self.sessions:
+            try:
+                s.close()
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+        self._pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
+
+

     """The run was told to stop (internal)."""

+def apply_render_quality(env: Any) -> str:
+    """Keep the sim real-time. With software rendering (MUJOCO_GL=osmesa: cloud VMs, a container without a GPU) the
+    example's 4096-px shadow maps and 4x anti-aliasing cost ~320 ms per 3-camera capture (one per 200 ms of control),
+    so physics ran at ~0.55x the match clock (tools/render_bench.py, 8 Nov). 'fast' drops shadows and anti-aliasing:
+    ~50 ms. SIM_RENDER=full|fast|auto (auto = fast under osmesa, full with a GPU: egl/glfw/wgl)."""
+    mode = os.environ.get("SIM_RENDER", "auto").strip().lower()
+    if mode == "auto":
+        mode = "fast" if os.environ.get("MUJOCO_GL", "").lower() == "osmesa" else "full"
+    if mode == "fast":
+        env.model.light_castshadow[:] = 0
+        env.model.vis.quality.offsamples = 0
+        if getattr(env, "renderer", None) is not None:         # rebuilt on the next render with the new quality
+            env.renderer.close()
+            env.renderer = None
+    return mode
+
+
+class PipelinedPolicy:
+    """N Dream sessions used in turn, for throughput: a Dream client runs one get_action at a time, so with a ~1 s
+    round trip one session can't ask more than ~1 Hz. Fed the same observations, sessions take requests in
+    rotation, so a new chunk can be asked for every 0.5 s with two or three in flight (sim_station._async_control
+    installs each reply at its own clock row and drops one overtaken by a newer reply). N x the Dream usage."""
+
+    def __init__(self, sessions: list[Any]):
+        self.sessions = sessions
+
+    def set_observation(self, *args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
+        for s in self.sessions:
+            s.set_observation(*args, **kwargs)
+
+    def close(self) -> None:
+        for s in self.sessions:
+            try:
+                s.close()
+            except Exception:
+                pass
+
+
+class _MiniPacer:
+    """dream.pacing.Pacer's rule for an SDK without it: async while the median round trip is under sync_above_ms."""
+
+    def __init__(self, execution_horizon: int = 30, action_hz: float = 20.0, sync_above_ms: float = 700.0):
+        self.execution_horizon, self.action_hz, self.sync_above_ms = execution_horizon, action_hz, sync_above_ms
+        self._rtt: deque[float] = deque(maxlen=5)
+        self.mode = "async"
+
+    def record(self, rtt_s: float) -> None:
+        self._rtt.append(rtt_s)
+        median = sorted(self._rtt)[len(self._rtt) // 2]
+        self.mode = "sync" if median * 1e3 > self.sync_above_ms else "async"
+
+    def refill_due(self, executed: int, remaining: int) -> bool:
+        if self.mode == "sync" or not self._rtt:
+            return remaining <= 0
+        rtt = min(self._rtt)
+        return executed + rtt * self.action_hz >= self.execution_horizon or remaining / self.action_hz <= rtt + 0.1
+
+

+    def _joint_telemetry(self, data: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:

+                except Exception:

         self._finish_run(policy, pool, IDLE)
 
+    def _async_control(self, policy: Any, pool: ThreadPoolExecutor, windows: dict, model: str, poses: bool,
+                       steps: int) -> None:
+        """Dream's async pacing, as `dream.pacing` and `dream.smoothing` document it for a control loop:
+
+        - every 50 ms one row of the current chunk is commanded and physics steps (it never holds for inference);
+        - one session: the next get_action goes out when the SDK's Pacer says so (refill_due), on its own thread,
+          with `commanded` = the joints last commanded, so the SDK's seam join starts exactly where the arm is;
+        - N sessions (PipelinedPolicy): a request goes out every `execution_horizon` rows to an idle session, so
+          with a ~1 s round trip two or three are in flight and a new chunk still lands every 0.5 s; a reply
+          overtaken by a newer one is dropped, and each takeover is cross-faded over RAMP rows locally (the SDK's
+          seam join only knows its own session's previous chunk);
+        - each reply is installed at its clock row: the rows the loop ran while it was in flight;
+        - a chunk that runs dry (a late reply) holds its last target until the next lands.
+        execution_horizon 10 at 20 Hz = a new chunk every 0.5 s = 2 Hz calls. Returns only by raising."""
+        RAMP = 6
+        dc, env = self.dc, self.env
+        try:
+            from dream.pacing import Pacer
+        except ImportError:                     # an SDK without pacing: same rule, smallest form
+            Pacer = _MiniPacer
+        horizon = int(self.execution_horizon)
+        pacer = Pacer(execution_horizon=horizon, action_hz=20.0, sync_above_ms=float(self.sync_above_ms))
+        stats = self.dream_stats
+        sessions = policy.sessions if isinstance(policy, PipelinedPolicy) else [policy]
+        stats["sessions"] = len(sessions)
+        req_pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(sessions), thread_name_prefix="dream-request")
+        inflight: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
+        seq = installed_seq = 0
+        last_request_rows: int | None = None
+        chunk = None                    # (left rows, right rows) of the plan being executed
+        blend: dict[str, Any] | None = None
+        idx = executed = rows_total = restarts = 0
+        retry_at = 0.0
+        targets = self._current_targets()
+        # Absolute 20 Hz schedule: a step that renders the cameras (~80 ms) is made up by the quicker steps after it,
+        # so simulated time keeps pace with the match clock instead of losing every render's overrun.
+        next_t = time.monotonic()
+        try:
+            while True:
+                self._check_stop()
+                tick = time.monotonic()
+                busy = {r["k"] for r in inflight}
+                idle = [k for k in range(len(sessions)) if k not in busy]
+                if idle and tick >= retry_at:
+                    if len(sessions) == 1:
+                        due = not inflight and (chunk is None or pacer.refill_due(executed, len(chunk[0]) - idx))
+                    else:
+                        due = (chunk is not None or not inflight) and (
+                            last_request_rows is None or rows_total - last_request_rows >= horizon
+                            or (chunk is not None and idx >= len(chunk[0])))
+                    if due:
+                        k = idle[0]
+                        commanded = None
+                        if chunk is not None and self.policy_kind == "dream":
+                            commanded = {"q_l_chunk": [float(v) for v in targets["left"]],
+                                         "q_r_chunk": [float(v) for v in targets["right"]]}
+                        obs = dc.observation(env, windows, self.prompt, model, poses)
+                        session = sessions[k]
+                        fut = (req_pool.submit(session.get_action, commanded=commanded) if commanded is not None
+                               else req_pool.submit(session.get_action))
+                        seq += 1
+                        inflight.append({"future": fut, "k": k, "sent_at": tick, "sent_rows": rows_total,
+                                         "obs": obs, "seq": seq})
+                        last_request_rows = rows_total
+                        stats["requests"] += 1
+                        stats["request_t"].append(round(tick - (self._run_started or tick), 3))
+                for r in [r for r in inflight if r["future"].done()]:
+                    inflight.remove(r)
+                    err = r["future"].exception()
+                    if err is not None:
+                        stats["errors"] += 1
+                        self._record_trip(windows, r["obs"], r["sent_at"], None, f"{type(err).__name__}: {err}")
+                        wait_s = self._retry_wait(err)
+                        if type(err).__name__ == "DreamSessionExpired" and restarts < 3:
+                            restarts += 1
+                        elif wait_s is not None:
+                            retry_at = time.monotonic() + wait_s
+                        elif not poses and self.policy_kind == "dream" and dc.needs_poses(err):
+                            self._close_policy(policy)
+                            poses = True
+                            policy = self._open_with_retry(model, poses)
+                            self._live_policy = policy
+                            sessions = policy.sessions if isinstance(policy, PipelinedPolicy) else [policy]
+                            dc.feed(policy, env, windows, self.prompt, model, steps, every=0, station=poses)
+                            inflight, chunk, blend, last_request_rows = [], None, None, None
+                            break
+                        else:
+                            raise err
+                        continue
+                    reply = r["future"].result()
+                    restarts = 0
+                    rtt = time.monotonic() - r["sent_at"]
+                    pacer.record(rtt)
+                    stats["replies"] += 1
+                    stats["rtt_s"].append(round(rtt, 3))
+                    stats["mode"] = getattr(pacer, "mode", stats["mode"]) if len(sessions) == 1 else "pipelined"
+                    self._record_trip(windows, r["obs"], r["sent_at"], reply, None)
+                    if r["seq"] < installed_seq:            # a newer chunk already took over
+                        stats["stale"] = stats.get("stale", 0) + 1
+                        continue
+                    adjustments: list[str] = []
+                    left, right = dc.validate_chunk(env, reply, 48, verbose=False, adjustments=adjustments)
+                    clock = 0 if chunk is None else min(rows_total - r["sent_rows"], len(left) - 1)
+                    if len(sessions) > 1 and chunk is not None:
+                        blend = {"old": chunk, "at": idx, "n": 0}
+                    chunk, idx, executed, installed_seq = (left, right), clock, 0, r["seq"]
+                    with self._lock:
+                        self.chunks += 1
+                        self.clamps += len(adjustments)
+                        if self.state == STARTING:
+                            self.state = RUNNING
+                if chunk is not None and idx < len(chunk[0]):
+                    left_row, right_row = chunk[0][idx].copy(), chunk[1][idx].copy()
+                    if blend is not None:
+                        old, at = blend["old"], min(blend["at"], len(blend["old"][0]) - 1)
+                        w = (blend["n"] + 1) / RAMP
+                        left_row = (1 - w) * old[0][at] + w * left_row
+                        right_row = (1 - w) * old[1][at] + w * right_row
+                        blend["at"] += 1
+                        blend["n"] += 1
+                        if blend["n"] >= RAMP - 1:
+                            blend = None
+                    targets = {"left": left_row, "right": right_row}
+                    idx += 1
+                    executed += 1
+                elif chunk is not None:
+                    stats["dry_rows"] = stats.get("dry_rows", 0) + 1   # reply late: hold the last target
+                env.step(targets["left"], targets["right"])
+                if steps % dc.FRAME_EVERY_STEPS == 0:
+                    dc.capture(env, windows, keep=True)
+                    self._last_render = time.monotonic()
+                    dc.feed(policy, env, windows, self.prompt, model, steps, station=poses)
+                elif time.monotonic() - self._last_render > 1.0:
+                    self._render()
+                steps += 1
+                rows_total += 1
+                self.hold = targets
+                next_t += 0.05
+                if self.realtime:
+                    lag = next_t - time.monotonic()
+                    if lag > 0:
+                        time.sleep(lag)
+                    elif lag < -1.0:        # over a second behind: the machine can't keep up; don't burst to catch up
+                        stats["resyncs"] = stats.get("resyncs", 0) + 1
+                        next_t = time.monotonic()
+        finally:
+            req_pool.shutdown(wait=False, cancel_futures=True)
+
+    def dream_summary(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
+        """Call rate and round trips for match.json: requests per second of run, median/p90 round trip."""
+        s = self.dream_stats
+        rtt = sorted(s["rtt_s"])
+        t = s["request_t"]
+        span = (t[-1] - t[0]) if len(t) > 1 else 0.0
+        return {"mode": s["mode"], "execution_horizon": self.execution_horizon, "requests": s["requests"],
+                "replies": s["replies"], "errors": s["errors"], "dry_rows": s.get("dry_rows", 0),
+                "call_hz": round((len(t) - 1) / span, 3) if span > 0 else None,
+                "rtt_p50_s": rtt[len(rtt) // 2] if rtt else None,
+                "rtt_p90_s": rtt[int(0.9 * (len(rtt) - 1))] if rtt else None,
+                **({"hedge": self.hedged.stats()} if getattr(self, "hedged", None) is not None else {})}
+
     def _first_reply_pending(self) -> bool:
         with self._lock:
             return self.state == STARTING
 
     def _open_with_retry(self, model: str, poses: bool) -> Any:
+        pipe = max(1, int(getattr(self, "dream_pipeline", 1)))
+        if pipe > 1 and self.execution_horizon:
+            with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=pipe) as ex:
+                return PipelinedPolicy(list(ex.map(lambda _: self._open_one(model, poses), range(pipe))))
+        n = max(1, int(getattr(self, "hedge", 1)))
+        if n == 1 or self.policy_kind != "dream":
+            return self._open_one(model, poses)
+        with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=n) as ex:
+            sessions = list(ex.map(lambda _: self._open_one(model, poses), range(n)))
+        self.hedged = HedgedPolicy(sessions, cutoff_s=getattr(self, "hedge_cutoff_s", None),
+                                   max_in_flight=getattr(self, "hedge_max_in_flight", None),
+                                   max_calls=getattr(self, "hedge_max_calls", None))
+        return self.hedged
+

         while True:
             self._check_stop()
             try:

+"""Dream's async pacing in the sim (sim_station._async_control): 2 Hz calls, arms never hold for inference."""
+import json
+import time
+import urllib.request
+
+import pytest
+
+from sim_station import SimStation
+
+
+def call(sim, method, path, body=None):
+    req = urllib.request.Request(sim.api_url + path, method=method,
+                                 data=json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None,
+                                 headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})
+    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as resp:
+        return resp.status, json.loads(resp.read() or b"{}")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("latency, pipeline", [(0.05, 1), (0.4, 1), (0.9, 2), (1.3, 3)])
+def test_async_pacing_calls_at_2hz_and_physics_keeps_running(latency, pipeline):
+    """One session reaches 2 Hz while its round trip is under 0.5 s; past that, sessions in turn (pipeline)."""
+    sim = SimStation(policy="scripted", scene="hill_climb", realtime=True, api_port=0, view_port=0,
+                     scripted_latency_s=latency, execution_horizon=10)
+    sim.dream_pipeline = pipeline
+    sim = sim.start()
+    try:
+        assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "go"})[0] == 200
+        t0, sim_t0 = time.monotonic(), None
+        while time.monotonic() - t0 < 8:
+            time.sleep(0.25)
+            with sim._lock:
+                t = sim._poses.get("referee", {}).get("t") if sim._poses.get("referee") else None
+            if sim_t0 is None and sim.dream_stats["replies"] >= 1:
+                sim_t0, wall0 = t, time.monotonic()
+        with sim._lock:
+            sim_t1 = sim._poses["referee"]["t"]
+        wall = time.monotonic() - wall0
+        s = sim.dream_summary()
+        assert s["mode"] == ("async" if pipeline == 1 else "pipelined") and s["errors"] == 0
+        assert 1.5 <= s["call_hz"] <= 2.6, s                # a new chunk every ~0.5 s; about 12 gaps in the window
+        assert (sim_t1 - sim_t0) / wall > 0.85, (sim_t1 - sim_t0, wall)   # physics never paused for a reply
+        assert s.get("dry_rows", 0) < 10, s                  # the next chunk lands before the current runs out
+    finally:
+        sim.close()
+
+
+def test_sync_mode_is_unchanged_without_a_horizon():
+    sim = SimStation(policy="scripted", scene="hill_climb", realtime=True, api_port=0, view_port=0)
+    try:
+        assert sim.execution_horizon is None and sim.dream_stats["mode"] == "sync"
+    finally:
+        sim.close()

+"""Offline benchmark coverage. Every HTTP entry point is blocked or replaced with a fake."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import base64
+import copy
+import io
+import json
+import threading
+import urllib.error
+import urllib.request
+
+import pytest

+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.verifiers import openrouter_request_body, openrouter_transport, parse_monitor_reply
+from tools import bench_judges as bench
+
+KEY = "test-only-credential-must-never-appear-in-reports"
+JPEG = b"\xff\xd8offline-frame\xff\xd9"
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def no_network(monkeypatch):
+    def blocked(*args, **kwargs):
+        raise AssertionError("network is forbidden in these tests")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", blocked)
+    monkeypatch.setenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY", KEY)
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def plan():
+    return load_plan(bench.ROOT / "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml")
+
+
+def response(done=False, guard=False, cost=.01, text=None):
+    answer = text if text is not None else '<json>' + json.dumps({
+        "stage_done": done, "guard": guard, "confidence": .95, "reason": "offline"}) + '</json>'
+    return {"choices": [{"message": {"content": answer}}],
+            "usage": {"cost": cost, "prompt_tokens": 100, "completion_tokens": 20}}
+
+
+def fake_http(monkeypatch, fn):
+    def send(request, timeout):
+        assert request.full_url == "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions"
+        assert request.get_header("Authorization") == f"Bearer {KEY}"
+        assert KEY not in request.data.decode()
+        return io.BytesIO(json.dumps(fn(json.loads(request.data))).encode())
+    monkeypatch.setattr(urllib.request, "urlopen", send)
+
+
+def test_translation_preserves_system_text_images_and_wire_fields():
+    image = {"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": "image/jpeg",
+                                         "data": base64.b64encode(JPEG).decode()}}
+    payload = {"system": [{"type": "text", "text": "station rules"}], "max_tokens": 4000,
+               "output_config": {"effort": "low"}, "_frames_sig": "private bookkeeping",
+               "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": [{"type": "text", "text": "overhead"}, image,
+                                                           {"type": "text", "text": "question"}]},
+                            {"role": "assistant", "content": "prior text"}]}
+    body = openrouter_request_body("vision/model", payload)
+    assert bench.validate_translation(payload, body) == 1
+    assert body["messages"][0] == {"role": "system", "content": "station rules"}
+    broken = copy.deepcopy(body)
+    broken["messages"][1]["content"].reverse()
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="translation mismatch"):
+        bench.validate_translation(payload, broken)
+
+
+def test_raw_stage_answer_survives_monitor_confidence_gate():
+    verdict = parse_monitor_reply('<json>{"stage_done": true, "confidence": 0.2}</json>', 0)
+    assert verdict.stage_done is True and verdict.done is None
+    negative = parse_monitor_reply('<json>{"stage_done": false, "confidence": 0.9}</json>', 0)
+    assert negative.stage_done is False and negative.done is None
+    assert parse_monitor_reply("broken", 0).stage_done is None
+
+
+def test_race_collects_loser_cost_after_winning_latency(plan, monkeypatch):
+    release_loser = threading.Event()
+    slow_finished = threading.Event()
+    def respond(body):
+        if body["model"] == "slow/model":
+            assert release_loser.wait(5)
+            slow_finished.set()
+            return response(cost=.07)
+        return response(cost=.01)
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, respond)
+    original = bench.Accounting.drain
+    def drain(self, expected, timeout_s):
+        assert not slow_finished.is_set()  # check_now returned while the loser was still waiting
+        release_loser.set()
+        return original(self, expected, timeout_s)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(bench.Accounting, "drain", drain)
+    row = bench.run_trial(plan, bench.Judge("openrouter", ("fast/model", "slow/model")),
+                          ("photo", "ball_1", False, False), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,
+                          dry_run=False, timeout_s=5)
+    assert slow_finished.is_set() and row["drained"]
+    assert row["stage_correct"] and row["guard_correct"]
+    assert row["cost_usd"] == pytest.approx(.08)
+    assert row["attempts_finished"] == 2
+    summary = bench.summaries([row])[0]
+    assert summary["cost_per_call_usd"] == pytest.approx(.04)
+    assert summary["cost_per_trial_usd"] == pytest.approx(.08)
+
+
+def test_real_monitor_hedge_recovers_from_bad_json_and_counts_every_copy(plan, monkeypatch):
+    lock = threading.Lock()
+    counter = 0
+    def respond(body):
+        nonlocal counter
+        with lock:
+            counter += 1
+            n = counter
+        return response(cost=.02, text="bad JSON" if n == 1 else None)
+    fake_http(monkeypatch, respond)
+    row = bench.run_trial(plan, bench.Judge("openrouter", ("vision/model",), 2),
+                          ("photo", "ball_1", False, False), {"overhead": JPEG}, 1,
+                          dry_run=False, timeout_s=5)
+    assert row["stage_correct"] and not row["parse_failure"]
+    assert counter == row["attempts_finished"] == 2
+    assert row["cost_usd"] == pytest.approx(.04)
+    assert plan.claude_user.get("hedge", 1) == 1
+

+@pytest.mark.parametrize("text,parse_failure,transport_failure", [

+def test_paid_empty_or_malformed_response_retains_cost(plan, monkeypatch, text, parse_failure, transport_failure):

+def test_http_failure_callback_contains_no_exception_message(monkeypatch):

+def test_dry_run_covers_all_configs_cases_and_hedges_without_keys(tmp_path, monkeypatch, capsys):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("OPENROUTER_API_KEY")

+    assert (tmp_path / "summary.csv").read_text().startswith("judge,trials,")
+    assert KEY not in capsys.readouterr().out + (tmp_path / "results.json").read_text()

+"""harness.cable_end: ferrule candidates and the USB-C / USB-A classifier."""
+
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import cv2
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from harness.cable_end import CableEndClassifier, crop, ferrules
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+MODEL = ROOT / "models" / "cable_end.onnx"
+DATA = Path(__file__).parent / "data"
+ORANGE = (0, 70, 235)   # BGR, the anodised ferrule
+
+
+def _table():
+    return np.full((360, 640, 3), (200, 205, 208), np.uint8)
+
+
+def test_a_ferrule_is_a_candidate_and_a_cable_run_is_not():
+    im = _table()
+    cv2.rectangle(im, (300, 160), (340, 182), ORANGE, -1)        # ferrule: compact, filled
+    cv2.line(im, (20, 300), (600, 330), ORANGE, 4)               # cable: long and thin
+    c = ferrules(im)
+    assert len(c) == 1 and abs(c[0]["cx"] - 320) < 3 and abs(c[0]["cy"] - 171) < 3
+    assert c[0]["side"] > 40 * 2                                  # the crop keeps the tip and the relief in view
+    assert crop(im, c[0]).shape == (96, 96, 3)
+
+
+def test_no_orange_no_candidates():
+    assert ferrules(_table()) == []
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_photo_ends_are_labelled():
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL, min_prob=0.5)
+    for name, want in (("cable_usbc_end.jpg", "usb_c"), ("cable_usba_end.jpg", "usb_a")):
+        ends = clf.ends_jpeg((DATA / name).read_bytes())
+        assert ends, name
+        best = max(ends, key=lambda e: e.prob)
+        assert best.label == want, (name, [(e.label, round(e.prob, 2)) for e in ends])
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_batches_of_any_size_in_any_order():
+    # OpenCV 5.0 DNN crashed the process when a net was reused with a bigger batch than before (2, 5, 4, 4, 16)
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL)
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
+    one = rng.random((1, 3, 96, 96)).astype(np.float32)
+    ref = clf._forward(one)
+    for b in (2, 5, 4, 4, 16, 3, 24, 1, 9, 17):
+        x = rng.random((b, 3, 96, 96)).astype(np.float32)
+        x[0] = one[0]
+        out = clf._forward(x)
+        assert out.shape == (b, 3)
+        assert np.abs(out[0] - ref[0]).max() < 1e-4
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model")
+def test_plain_table_has_no_ends():
+    clf = CableEndClassifier(MODEL)
+    assert clf.ends(_table()) == []

     exe = tmp_path / "claude"
     exe.write_text(FAKE.format(python=sys.executable))
     exe.chmod(exe.stat().st_mode | stat.S_IEXEC)
+    if os.name == "nt":      # Windows can't run a shebang script: launch it through a .cmd next to it
+        wrapper = tmp_path / "claude.cmd"
+        wrapper.write_text(f'@"{sys.executable}" "{exe}" %*' + chr(10))
+        exe = wrapper
     log = tmp_path / "log.json"
     monkeypatch.setenv("FAKE_CLAUDE_LOG", str(log))
     monkeypatch.setenv("HARNESS_CLAUDE_BIN", str(exe))

+"""Cloud planning is offline and must never invoke gcloud in a dry-run."""
+import argparse
+import io
+import json
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+
+from cloud import collect, control
+from cloud.judge import DelayedOracle
+from cloud.run import prepare_plan
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerdict
+from experiments.sim_match import OracleVerifier
+from harness.plan import Stage
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+
+
+def test_matrix_pilot_balanced_and_full_unique():
+    full = control.tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml")
+    pilot = control.tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml", True)
+    assert len(full) == 1000 and len(pilot) == 10
+    assert len({(x["name"], x["seed"]) for x in full}) == 1000
+    assert {x["name"] for x in pilot} == {"hill_climb_v4", "hybrid_v4"}
+
+
+def job_args(judge="oracle"):
+    return argparse.Namespace(project="hill-climb-sims", region="us-central1", bucket="example",
+                              judge=judge, image="example/image:tag", job="pilot-v1", parallelism=8,
+                              matches_per_task=1, machine_type="e2-standard-2",
+                              judge_models="google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite", judge_mode="race", judge_hedge=1,
+                              dream_hedge=1, private=False)
+
+
+def test_batch_limits_and_runtime_secret_references():
+    entries = control.tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml", True)
+    job = control.job_config(job_args(), entries)
+    group = job["taskGroups"][0]
+    assert (group["taskCount"], group["parallelism"], group["taskCountPerNode"]) == (10, 8, 1)
+    assert job["allocationPolicy"]["instances"][0]["policy"]["provisioningModel"] == "SPOT"
+    assert set(group["taskSpec"]["environment"]["secretVariables"]) == {"DREAM_API_KEY"}
+    api = control.job_config(job_args("api"), entries)
+    assert "ANTHROPIC_API_KEY" in api["taskGroups"][0]["taskSpec"]["environment"]["secretVariables"]
+    assert api["labels"] != job["labels"]
+    grouped = job_args()
+    grouped.matches_per_task = 3
+    assert control.job_config(grouped, entries)["taskGroups"][0]["taskCount"] == 4
+
+
+def test_upload_failure_is_never_retried():
+    job = control.job_config(job_args(), control.tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml", True))
+    spec = job["taskGroups"][0]["taskSpec"]
+    assert spec["lifecyclePolicies"] == [{"action": "FAIL_TASK", "actionCondition": {"exitCodes": [1, 75]}}]
+
+
+def test_preflight_failure_runs_no_match_and_pins_gcloud_python(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    from cloud import entrypoint
+    seen = []
+
+    def fake_run(cmd, **kw):
+        seen.append((cmd, kw.get("env", {}).get("CLOUDSDK_PYTHON"), kw.get("env", {})))
+        return argparse.Namespace(returncode=1, stderr=b"denied")
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(entrypoint.subprocess, "run", fake_run)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(entrypoint, "vm_token", lambda: "tok")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(entrypoint, "TOKEN_FILE", tmp_path / "token")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("CLOUDSDK_CONFIG", "/host/config")
+    monkeypatch.setattr(entrypoint.subprocess, "Popen", lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail("match started"))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("OUTPUT_PREFIX", "gs://example/runs/x")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DREAM_API_KEY", "k")
+    monkeypatch.setenv("CLOUDSDK_PYTHON", "/usr/bin/python3")
+    monkeypatch.delenv("MATRIX_SHA256", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.argv", ["entrypoint", "--matrix", str(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml"), "--limit", "1",
+                                     "--out", str(tmp_path)])
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as stop:
+        entrypoint.main()
+    assert stop.value.code == entrypoint.UPLOAD_FAILED
+    assert seen and seen[0][0][:3] == ["gcloud", "storage", "cp"]
+    import sys
+    assert seen[0][1] == sys.executable
+    env = seen[0][2]
+    assert "CLOUDSDK_CONFIG" not in env
+    assert env["CLOUDSDK_AUTH_ACCESS_TOKEN_FILE"] == str(tmp_path / "token")
+
+
+def test_local_output_folder_for_home_cluster(monkeypatch, tmp_path):
+    from cloud import entrypoint
+    monkeypatch.setattr(entrypoint.subprocess, "run", lambda *a, **k: pytest.fail("gcloud used for local output"))
+    out, results = tmp_path / "out", tmp_path / "results"
+    monkeypatch.setenv("OUTPUT_PREFIX", str(results))
+    monkeypatch.setenv("DREAM_API_KEY", "k")
+    monkeypatch.delenv("MATRIX_SHA256", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.argv", ["entrypoint", "--matrix", str(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml"), "--config",
+                                     "hill_climb_v4", "--seed-start", "900", "--out", str(out), "--preflight-only"])
+    entrypoint.main()
+    assert len(list((results / "_preflight").glob("*.json"))) == 1
+    match = tmp_path / "m" / "hill_climb_v4-900"
+    match.mkdir(parents=True)
+    (match / "match.json").write_text("{}")
+    assert entrypoint.put(match, str(results / "a1") + "/", recursive=True)
+    assert (results / "a1" / "hill_climb_v4-900" / "match.json").exists()
+

+def test_openrouter_judge_and_private_network():
+    entries = control.tasks(ROOT / "cloud/matrix.yaml", True)
+    a = job_args("openrouter")
+    a.judge_models, a.judge_mode, a.judge_hedge, a.private = "m1,m2", "alternate", 2, True
+    job = control.job_config(a, entries)
+    env = job["taskGroups"][0]["taskSpec"]["environment"]
+    assert "OPENROUTER_API_KEY" in env["secretVariables"]
+    cmd = job["taskGroups"][0]["taskSpec"]["runnables"][0]["container"]["commands"]
+    assert cmd[cmd.index("--judge-models") + 1] == "m1,m2" and cmd[cmd.index("--judge-mode") + 1] == "alternate"
+    assert job["allocationPolicy"]["network"]["networkInterfaces"][0]["noExternalIpAddress"] is True
+    assert job["labels"] != control.job_config(job_args("openrouter"), entries)["labels"]
+    assert env["variables"]["HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING"] == "low"

+    assert fast["taskGroups"][0]["taskSpec"]["environment"]["variables"]["HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING"] == "none"
+    assert fast["labels"] != job["labels"]
+

+def test_oracle_uses_monitor_parallel_scheduler_and_drops_stale(monkeypatch):

+    from cloud import judge

+    assert mon.calls == 2

new file mode 100644

+"""harness.decide: with default weights the equations make the same calls as the runner's rules."""
+
+import random
+
+
+
+from harness.decide import (Action, AttemptState, DoneWeights, FailWeights, Look, StageModel, action_value,
+                            attempt_fails, choose, look_failing, plan_timeouts, run_looks)
+
+CONFIRM, WINDOW = 2, 15.0
+
+
+def runner_done(looks):
+    """The done branch of Runner._watch, transcribed (frames always present, no immediate verdicts)."""
+    consec_done, last_done_t = 0, None
+    for lk in looks:
+        failing = lk.done is False or lk.failing
+        if lk.done is True and lk.same_frames:
+            continue
+        if lk.done is True:
+            consec_done += 1
+            recent = last_done_t is not None and lk.t - last_done_t <= WINDOW
+            last_done_t = lk.t
+            if consec_done >= CONFIRM or recent:
+                return lk.t
+        elif failing and last_done_t is not None and lk.t - last_done_t <= WINDOW:
+            pass
+        elif failing:
+            consec_done = 0
+        elif lk.done is not None or lk.monitor:
+            consec_done = 0
+    return None
+
+
+def random_looks(rng):
+    t, out, have_done = 0.0, [], False
+    for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 14)):
+        t += rng.choice([1.0, 3.0, 3.0, 6.0, 12.0, 20.0])
+        done = rng.choice([True, True, False, None])
+        out.append(Look(t, done, failing=rng.random() < 0.2, same_frames=have_done and done is True and rng.random() < 0.3,
+                        monitor=rng.random() < 0.8))
+        have_done = have_done or done is True
+    return out
+
+
+def test_default_done_weights_match_the_runner_on_random_sequences():
+    rng = random.Random(7)
+    for _ in range(5000):
+        looks = random_looks(rng)
+        assert run_looks(looks) == runner_done(looks), looks
+
+
+def test_done_cases_by_hand():
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, True)]) == 6                     # confirm 2
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, False), Look(9, True)]) == 9      # second done within the window
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(25, False), Look(28, True)]) is None  # outside it, the no resets
+    assert run_looks([Look(3, True), Look(6, True, same_frames=True)]) is None  # same frames: one look, not two
+
+
+def test_fast_sensor_evidence_can_pass_a_stage_sooner_when_weighted():
+    looks = [Look(3, True, sensor_release=1.0)]
+    assert run_looks(looks) is None
+    assert run_looks(looks, DoneWeights(w_sensor_release=1.0)) == 3
+
+
+def runner_fails(a: AttemptState) -> bool:
+    """The fail rules of Runner._watch (stall-on-progress only checked past the settle gate, as here)."""
+    if a.consec_fail >= CONFIRM and (not a.monitor or a.elapsed >= 0.5 * a.timeout_s):
+        return True
+    if a.stall_s is not None and a.consec_stalled >= CONFIRM:
+        return True
+    if a.stall_s is not None and a.since_rise_s is not None and a.since_rise_s >= a.stall_s:
+        return True
+    return a.elapsed >= a.timeout_s
+
+
+def test_default_fail_weights_match_the_runner():
+    rng = random.Random(3)
+    for _ in range(20000):
+        timeout = rng.choice([10.0, 20.0, 40.0, 60.0])
+        stall = rng.choice([None, 20.0, 35.0])
+        a = AttemptState(elapsed=rng.uniform(0, 70), timeout_s=timeout, consec_fail=rng.randint(0, 3),
+                         consec_stalled=rng.randint(0, 3), since_rise_s=rng.choice([None, rng.uniform(0, 40)]),
+                         stall_s=stall, monitor=rng.random() < 0.8)
+        assert attempt_fails(a) == runner_fails(a), a
+
+
+def test_a_regression_makes_a_failing_look_not_an_instant_fail():
+    assert look_failing(None, False, progress=0.3, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=False)
+    assert not look_failing(None, False, progress=0.4, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=False)
+    assert not look_failing(None, False, progress=0.3, max_progress=0.6, done_seen=True)
+
+
+def test_sum_combination_lets_weak_signals_add_up():
+    a = AttemptState(elapsed=30, timeout_s=60, consec_fail=1, since_rise_s=10, stall_s=20)
+    assert not attempt_fails(a)                                  # every rule is half way
+    assert attempt_fails(a, FailWeights(combine="sum"))           # together they pass 1
+
+
+STAGES = [
+    StageModel("unplug", value=10, p_max=0.9, tau_c=25, t_min=5, gate=True),
+    StageModel("extra_cable", value=5, p_max=0.5, tau_c=15, t_min=4),
+    StageModel("tower", value=20, p_max=0.7, tau_c=40, t_min=8, gate=True),
+    StageModel("ball", value=5, p_max=0.6, tau_c=12, t_min=4),
+]
+
+
+def test_value_of_time_never_falls_as_time_grows():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    for i in range(len(STAGES)):
+        assert all(plan.W[i][L] <= plan.W[i][L + 1] + 1e-9 for L in range(300))
+    assert plan.value(0, 300) > plan.value(0, 60) > 0
+
+
+def test_optional_stage_gets_less_time_when_the_clock_is_short():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    assert plan.timeout(1, 60) <= plan.timeout(1, 250)
+    assert plan.timeout(1, 20) == 0 or plan.timeout(1, 20) < plan.timeout(1, 250)   # often skipped when short
+
+
+def test_gate_stage_is_never_skipped_while_it_can_still_pay():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    assert plan.timeout(0, 120) > 0 and plan.timeout(2, 100) > 0
+
+
+def test_action_choice_weighs_points_time_and_risk():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 300)
+    keep = Action("keep_going", p=0.5, cost_s=10)
+    reset = Action("reset_then_retry", p=0.7, cost_s=25)
+    risky = Action("reach_across_tower", p=0.8, cost_s=8, risk=0.3)
+    assert choose(plan, 2, 200, [keep, reset, risky]).name != "reach_across_tower"
+    assert action_value(plan, 2, 5, reset) <= action_value(plan, 2, 5, keep)   # 5 s left: a 25 s reset can't pay
+
+
+def test_action_too_long_for_the_clock_is_worth_nothing():
+    plan = plan_timeouts(STAGES, 60)
+    assert action_value(plan, 3, 3, Action("x", p=1.0, cost_s=10)) == 0.0

+"""The decide shadow inside a real Runner: logs `decide` events, agrees with the runner on defaults, never acts."""
+
+import pytest
+from conftest import stage
+from test_v2 import Script, build, events, mv
+
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+
+
+def run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, stages, items, **top):
+    path = write_plan(stages, verifier="monitor", **top)
+    log = RunLog(tmp_path / "runs")
+    result = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script(items, repeat=False), log).run()
+    return result, events(log, "decide"), log
+
+
+def test_shadow_passes_on_the_same_look_as_the_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(None, progress=0.3, frames_sig="a"), mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="b"),
+             mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="b"),           # same frames: not a second look
+             mv(True, progress=1.0, frames_sig="c")]
+    result, dec, log = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10)], items, confirm=2)
+    assert result.ok
+    assert [d["would_pass"] for d in dec] == [False, False, False, True]
+    assert [d["done_score"] for d in dec] == [0.0, 1.0, 1.0, 2.0]
+
+
+def test_stage_confirm_1_is_followed(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10, confirm=1)],
+                         [mv(None, frames_sig="a"), mv(True, frames_sig="b")], confirm=2)
+    assert result.ok and [d["would_pass"] for d in dec] == [False, True]
+
+
+def test_shadow_fails_on_the_same_look_as_the_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(None, "stalled"), mv(None, "ok"), mv(None, "stalled"), mv(None, "stalled")]
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10, stall_s=100, on_fail="skip")],
+                         items, confirm=2)
+    assert result.stages[0].reason.startswith("stalled")
+    assert [d["would_fail"] for d in dec] == [False, False, False, True]
+    assert dec[-1]["terms"]["stalled"] == 1.0
+
+
+def test_plan_weights_change_only_the_log(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    items = [mv(True, frames_sig="a"), mv(None, frames_sig="b"), mv(True, frames_sig="c"), mv(True, frames_sig="d")]
+    result, dec, _ = run(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, [stage("a", timeout_s=10)], items, confirm=2,
+                         decide={"done": {"theta": 1.0}})
+    assert result.ok
+    assert dec[0]["would_pass"] is True        # the shadow (threshold 1) would have passed on the first done
+    assert len(dec) == 3                       # the runner still decided (second done within 15 s): the shadow did not act
+
+
+def test_unknown_weight_is_a_plan_error(write_plan):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")], decide={"done": {"w_monitr": 2}})
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="unknown weight"):
+        load_plan(path)

+"""Hedged monitor calls, OpenRouter request translation, layered prompts, plan keys."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import time
+
+from conftest import stage
+
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.promptgen import Context, compose
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, to_openai_messages
+
+
+def test_openai_translation_keeps_images_and_system():
+    payload = {"system": "be a monitor", "max_tokens": 50, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": [
+        {"type": "text", "text": "is it done?"},
+        {"type": "image", "source": {"type": "base64", "media_type": "image/jpeg", "data": "QUJD"}}]}]}
+    msgs = to_openai_messages(payload)
+    assert msgs[0] == {"role": "system", "content": "be a monitor"}
+    parts = msgs[1]["content"]
+    assert parts[0] == {"type": "text", "text": "is it done?"}
+    assert parts[1]["image_url"]["url"] == "data:image/jpeg;base64,QUJD"
+
+
+def test_layered_prompt_levels():
+    pre = "The strip has no power."
+    p = f"{pre} Pull the cable out."
+    ctx = Context(prefix=pre, holding={"right": "a cable", "left": "nothing"}, tower=["blue cube"], attempt=2,
+                  progress=0.4, last_failure="the gripper slipped off the plug")
+    assert compose(p, 0, ctx) == "Pull the cable out."
+    assert compose(p, 1, ctx) == p
+    l5 = compose(p, 5, ctx)
+    assert l5.startswith(pre) and l5.endswith("Pull the cable out.")
+    for bit in ("right gripper holds a cable", "blue cube", "try 2", "40%", "slipped", "8-ball"):
+        assert bit in l5
+    assert "left gripper" not in l5
+
+
+def test_plan_reads_new_keys(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")])
+    raw = path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
+    path.write_text(raw + "\nconfirm_gap_s: 3\nprompt_level: 4\nfast: {rate_hz: 25, mode: live}\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    assert plan.confirm_gap_s == 3.0 and plan.prompt_level == 4 and plan.fast["rate_hz"] == 25
+
+
+def test_hedged_monitor_takes_the_first_good_reply(write_plan):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a")])
+    path.write_text(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") + "\nclaude: {hedge: 3}\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    delays = iter([0.4, 0.05, 0.3])
+    reply = "<json>" + json.dumps({"stage_done": True, "confidence": 0.9, "progress": 0.9, "status": "ok", "reason": "fast one"}) + "</json>"
+
+    def transport(payload):
+        time.sleep(next(delays))
+        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": reply}]}
+    mv = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=transport)
+    t = time.monotonic()
+    v = mv._call({"_frames_sig": "x"}, 0)
+    assert v.done is True and time.monotonic() - t < 0.3 and mv.hedge_wins == [1]
+
+
+def test_reasoning_level_reaches_openrouter(monkeypatch):
+    from harness.verifiers import openrouter_request_body
+    payload = {"max_tokens": 10, "output_config": {"effort": "low"}, "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": "x"}]}
+    assert openrouter_request_body("m", payload)["reasoning"] == {"effort": "low"}
+    monkeypatch.setenv("HARNESS_OPENROUTER_REASONING", "none")
+    assert openrouter_request_body("m", payload)["reasoning"] == {"enabled": False}
+
+
+def test_parser_accepts_fenced_and_bare_json():
+    from harness.verifiers import parse_monitor_reply
+    obj = '{"stage_done": true, "confidence": 0.9, "progress": 1, "status": "ok", "reason": "r", "guard": false}'
+    for text in (f"<json>{obj}</json>", f"thinking...\n```json\n{obj}\n```", f"Q1: yes. {obj}"):
+        v = parse_monitor_reply(text, 0)
+        assert v.done is True and v.guard is False, text

+"""final-harness: the pieces added when main, start-pose-live-guard and hybrid-harness were put together."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+from conftest import stage
+
+from harness import runner as runner_mod
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.runner import Runner
+
+
+class _Station:
+    dry_run = False
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.posts: list[str] = []
+
+    def reset(self) -> dict:
+        self.posts.append("/reset")
+        return {"state": "resetting"}
+
+    def home(self) -> dict:
+        self.posts.append("/home")
+        return {"state": "homing"}
+
+    def status(self) -> dict:
+        return {"state": "idle"}
+
+    def wait_for_state(self, states, timeout_s, poll_s=0.5) -> dict:
+        return {"state": "idle"}
+
+
+class _Guard:
+    def __init__(self, armed: bool) -> None:
+        self.armed, self.tripped = armed, None
+
+
+def _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, guard=None) -> tuple[Runner, _Station]:
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a")]))
+    st = _Station()
+    r = Runner(plan, st, None, {}, RunLog(tmp_path / "run"), sleep=lambda s: None)
+    r.live_guard = guard
+    r._t0 = r._clock()
+    return r, st
+
+
+def _events(r: Runner, kind: str) -> list[dict]:
+    lines = (r.log.path / "events.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines()
+    return [e for e in map(json.loads, lines) if e.get("event") == kind or e.get("kind") == kind]
+
+
+def test_reset_is_skipped_while_the_live_guard_is_armed(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r, st = _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, _Guard(armed=True))
+    r._reset_and_wait("auto reset 1 after: SVD did not converge")
+    assert st.posts == []                       # /reset moves both arms and can't be cancelled: not while armed
+    assert _events(r, "reset_skipped")
+
+
+def test_reset_goes_through_the_motion_checks(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r, st = _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, _Guard(armed=False))
+    r._stop_event.set()                         # operator stop: nothing that moves an arm may follow
+    with pytest.raises(runner_mod._Stop):
+        r._reset_and_wait("reset_first")
+    assert st.posts == []
+
+
+def test_reset_is_sent_when_the_guard_is_disarmed(write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r, st = _runner(write_plan, tmp_path, _Guard(armed=False))
+    r._reset_and_wait("reset_first")
+    assert st.posts == ["/reset"]
+
+
+# ---- --match --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+from harness import cli  # noqa: E402
+
+PLANS = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "plans"
+
+
+def _args(*extra: str):
+    return cli._parser().parse_args(["plans/x.yaml", "--live", *extra])
+
+
+def test_match_plans_pass_the_match_check():
+    for name in ("hill_climb_v4.yaml", "hybrid_v4.yaml", "hybrid_v4_long.yaml"):
+        assert cli.match_problems(_args("--match"), load_plan(PLANS / name)) == [], name
+
+
+def test_match_refuses_budget_and_human_verifiers(write_plan):
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a")]))          # conftest plans default to the manual verifier
+    probs = cli.match_problems(_args("--match", "--budget", "600"), plan)
+    assert any("--budget" in p for p in probs) and any("answered by a person" in p for p in probs)
+    assert any("typed y/n" in p for p in cli.match_problems(_args("--match", "--verifier", "monitor+manual"), plan))
+    assert cli.match_problems(_args("--match", "--verifier", "monitor"), plan) == []
+
+
+def test_match_turns_the_operator_off(write_plan, monkeypatch):
+    seen = {}
+
+    def stop_before_running(*a, **k):
+        raise SystemExit(0)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "RunLog", stop_before_running)
+    monkeypatch.setattr("builtins.input", lambda *_: "yes")
+    orig = cli._parser
+
+    def parser():
+        p = orig()
+        parse = p.parse_args
+
+        def wrapped(argv=None):
+            ns = parse(argv)
+            seen["ns"] = ns
+            return ns
+        p.parse_args = wrapped
+        return p
+    monkeypatch.setattr(cli, "_parser", parser)
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit):
+        cli.main([str(PLANS / "hill_climb_v4.yaml"), "--live", "--match"])
+    assert seen["ns"].no_operator is True

+"""Hedged Dream calls: losers are cut, slow calls are replaced within caps, and a cancelled reply is never used.
+
+Offline: fake sessions, and a local fake gateway behind the SDK's real Transport. No Dream call is made."""
+
+import json
+import select
+import threading
+import time
+from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
+
+import pytest
+
+from sim_station import HedgedPolicy
+
+
+class Fake:
+    """A session whose call ends after `delay` seconds or when `stop` is set. It does not have a socket to cut."""
+    in_flight = 0
+    peak = 0
+    lock = threading.Lock()
+
+    def __init__(self, name, *delays):
+        self.name, self.delays, self.n = name, list(delays), 0
+
+    def set_observation(self, *a, **k):
+        pass
+
+    def get_action(self):
+        with Fake.lock:
+            Fake.in_flight += 1
+            Fake.peak = max(Fake.peak, Fake.in_flight)
+        d = self.delays[min(self.n, len(self.delays) - 1)]
+        self.n += 1
+        time.sleep(d)
+        with Fake.lock:
+            Fake.in_flight -= 1
+        return {"from": self.name, "call": self.n}
+
+    def close(self):
+        pass
+
+
+@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
+def reset():
+    Fake.in_flight = Fake.peak = 0
+
+
+def events(hp, name):
+    return [c for c in hp.calls if c["event"] == name]
+
+
+def test_first_reply_wins_and_the_loser_is_cancelled_and_logged():
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Fake("slow", 0.4), Fake("fast", 0.02)])
+    assert hp.get_action()["from"] == "fast" and hp.last_winner == 1
+    time.sleep(0.5)
+    assert [c["session"] for c in events(hp, "cancelled")] == [0]
+    assert events(hp, "cancelled")[0]["why"] == "lost the race"
+    assert hp.counts["started"] == 2 and hp.counts["finished"] == 1 and hp.counts["applied"] == 1 and hp.counts["stopped"] == 1
+    assert all("latency_s" in c for c in hp.calls)
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_a_cancelled_calls_late_reply_is_never_used():
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Fake("late", 0.3, 0.01), Fake("fast", 0.02, 0.5)])
+    first = hp.get_action()
+    assert first["from"] == "fast"
+    time.sleep(0.4)                     # the loser has now finished with a real reply
+    second = hp.get_action()
+    assert second == {"from": "late", "call": 2}        # a fresh call, not the first call's stale reply
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_cutoff_replaces_a_slow_call_on_a_free_session():
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Fake("hung", 2.0), Fake("spare", 0.02)], cutoff_s=0.1, max_in_flight=1, max_calls=3)
+    t = time.monotonic()
+    assert hp.get_action()["from"] == "spare" and time.monotonic() - t < 1.0
+    assert events(hp, "cancelled")[0]["session"] == 0
+    assert "cutoff" in events(hp, "cancelled")[0]["why"]
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_the_caps_hold_when_every_call_is_slow():
+    sessions = [Fake(str(i), 0.25) for i in range(4)]
+    hp = HedgedPolicy(sessions, cutoff_s=0.05, max_in_flight=2, max_calls=3)
+    assert hp.get_action() is not None
+    assert Fake.peak <= 3                       # 2 running, plus at most one fake that can't be cut
+    assert hp.counts["started"] <= 3
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_the_last_call_is_not_cut_without_a_replacement():
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Fake("only", 0.2)], cutoff_s=0.05, max_calls=1)
+    assert hp.get_action()["from"] == "only"
+    assert not events(hp, "cancelled")
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_every_session_failing_raises():
+    class Bad(Fake):
+        def get_action(self):
+            raise RuntimeError("boom")
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Bad("a"), Bad("b")])
+    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="boom"):
+        hp.get_action()
+    assert hp.counts["error"] == 2
+    hp.close()
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------- the SDK's real Transport against a local fake gateway
+transport = pytest.importorskip("dream.transport")
+
+
+class Gateway(ThreadingHTTPServer):
+    daemon_threads = True
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        super().__init__(("127.0.0.1", 0), Handler)
+        self.delays, self.requests, self.saw_disconnect = [], 0, threading.Event()
+
+    def handle_error(self, request, client_address):
+        pass                                     # a cut connection is the point of these tests
+
+
+class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
+    protocol_version = "HTTP/1.1"
+
+    def log_message(self, *a):
+        pass
+
+    def do_POST(self):
+        self.rfile.read(int(self.headers["Content-Length"]))
+        srv = self.server
+        srv.requests += 1
+        delay = srv.delays.pop(0) if srv.delays else 0.0
+        end = time.monotonic() + delay
+        while time.monotonic() < end:                    # holds the reply; notices the client going away
+            ready, _, _ = select.select([self.connection], [], [], 0.01)
+            if ready and not self.connection.recv(1, 2):  # MSG_PEEK: EOF
+                srv.saw_disconnect.set()
+                self.close_connection = True
+                return
+        body = json.dumps({"n": srv.requests}).encode()
+        self.send_response(200)
+        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
+        self.end_headers()
+        self.wfile.write(body)
+
+
+class Session:
+    def __init__(self, port):
+        self._transport = transport.Transport("127.0.0.1", port, 5000)
+
+    def set_observation(self, *a, **k):
+        pass
+
+    def get_action(self):
+        return self._transport.request({"x": 1}, {})
+
+    def close(self):
+        self._transport.close()
+
+
+@pytest.fixture
+def gateway():
+    g = Gateway()
+    threading.Thread(target=g.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
+    yield g
+    g.shutdown()
+
+
+def test_a_real_connection_is_cut_at_once_and_not_resent(gateway):
+    port = gateway.server_address[1]
+    slow, fast = Session(port), Session(port)
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([slow, fast])
+    gateway.delays[:] = [0.0, 0.0]
+    hp.get_action()                              # both connections are now reused keep-alives
+    time.sleep(0.2)
+    before = gateway.requests
+    gateway.delays[:] = [3.0, 0.02]              # the first request to land is slow, the second fast
+    t = time.monotonic()
+    assert hp.get_action() is not None and time.monotonic() - t < 2.0
+    assert gateway.saw_disconnect.wait(1.0)      # the server really saw the connection close
+    time.sleep(0.5)
+    stopped = events(hp, "stopped")
+    assert stopped and stopped[-1]["after_cut_s"] < 0.5
+    assert gateway.requests == before + 2        # the cut call was not sent again by the SDK's retry
+    hp.close()
+
+
+def test_a_session_works_again_after_being_cut(gateway):
+    port = gateway.server_address[1]
+    hp = HedgedPolicy([Session(port), Session(port)])
+    gateway.delays[:] = [3.0, 0.02]
+    hp.get_action()
+    gateway.delays[:] = [0.0, 0.0]
+    time.sleep(0.2)
+    assert hp.get_action() is not None           # the cut session reconnects for the next call
+    assert sum(hp.wins) == 2
+    hp.close()

+"""Which way a cube's hole runs, and upright sticks, on drawn test images."""
+
+import io
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import pytest
+from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
+
+from harness.detect import detect, load_config, top_cube_hole
+
+CFG = load_config(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "plans" / "table_colors.yaml")
+BLUE, PINK, YELLOW, DARK, WOOD = (40, 120, 190), (210, 100, 170), (235, 205, 60), (30, 35, 45), (232, 224, 210)
+
+
+def jpeg(draw) -> bytes:
+    im = Image.new("RGB", (640, 480), WOOD)
+    draw(ImageDraw.Draw(im))
+    buf = io.BytesIO()
+    im.save(buf, "JPEG", quality=95)
+    return buf.getvalue()
+
+
+def cube(d, x=280, y=200, s=80, color=BLUE):
+    d.rectangle([x, y, x + s, y + s], fill=color)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("hole, axis", [
+    ((320, 235, 13, 13), "vertical"),       # on top: centred or a little high
+    ((320, 262, 13, 13), "horizontal-y"),   # on the face toward the camera
+    ((345, 240, 6, 13), "horizontal-x"),    # on a side face: off to one side and narrow
+])
+def test_hole_axis(hole, axis):
+    cx, cy, rx, ry = hole
+
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d)
+        d.ellipse([cx - rx, cy - ry, cx + rx, cy + ry], fill=DARK)
+    box = next(b for b in detect(jpeg(draw), CFG, overhead=True) if b.cls == "blue cube")
+    assert box.hole_axis == axis and box.confidence > 0.5
+    assert f"hole {axis}" in box.text()
+    assert box.info()["shape"] == "cube" and box.info()["colour"] == "blue"
+
+
+def test_no_hole_means_it_runs_sideways_out_of_view():
+    box = next(b for b in detect(jpeg(cube), CFG) if b.cls == "blue cube")
+    assert box.hole_axis == "horizontal"
+
+
+def test_only_the_overhead_view_is_trusted():
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d)
+        d.ellipse([307, 222, 333, 248], fill=DARK)
+    box = next(b for b in detect(jpeg(draw), CFG, overhead=False) if b.cls == "blue cube")
+    assert box.confidence < 0.6 and "hole" not in box.text()
+
+
+def test_table_showing_through_counts_as_a_hole():
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d, color=PINK)
+        d.ellipse([306, 222, 334, 250], fill=WOOD)
+    assert next(b for b in detect(jpeg(draw), CFG) if b.cls == "pink cube").hole_axis == "vertical"
+
+
+def test_edge_shadow_is_not_a_hole():
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d)
+        d.rectangle([280, 262, 360, 280], fill=DARK)    # a shadow along the bottom edge
+    assert next(b for b in detect(jpeg(draw), CFG) if "cube" in b.cls).hole_axis == "horizontal"
+
+
+def test_sticks_have_no_hole_and_a_stick_on_a_cube_is_upright():
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d)
+        d.rectangle([312, 80, 328, 199], fill=YELLOW)
+        d.rectangle([60, 400, 200, 418], fill=PINK)
+    boxes = detect(jpeg(draw), CFG)
+    upright = next(b for b in boxes if b.cls == "yellow stick")
+    lying = next(b for b in boxes if b.cls == "pink stick")
+    assert upright.orientation == "upright" and upright.hole_axis is None
+    assert lying.orientation == "lying"
+
+
+def test_top_cube_of_a_stack():
+    def draw(d):
+        cube(d, y=280)
+        cube(d, y=199, color=PINK)
+        d.ellipse([306, 222, 334, 250], fill=DARK)
+    axis, conf = top_cube_hole(detect(jpeg(draw), CFG, overhead=True))
+    assert axis == "vertical" and conf > 0.5
+    assert top_cube_hole([]) == (None, 0.0)

+"""The hybrid pieces: monitor-written prompts, /reset first and after Dream's IK error, gripper sensors over the
+images, tower prompts with <top>, the operator talking in plain words, and plans/hybrid_v4.yaml."""
+
+import json
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from conftest import stage
+from test_v2 import Script, Transport, build, events, mv, prompts, reply_text, routes
+
+from harness.log import RunLog
+from harness.operator import Operator, load_lessons, parse_reply
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.runner import AUTO_RESET_MAX, Runner
+from harness.verifiers import MonitorVerifier, parse_monitor_reply
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
+
+
+def test_hybrid_plan_loads_and_cable_section_is_optional():
+    plan = load_plan(ROOT / "plans/hybrid_v4.yaml")
+    names = [s.name for s in plan.stages]
+    assert plan.reset_first and plan.rewrite_prompts and plan.system_prompt and plan.time_budget_s == 295
+    assert names[0] == "cable_unplug" and "cable_extra_4_down" in names and "stack_2" in names and names[-1].startswith("ball_")
+    assert all(s.prompt.startswith(plan.prompt_prefix) for s in plan.stages if s.name.startswith("cable_"))
+    assert not any(n.startswith("cable_") for n in names[names.index("arms_clear"):])
+    assert all(s.stall_s and s.stall_s >= 30 for s in plan.stages if s.name.startswith(("stack", "tower")))
+    assert "{" not in plan.system_prompt and "coloured golf ball" in plan.stages[-1].prompt
+
+
+def test_next_prompt_parsed_and_asked_for(write_plan):
+    v = parse_monitor_reply(reply_text(next_prompt="  pick up the pink cube\n and place it gently "), 0)
+    assert v.next_prompt == "pick up the pink cube and place it gently"
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a", verifier="monitor")], rewrite_prompts=True, system_prompt="lessons"))
+    t = Transport(reply_text())
+    m = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=t)
+    m.check_now(plan.stages[0], {"video.scene": b"\xff\xd8x\xff\xd9"}, 1.0)
+    assert t.payloads[0]["system"] == "lessons"
+    assert "next_prompt" in t.payloads[0]["messages"][0]["content"][-1]["text"]
+
+
+def test_monitor_rewrites_the_prompt_with_prefix_cooldown_and_cap(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
+    monkeypatch.setattr("harness.runner.REWRITE_COOLDOWN_S", 0.0)
+    pre = "No power here."
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", prompt=f"{pre} pull the plug", timeout_s=3.0, max_attempts=1, on_fail="skip")],
+                      rewrite_prompts=True, prompt_prefix=pre)
+    sv = Script([mv(None, "ok", next_prompt="ignored: status ok"), mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip the rightmost plug"),
+                 mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip the second plug"),
+                 mv(None, "failing", next_prompt="grip the third plug"), mv(None, "stalled", next_prompt="grip a fourth plug"),
+                 mv(True, progress=1.0)], repeat=True)
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv)
+    r.run()
+    sent = prompts(fake)
+    rewrites = events(r.log, "prompt_rewrite")
+    assert len(rewrites) == 3                                   # REWRITE_MAX per attempt
+    assert sent[1] == f"{pre} grip the rightmost plug"          # the prefix is put back on
+    assert all(e["previous"] for e in rewrites)
+
+
+def test_reset_first_then_auto_reset_after_svd(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    fake.reset_s = fake.home_s = 0.05
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=6.0)], reset_first=True)
+    hits = {"n": 0}
+
+    def svd(st, frames, elapsed):
+        hits["n"] += 1
+        if hits["n"] in (2, 6, 10):
+            with fake._lock:
+                fake.last_error = "LinAlgError: SVD did not converge"
+                fake.state = "error"
+        return mv(True, progress=1.0) if hits["n"] > 14 else mv(None)
+
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([svd]))
+    r.run()
+    rs = routes(fake)
+    assert rs[0] == "/reset" and rs[1] == "/start"              # grippers open before the first stage
+    assert len(events(r.log, "auto_reset")) == AUTO_RESET_MAX   # a third SVD restarts without a reset
+    assert rs.count("/reset") == 1 + AUTO_RESET_MAX
+
+
+def test_gripper_note_reads_this_stations_sensors(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    r = build(fake, write_plan([stage("a")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(None)]))
+    r.station.grippers = lambda: {"right": {"gripper": 0.03, "commanded_gripper": None},
+                                  "left": {"gripper": 0.11, "commanded_gripper": 0.0}}
+    note = r._grip_note()
+    assert "right gripper shut on nothing" in note and "left gripper nearly shut (0.11): a thin plug or nothing" in note
+
+
+def test_done_seen_during_recovery_passes_after_a_second_look(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    rec = [{"name": "lift", "kind": "reset", "prompt": "open and lift", "settle_s": 0.05}]
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=0.3, max_attempts=3, recovery=rec)])
+    sv = Script([mv(None, "ok")])
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv)
+    r._reset_scene = lambda st, rc, v: (setattr(r, "_done_in_recovery", "plug out"), (True, "scene ok"))[1]
+    sv.check_now = lambda st, frames, e: mv(True, progress=1.0)
+    res = r.run()
+    assert res.stages[0].ok and res.stages[0].reason == "done during recovery: plug out"
+    assert events(r.log, "recovery_done_check")[0]["passed"]
+
+
+def test_tower_prompt_names_the_piece_below(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+                      tower_prompts={"cube": "put the <piece> on the <top>", "stick": "stand the <piece> on the <top>"})

+    r._tower = ["blue cube", "pink cube"]

+def test_operator_reply_parsing():
+    text = 'Skipping it.\n<json>{"reply": "ok, skipping stack_3", "skip_stages": ["stack_3", "nope"], ' \

+    assert acts == {"skip_stages": ["stack_3"], "note": "lower blocks slowly"}

+def test_operator_skips_ends_and_notes(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    path = write_plan([stage("a", timeout_s=5.0), stage("b"), stage("c", timeout_s=0.5)])
+    log = RunLog(tmp_path / "runs")
+    plan = load_plan(path)
+    lessons = tmp_path / "lessons.md"
+    t = Transport('<json>{"reply": "ok", "end_current_stage": true, "skip_stages": ["b"], '
+                  '"note": "carry blocks high"}</json>')
+    op = Operator(plan, log, transport=t, lessons_path=lessons, print_fn=lambda s: None)
+    log.listeners.append(op.record)
+    notes = []
+
+    class V(Script):
+        operator_notes = notes
+
+    sv = V([lambda st, f, e: (op.submit("skip the next step and stop this one") if st.name == "a" and len(sv.calls) == 2
+                              else None) or mv(None)])
+    r = build(fake, path, tmp_path, sv, log=log)
+    r.operator = op
+    res = r.run()
+    by = {s.name: s for s in res.stages}
+    assert by["a"].reason == "operator ended the stage" and by["b"].reason == "skipped by operator"
+    assert not any(c[0] == "b" for c in sv.calls) and any(c[0] == "c" for c in sv.calls)
+    assert notes == ["carry blocks high"]
+    assert "carry blocks high" in load_lessons(lessons)
+    kinds = [e["event"] for e in events(log)]
+    assert "operator_input" in kinds and "operator_reply" in kinds and "operator_action" in kinds
+    assert "skip the next step" in t.payloads[0]["messages"][0]["content"][-1]["text"]
+
+
+def test_trace_report_shows_rewrites_and_operator(tmp_path):

+           {"t": 2, "event": "operator_input", "text": "why?"},

+def _jpeg(color):
+    import io
+    from PIL import Image
+    out = io.BytesIO()
+    Image.new("RGB", (640, 360), color).save(out, "JPEG")
+    return out.getvalue()

+def test_stitched_history_and_two_calls_in_flight(write_plan):
+    import threading
+    plan = load_plan(write_plan([stage("a", verifier="monitor")], claude={"stitch": True, "parallel": 2,
+                                                                        "history_frames": 4}))
+    gate, started = threading.Event(), []
+
+    def slow(payload):
+        started.append(payload)
+        gate.wait(5)
+        return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": reply_text(progress=len(started) / 10)}]}
+
+    m = MonitorVerifier(plan, transport=slow)
+    st = plan.stages[0]
+    for t in range(4):
+        m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg((t * 40, 0, 0)), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, float(t))
+    assert len(started) == 2                       # at most two in flight
+    gate.set()
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red"), "video.wrist_r": _jpeg("blue")}, 5.0)
+    m.wait_idle(3)
+    last = started[-1]["messages"][0]["content"]
+    images = [c for c in last if c["type"] == "image"]
+    texts = [c["text"] for c in last if c["type"] == "text"]
+    assert len(images) == 3 and texts[0].startswith("Earlier frames in ONE image")   # grid + newest scene + wrist
+    v = m.check(st, {"video.scene": _jpeg("red")}, 6.0)
+    assert v.latency_s is not None
+
+

+    by = {s.name: s for s in res.stages}
+    assert by["down"].reason == "up did not pass" and not any(c[0] == "down" for c in sv.calls)
