+"""The learned joint fault detector: numpy inference matches the torch models it was trained as, the features match
+online and offline, and the shipped model loads and runs on live-shaped readings."""
+
+import json
+
+import numpy as np
+import pytest
+
+from harness.joint_fault_net import DEFAULT_MODEL, N_FEATURES, WINDOW, FaultModel, JointFaultNet, features
+from harness.joint_observer import YAM_LIMITS_DEG
+
+
+def _spec_from_torch(family, cfg):
+    torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
+    from tools.train_joint_fault import build_model, export
+    torch.manual_seed(0)
+    model = build_model(family, cfg).eval()
+    norm = (np.zeros(N_FEATURES, np.float32), np.ones(N_FEATURES, np.float32))
+    return model, export(model, family, cfg, norm, 0.5, {})
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("family,cfg", [
+    ("mlp", {"hidden": [16, 8]}),
+    ("tcn", {"channels": 8, "dilations": [1, 2, 4], "kernel": 3}),
+    ("gru", {"hidden": 8, "layers": 2}),
+    ("transformer", {"d_model": 16, "heads": 2, "layers": 2, "ff": 32}),
+])
+def test_numpy_forward_matches_torch(family, cfg):
+    torch = pytest.importorskip("torch")
+    model, spec = _spec_from_torch(family, cfg)
+    x = np.random.default_rng(1).normal(size=(5, WINDOW, N_FEATURES)).astype(np.float32)
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        want = model(torch.from_numpy(x)).double().numpy()
+    got = FaultModel(json.loads(json.dumps(spec))).logits(x)
+    assert np.allclose(got, want, atol=1e-5), (got, want)
+
+
+def test_online_features_match_offline():
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(2)
+    T = 30
+    q = np.cumsum(rng.normal(0, 2, T))
+    qc, dq, tau, tauc = q + 1, rng.normal(0, 20, T), rng.normal(0, 1, T), rng.normal(0, 1, T)
+    off = features(q, qc, dq, tau, tauc, -90, 90, 10)
+    on = [features([q[i]], [qc[i]], [dq[i]], [tau[i]], [tauc[i]], -90, 90, 10,
+                   q_cmd_prev=None if i == 0 else qc[i - 1])[0] for i in range(T)]
+    assert np.allclose(off, np.stack(on), atol=1e-6)
+
+
+@pytest.mark.skipif(not DEFAULT_MODEL.exists(), reason="no trained model shipped")
+def test_shipped_model_runs_and_flags_a_limit_run():
+    model = FaultModel.load()
+    net = JointFaultNet(model, YAM_LIMITS_DEG)
+    quiet, loud = [], []
+    for i in range(40):                                  # 2 s holding still mid-range
+        quiet += net.step(i * 0.05, "left", {"wrist_yaw": {"q_deg": 0.0, "q_cmd_deg": 0.0, "dq_deg_s": 0.0,
+                                                          "tau_nm": 0.1, "tau_cmd_nm": 0.1, "tau_max_nm": 10.0}})
+    q = 0.0
+    for i in range(40, 70):                              # then 200 deg/s straight at the +90 deg limit
+        q += 10.0
+        loud += net.step(i * 0.05, "left", {"wrist_yaw": {"q_deg": q, "q_cmd_deg": q + 10, "dq_deg_s": 200.0,
+                                                         "tau_nm": 0.5, "tau_cmd_nm": 0.5, "tau_max_nm": 10.0}})
+    assert not quiet
+    assert any(a["kind"] == "learned_fault_soon" for a in loud)

+"""Live joint telemetry: can_live decodes Dream's CAN frames; joint_telemetry turns torques into signals."""
+import json
+import math
+import time
+import urllib.request
+
+import pytest
+
+from harness.can_live import ArmState, decode_mit
+from harness.can_torque import LIMITS
+from harness.joint_telemetry import JointTelemetry, Thresholds
+
+
+def _f2u(x, lo, hi, bits):
+    x = min(max(x, lo), hi)
+    return int((x - lo) * ((1 << bits) - 1) / (hi - lo))
+
+
+def encode_mit(model, kp, kd, pos_deg, vel_deg_s, tau):
+    """Same packing as robots.damiao.encode_mit (dreamlabs 1.0.37), used when that package is not installed."""
+    pmax, vmax, tmax = LIMITS[model]
+    q, dq = _f2u(math.radians(pos_deg), -pmax, pmax, 16), _f2u(math.radians(vel_deg_s), -vmax, vmax, 12)
+    kp_u, kd_u, t = _f2u(kp, 0, 500, 12), _f2u(kd, 0, 5, 12), _f2u(tau, -tmax, tmax, 12)
+    return bytes([q >> 8, q & 0xFF, dq >> 4, ((dq & 0xF) << 4) | (kp_u >> 8), kp_u & 0xFF, kd_u >> 4,
+                  ((kd_u & 0xF) << 4) | (t >> 8), t & 0xFF])
+
+
+def encode_reply(motor_id, model, pos_deg, vel_deg_s, tau, status=1):
+    pmax, vmax, tmax = LIMITS[model]
+    q, dq = _f2u(math.radians(pos_deg), -pmax, pmax, 16), _f2u(math.radians(vel_deg_s), -vmax, vmax, 12)
+    t = _f2u(tau, -tmax, tmax, 12)
+    return bytes([(status << 4) | motor_id, q >> 8, q & 0xFF, dq >> 4, ((dq & 0xF) << 4) | (t >> 8), t & 0xFF, 31, 35])
+
+
+def test_decode_mit_inverts_dreams_encoder():
+    try:
+        from robots.damiao import encode_mit as dream_encode       # the real one, where installed
+    except ImportError:
+        dream_encode = encode_mit
+    d = decode_mit(dream_encode("metal_j2", 40.0, 1.5, 30.0, -12.0, 4.5), "metal_j2")
+    assert d["kp"] == pytest.approx(40.0, abs=0.2) and d["kd"] == pytest.approx(1.5, abs=0.01)
+    assert d["q_cmd_deg"] == pytest.approx(30.0, abs=0.02) and d["dq_cmd_deg_s"] == pytest.approx(-12.0, abs=0.5)
+    assert d["tau_ff_nm"] == pytest.approx(4.5, abs=0.1)
+
+
+def test_arm_state_pairs_command_and_reply_into_commanded_torque():
+    arm = ArmState("right")
+    arm.feed(2, encode_mit("metal_j2", 40.0, 1.0, 30.0, 0.0, 2.0), 1.0)           # shoulder_lift command
+    arm.feed(0x12, encode_reply(2, "metal_j2", 25.0, 0.0, 6.0), 1.001)            # its reply
+    j = arm.snapshot()["shoulder_lift"]
+    expected = (j["kp"] * math.radians(j["q_cmd_deg"] - j["q_deg"]) + j["kd"] * math.radians(j["dq_cmd_deg_s"] - j["dq_deg_s"])
+                + j["tau_ff_nm"])                       # gains as decoded: 12 bits quantize kp to ~0.12 N.m/rad
+    assert j["tau_cmd_nm"] == pytest.approx(expected, abs=2e-3) and j["tau_nm"] == pytest.approx(6.0, abs=0.1)
+    assert j["temp_rotor_c"] == 35 and j["tau_max_nm"] == 120.0
+    arm.feed(2, b"\xff" * 7 + b"\xfc", 1.002)                                      # enable frame: ignored
+    assert arm.snapshot()["shoulder_lift"]["kp"] == pytest.approx(40.0, abs=0.2)
+
+
+def _snap(grip_tau=0.0, lift_tau=0.0, lag=0.0, speed=10.0, tau_cmd=None):
+    return {"arms": {"right": {
+        "gripper": {"q_deg": 0.02, "q_cmd_deg": 0.0, "tau_nm": grip_tau, "tau_max_nm": 20.0},
+        "shoulder_lift": {"q_deg": -30.0, "q_cmd_deg": -30.0 + lag, "dq_deg_s": speed, "tau_nm": lift_tau,
+                          "tau_cmd_nm": lift_tau if tau_cmd is None else tau_cmd, "tau_max_nm": 120.0}}}}
+
+
+def test_signals_need_consecutive_readings_and_are_worded_for_the_monitor():
+    feed = iter([_snap(grip_tau=8.0)] * 2 + [_snap(grip_tau=1.0)] + [_snap(grip_tau=8.0)] * 3)
+    events = []
+    jt = JointTelemetry("http://x", fetch=lambda: next(feed), log=lambda *a, **k: events.append(k),
+                        thresholds=Thresholds(persist=3))
+    for _ in range(3):
+        jt.poll_once()
+    assert not jt.holding()["right"]                       # a dropout resets the streak
+    for _ in range(3):
+        jt.poll_once()
+    assert jt.holding()["right"] and "squeezing an object" in jt.note()
+    assert events and events[0]["kind"] == "holding" and jt.alerts()
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("snap, kind", [
+    (_snap(lift_tau=90.0), "strain"),
+    (_snap(lag=15.0, speed=0.5), "blocked"),
+    (_snap(lift_tau=50.0, tau_cmd=5.0), "contact"),
+])
+def test_strain_blocked_and_contact(snap, kind):
+    jt = JointTelemetry("http://x", fetch=lambda: snap, thresholds=Thresholds(persist=1))
+    jt.poll_once()
+    assert kind in {k for k, *_ in jt._active}
+
+
+def test_feed_down_gives_no_note():
+    def boom():
+        raise OSError("no route")
+    jt = JointTelemetry("http://x", fetch=boom)
+    jt.poll_once()
+    assert jt.note() == "" and not jt.ok
+
+
+def test_sim_serves_joints_in_can_live_shape():
+    sim_station = pytest.importorskip("sim_station")
+    try:
+        sim = sim_station.SimStation(policy="scripted", scene="hill_climb", realtime=True, api_port=0,
+                                     view_port=0).start()
+    except SystemExit:
+        pytest.skip("Dream's yam_mujoco example is not installed here")
+    try:
+        time.sleep(0.5)
+        with urllib.request.urlopen(sim.api_url + "/joints", timeout=10) as r:
+            snap = json.loads(r.read())
+        for arm in ("left", "right"):
+            joints = snap["arms"][arm]
+            assert set(joints) == {"shoulder_pan", "shoulder_lift", "elbow_flex", "wrist_flex", "wrist_yaw",
+                                   "wrist_roll", "gripper"}
+            assert all({"q_deg", "q_cmd_deg", "tau_nm", "tau_cmd_nm", "tau_max_nm"} <= set(j) for j in joints.values())
+        jt = JointTelemetry(sim.api_url, thresholds=Thresholds(persist=1))
+        jt.poll_once()
+        assert jt.ok
+    finally:
+        sim.close()
+

+def test_observer_warns_before_a_joint_limit_and_flags_divergence_and_faults():
+    from harness.joint_observer import JointObserver
+    obs = JointObserver()
+    alerts = []
+    for i in range(20):                       # shoulder_lift rising toward its 0 deg limit at 60 deg/s
+        t = i / 30
+        q = -40 + 60 * t
+        alerts = obs.observe(t, "right", {"shoulder_lift": {"q_deg": q, "dq_deg_s": 60.0}})
+        if alerts:
+            break
+    a = alerts[0]
+    assert a["kind"] == "limit_approach" and a["bound"] == 0.0 and q < 0 and a["eta_s"] > 0   # before the limit
+    obs2 = JointObserver()
+    for i in range(15):
+        out = obs2.observe(i / 30, "left", {"elbow_flex": {"q_deg": 40.0, "q_cmd_deg": 60.0, "dq_deg_s": 0.0,
+                                                          "status": 1}})
+    assert any(x["kind"] == "tracking_divergence" for x in out)
+    out = obs2.observe(1.0, "left", {"wrist_yaw": {"q_deg": 0.0, "status": 0xA, "temp_rotor_c": 56}})
+    assert {"fault", "overheat"} <= {x["kind"] for x in out}
+
+
+def test_telemetry_words_observer_alerts_for_the_monitor():
+    snap = {"t": 1.0, "arms": {"right": {}}, "observer_alerts": [
+        {"kind": "limit_approach", "arm": "right", "joint": "wrist_flex", "eta_s": 0.2}]}
+    jt = JointTelemetry("http://x", fetch=lambda: snap, thresholds=Thresholds(persist=1))
+    jt.poll_once()
+    assert "wrist_flex will reach its joint limit in 0.2 s" in jt.note()

+"""LiveKit observer: the parts that run without the livekit package (freshness gate, argument checks)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import pytest
+
+from harness import livekit_observer as lko
+
+
+class _Room:
+    def __init__(self, connected: bool = True) -> None:
+        self.connected = connected
+
+    def isconnected(self) -> bool:
+        return self.connected
+
+
+def _cameras(latest: dict, roles=("overhead", "wrist_l"), connected=True, reconnecting=False):
+    cams = object.__new__(lko.LiveKitCameras)      # skip __init__: it needs the livekit SDK
+    cams.room = _Room(connected)
+    cams._roles = {f"track_{r}": r for r in roles}
+    cams._latest = latest
+    cams._reconnecting = reconnecting
+    return cams
+
+
+def _frame(t: float) -> lko.ReceivedFrame:
+    return lko.ReceivedFrame(event=None, received_at=t)
+
+
+def test_sample_needs_every_view_fresh_and_new():
+    a, b = _frame(9.5), _frame(9.8)
+    cams = _cameras({"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b})
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {"overhead": a, "wrist_l": b}
+    assert cams.sample(10.0, 1.0, {"overhead": a}) == {}          # one view unchanged since last sample
+    assert cams.sample(11.0, 1.0, {}) == {}                       # overhead is 1.5 s old
+    assert _cameras({"overhead": a}).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}  # wrist missing
+
+
+def test_sample_is_empty_while_disconnected_or_reconnecting():
+    latest = {"overhead": _frame(9.9), "wrist_l": _frame(9.9)}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), connected=False).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+    assert _cameras(dict(latest), reconnecting=True).sample(10.0, 1.0, {}) == {}
+
+
+def test_camera_names_match_station_aliases():
+    assert lko.camera_matches("video.top", "overhead")
+    assert lko.camera_matches("left", "wrist_l")
+    assert not lko.camera_matches("right", "wrist_l")
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("argv, msg", [
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "ceiling=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--track", "wrist_l=x"], "unique overhead"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x", "--plan", "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml"], "used together"),
+    (["--publisher", "p", "--track", "overhead=x"], "LIVEKIT_URL"),
+])
+def test_bad_arguments_are_refused(argv, msg, monkeypatch, capsys):
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_URL", raising=False)
+    monkeypatch.delenv("LIVEKIT_TOKEN", raising=False)
+    with pytest.raises(SystemExit) as exc:
+        lko.main(argv)
+    assert exc.value.code == 2
+    assert msg in capsys.readouterr().err
+
+
+def test_seconds_must_be_positive():
+    import argparse
+    with pytest.raises(argparse.ArgumentTypeError):
+        lko.positive_seconds("0")
+    assert lko.positive_seconds("2.5") == 2.5

+"""Darsh's operator decisions (plans/operator_notes.yaml) reach the plan, the monitor and the Dream prompt."""
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from harness.operator_notes import apply_raw, attempts_for, entry_for, load
+from harness.plan import load_plan
+from harness.promptgen import Context, compose
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+NOTES = load(ROOT / "plans/operator_notes.yaml")
+
+
+def test_retry_rule_two_tries_three_for_high_points_pass_bars_untouched():
+    plan = load_plan(ROOT / "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml")

+def test_hedged_policy_returns_the_first_reply_and_feeds_every_session():
+    import time as _t
+
+    from sim_station import HedgedPolicy
+
+    class Fake:
+        def __init__(self, delay, fail=False):
+            self.delay, self.fail, self.obs, self.closed = delay, fail, 0, False
+
+        def set_observation(self, *a, **k):
+            self.obs += 1
+
+        def get_action(self):
+            _t.sleep(self.delay)
+            if self.fail:
+                raise RuntimeError("boom")
+            return {"from": self.delay}
+
+        def close(self):
+            self.closed = True
+
+    sessions = [Fake(0.3), Fake(0.02, fail=True), Fake(0.05)]
+    hp = HedgedPolicy(sessions)
+    hp.set_observation("frames", "state", timestamp=0.0)
+    t = _t.monotonic()
+    assert hp.get_action() == {"from": 0.05}            # the failing fast one is skipped, the next fastest wins
+    assert _t.monotonic() - t < 0.25 and hp.last_winner == 2
+    assert all(s.obs == 1 for s in sessions)
+    hp.close()
+    assert all(s.closed for s in sessions)
+
+
+def test_stop_while_the_first_request_is_in_flight_returns_to_idle(sim, monkeypatch):
+    settle(sim)
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sim, "scripted_latency_s", 3.0)        # the first reply is slow
+    assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "go"})[0] == 200
+    assert sim.state == "starting"
+    code, status = call(sim, "POST", "/stop", {})
+    assert code == 200 and status["state"] == "idle"
+    monkeypatch.undo()
+    assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "again"})[0] == 200   # nothing left stuck
+    assert wait_until(lambda: sim.state == "running", 60)
+    settle(sim)
+
+
+def test_a_failure_mid_run_goes_to_error_and_a_new_start_recovers(sim, monkeypatch):
+    settle(sim)
+    real_open = sim._open
+    calls = {"n": 0}
+
+    def flaky_open(model, poses):
+        policy = real_open(model, poses)
+        real_get = policy.get_action
+
+        def get_action(commanded=None):
+            calls["n"] += 1
+            if calls["n"] > 3:
+                raise RuntimeError("model fell over")
+            return real_get()
+        policy.get_action = get_action
+        return policy
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sim, "_open", flaky_open)
+    assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "go"})[0] == 200
+    assert wait_until(lambda: sim.state == "error", 60)
+    assert call(sim, "GET", "/status")[1]["last_error"]["message"] == "model fell over"
+    monkeypatch.undo()
+    assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "go"})[0] == 200
+    assert wait_until(lambda: sim.state == "running", 60)
+    settle(sim)
+
+
+def test_a_retryable_refusal_waits_in_starting_then_runs(sim, monkeypatch):
+    settle(sim)
+    real_open = sim._open
+    refusals = {"n": 0}
+
+    class Warming(Exception):
+        retryable, reason, retry_after_s = True, "pool_warming", 0.2
+
+    def warming_open(model, poses):
+        policy = real_open(model, poses)
+        real_get = policy.get_action
+
+        def get_action(commanded=None):
+            if refusals["n"] < 2:
+                refusals["n"] += 1
+                raise Warming("pool warming")
+            return real_get()
+        policy.get_action = get_action
+        return policy
+
+    monkeypatch.setattr(sim, "_open", warming_open)
+    assert call(sim, "POST", "/start", {"prompt": "go"})[0] == 200
+    assert wait_until(lambda: sim.state == "running", 60)
+    assert refusals["n"] == 2
+    monkeypatch.undo()
+    settle(sim)

+"""plan.start_pose (home before a stage when the arms start far from every recorded start) and the live-frame guard."""
+
+import json
+import threading
+
+import pytest
+
+from conftest import stage, wait_until
+from test_v2 import Script, build, events, mv, routes
+from harness.live_guard import LiveFrames, LiveGuard
+from harness.plan import parse_plan
+from harness.start_pose import StartPoses, joints_from_arms, joints_from_observation
+
+HOME = [0.0] * 6
+RAISED = [0.3, -1.7, 0.75, 0.1, 0.05, -0.1]
+
+
+def _ref(**by_prompt):
+    return StartPoses({p: [{"left": l, "right": r, "episode": f"ep{i}"} for i, (l, r) in enumerate(starts)]
+                       for p, starts in by_prompt.items()})
+
+
+# -- start pose: the check --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def test_check_statuses():
+    ref = _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    near = {"left": HOME, "right": [0.1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]}
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", near, 0.35).status == "ok"
+    far = ref.check("do unplug", {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}, 0.35)
+    assert far.status == "far" and far.distance["right"] == pytest.approx(1.7) and far.nearest == "ep0"
+    assert ref.check("other prompt", near, 0.35).status == "no_reference"
+    assert ref.check("do unplug", None, 0.35).status == "no_state"
+
+
+def test_nearest_start_wins():
+    ref = _ref(p=[(HOME, HOME), (HOME, RAISED)])
+    chk = ref.check("p", {"left": HOME, "right": [0.25, -1.6, 0.7, 0.1, 0.0, -0.1]}, 0.35)
+    assert chk.status == "ok" and chk.nearest == "ep1"
+
+
+def test_joint_sources():
+    assert joints_from_arms({"left": {"gripper": 1.0}, "right": {"gripper": 1.0}}) is None   # old add-on
+    assert joints_from_arms({"right": {"positions_rad": RAISED}}) == {"right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"state.left_arm": HOME, "state.right_arm": RAISED, "prompt": "x"}) == \
+        {"left": HOME, "right": RAISED}
+    assert joints_from_observation({"prompt": "x"}) is None
+
+
+# -- start pose: the plan ---------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _plan(**sp):
+    return parse_plan({"task": "t", "start_pose": {"reference": "r.json", **sp},
+                       "stages": [{"name": "unplug_cable", "prompt": "u", "success": "u?"}]})
+
+
+def test_start_pose_parses():
+    sp = _plan(home_before=["unplug"], tolerance_rad=0.2).start_pose
+    assert sp.homes("unplug_cable") and sp.tolerance_rad == 0.2 and sp.max_homes == 1 and sp.home_pose["left"] == HOME
+
+
+@pytest.mark.parametrize("sp, match", [
+    ({"home_before": ["stack"]}, "matches no stage"),
+    ({"home_pose": {"left": HOME}}, "home_pose"),
+    ({"tolerance_rad": 0}, "tolerance_rad"),
+    ({"homes": 2}, "unknown key"),
+])
+def test_bad_start_pose_rejected(sp, match):
+    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=match):
+        _plan(**sp)

+# -- start pose: the runner -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+def _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, *, right, home_before=("unplug_cable",), refs=None, verdicts=None,
+                 max_attempts=2):
+    path = write_plan([stage("unplug_cable", prompt="do unplug", max_attempts=max_attempts, timeout_s=0.6)],
+                      start_pose={"reference": "unused.json", "home_before": list(home_before)}, time_budget_s=300)
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script(verdicts or [mv(True)]))
+    runner.start_poses = refs or _ref(**{"do unplug": [(HOME, HOME)]})
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": right}}
+    return runner
+
+
+def test_far_start_homes_before_the_first_prompt(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=RAISED)
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.index("/home") < r.index("/start")
+    ev = events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]
+    assert ev["status"] == "far" and ev["source"] == "arms" and ev["why"] == "stage start"
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose_home")[0]["done"] is True
+
+
+def test_near_start_does_not_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)
+    assert events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "ok"

+    assert runner.run().ok
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake) and events(runner.log, "start_pose")[0]["status"] == "far"

+def test_max_homes_per_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+def test_retry_after_far_start_homes_then_starts_fresh(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    arms = {"right": HOME, "failed": False}
+    verdict = Script([lambda st, f, e: mv(True) if arms["failed"] else mv(False, "failing")])
+    runner = _pose_runner(fake, write_plan, tmp_path, right=HOME, verdicts=None)
+    runner.verifiers = {k: verdict for k in runner.verifiers}
+    runner.station.grippers = lambda: {"left": {"positions_rad": HOME}, "right": {"positions_rad": arms["right"]}}
+    orig = runner._attempt
+
+    def attempt(*a, **k):
+        out = orig(*a, **k)
+        arms["right"], arms["failed"] = RAISED, True   # the failed attempt left the arm up over the hub
+        return out
+    runner._attempt = attempt
+    assert runner.run().ok
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r.count("/home") == 1 and r.count("/start") == 2 and r.index("/home") < len(r) - 1
+    assert [e["mode"] for e in events(runner.log, "retry")] == ["home_then_retry"]

+    runner.start_poses = None
+    assert runner.run().ok and not events(runner.log, "start_pose")

+def test_reference_tool(tmp_path):
+    pa = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow")
+    pq = pytest.importorskip("pyarrow.parquet")
+    from tools.start_poses import main as build_ref
+    ds = tmp_path / "set"
+    (ds / "meta").mkdir(parents=True)
+    (ds / "data" / "chunk-000").mkdir(parents=True)
+    names = [f"left_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + ["left_gripper"] + [f"right_joint_{j}" for j in range(6)] + \
+        ["right_gripper"]
+    (ds / "meta" / "info.json").write_text(json.dumps({"features": {"observation.state": {"names": names}}}))
+    (ds / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").write_text('{"task_index": 0, "task": "a"}\n{"task_index": 1, "task": "b"}\n')
+    rows = [[0.0] * 7 + [0.1] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.2] * 7, [0.0] * 7 + [0.9] * 7]
+    pq.write_table(pa.table({"observation.state": rows, "task_index": [0, 0, 1]}),
+                   ds / "data" / "chunk-000" / "episode_000000.parquet")
+    out = tmp_path / "ref.json"
+    assert build_ref([str(out), str(ds)]) == 0
+    ref = StartPoses.load(out)
+    assert ref.by_prompt["a"][0]["right"] == [0.1] * 6 and ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["right"] == [0.9] * 6
+    assert ref.by_prompt["b"][0]["episode"] == "set/episode_000000/seg1"

+# -- live guard -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------
+class _Feed:
+    """Distinct frames each grab unless frozen."""
+
+    def __init__(self):
+        self.n, self.frozen = 0, False
+
+    def __call__(self):
+        if not self.frozen:
+            self.n += 1
+        return {"wrist_r": b"jpeg%d" % self.n}
+
+
+def _guard(answers, confirm=3):
+    stops, log = [], []
+    seq = list(answers)
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: seq.pop(0) if seq else (False, "no"), lambda: stops.append(1),
+                  confirm_frames=confirm, log=lambda name, **k: log.append((name, k)))
+    return g, stops, log
+
+
+def test_live_guard_needs_confirm_distinct_yes_in_a_row():
+    g, stops, log = _guard([(True, "y"), (False, "n"), (True, "y"), (None, "?"), (True, "y"), (True, "y")])
+    g.step()                                   # disarmed: nothing is looked at
+    assert g.ticks == 0
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped == "y" and stops == [1]
+    assert [k["count"] for n, k in log if n == "guard_yes"] == [1, 1, 2, 3]
+    g.step()
+    assert stops == [1]                        # stops once
+
+
+def test_live_guard_frozen_frames_count_once():
+    g, stops, _ = _guard([(True, "y")] * 5)
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.source.frozen = True
+    for _ in range(5):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops
+
+
+def test_live_guard_answer_time_and_disarm():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 100.0
+    t[0] = 105.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+    g.disarm()
+    t[0] = 110.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 105.0
+
+
+def test_live_frames_probe_finds_no_route(fake):
+    lf = LiveFrames(fake.api_url, ("wrist_l", "wrist_r"))
+    assert lf.probe() == [] and "404" in lf.last_error
+    assert lf.roles == ["wrist_l", "wrist_r"]          # nothing dropped: a camera that comes back is used again

+def test_yes_counts_per_camera_not_across_cameras():
+    n = {"l": 0}
+
+    def feed():
+        n["l"] += 1
+        return {"wrist_l": b"moving%d" % n["l"], "wrist_r": b"frozen-yes"}
+    stops = []
+    g = LiveGuard(feed, lambda f: (True, "y") if "wrist_r" in f else (False, "n"), lambda: stops.append(1))
+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    for _ in range(6):
+        g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops            # one frozen yes frame is one look

+def test_frozen_frames_do_not_refresh_the_blind_clock():
+    t = [100.0]
+    g = LiveGuard(lambda: {"wrist_r": b"same"}, lambda f: (False, "n"), lambda: None, clock=lambda: t[0])
+    g.arm("ball_1")

+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0
+    t[0] = 130.0
+    g.step()
+    assert g.last_answer_t == 101.0                   # max_blind_s will see the camera as blind

+def test_a_judgment_from_before_a_rearm_is_dropped():

+    g.arm("ball_1")
+    g.step()
+    assert g.tripped is None and not stops

+def test_no_motion_command_after_a_trip(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    with pytest.raises(_Guard):
+        runner._issue("do ball_1", "stage")
+    assert routes(fake) == before                     # nothing was sent

+def test_operator_stop_during_the_stop_wait_means_no_home(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    runner._issue("do unplug", "stage", force_start=True)          # running, so the gate must /stop first

+    runner.station.stop = lambda: (runner.request_stop(), orig_stop())[1]
+    with pytest.raises(_Stop):
+        runner._start_pose_gate(stage0, "do unplug", "retry")
+    assert "/home" not in routes(fake)

+def test_a_trip_ends_a_wait_for_running_at_once(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    import time as _t
+    from harness.runner import _Guard
+    runner = build(fake, write_plan([stage("ball_1")]), tmp_path, Script([mv(True)]))
+    runner.live_guard = type("G", (), {"tripped": "white ball", "last_answer_t": None})()
+    t0 = _t.monotonic()
+    with pytest.raises(_Guard, match="while waiting for running"):
+        runner._wait({"running"}, 30.0, "running")       # the fake is idle: without the trip this waits 30 s
+    assert _t.monotonic() - t0 < 2.0

+def test_live_guard_trip_ends_the_match_mid_stage(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):
+    q = "Is a gripper holding a black ball?"
+    path = write_plan([stage("stack", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip"), stage("ball_1", timeout_s=5.0, on_fail="skip"),
+                       stage("ball_2", timeout_s=0.3, on_fail="skip")],
+                      verifier="monitor", guard={"question": q, "stages": ["ball_"]})
+    runner = build(fake, path, tmp_path, Script([mv(None, guard=False)]))
+    armed = threading.Event()
+    g = LiveGuard(_Feed(), lambda f: (True, "white ball between the fingers"), runner.station.stop, rate_hz=50,
+                  confirm_frames=3, log=runner.log.event)
+    orig_arm = g.arm
+    g.arm = lambda s: (orig_arm(s), armed.set())
+    runner.live_guard = g
+    g.start()
+    try:
+        result = runner.run()
+    finally:
+        g.close()
+    assert armed.is_set()
+    assert not result.ok and result.guard_tripped and "live frames" in result.reason
+    assert [r.name for r in result.stages] == ["stack"]
+    trip = [e for e in events(runner.log, "guard_tripped") if e.get("source") == "live"]
+    assert trip and trip[0]["stage"] == "ball_1" and trip[0]["stop"] == "sent"
+    assert routes(fake).count("/stop") >= 1
+    assert not wait_until(lambda: g.ticks > 10_000, timeout=0.05)   # thread is idle after the trip
+
+

+def test_trip_during_a_motion_command_stops_again(fake, write_plan, tmp_path):

+    runner.live_guard = g

+        runner._motion(slow_start, "do ball_1", None)
+    r = routes(fake)
+    assert r[-2:] == ["/start", "/stop"]               # the start that overtook the trip is stopped

+    assert all(g == {"wrist_r": b"jpeg"} for g in got)  # the live camera every tick
+    assert took < 0.5                                  # the dead one (1 s per try) never held a tick up

+"""The matrix state-space model must make the runner's decisions (decide.py is the reference for the done rule)."""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import random
+import time
+
+import numpy as np
+
+from harness import decide
+from harness.hedge import first_of
+from harness.statespace import Observer, StateSpace, Weights
+
+
+def _looks(rng: random.Random, n: int = 40):
+    t, out = 0.0, []
+    for _ in range(n):
+        t += rng.choice([1.0, 2.5, 3.0, 5.0, 9.0])
+        r = rng.random()
+        done = True if r < 0.35 else (False if r < 0.55 else None)
+        failing = done is not True and rng.random() < 0.3
+        same = done is True and rng.random() < 0.15
+        out.append(decide.Look(t, done, failing=failing, same_frames=same, monitor=True))
+    return out
+
+
+def _ss_pass_time(looks, w: Weights) -> float | None:
+    ss = StateSpace(w)
+    ss.start(timeout_s=1e9, stall_s=None)
+    prev = 0.0
+    for lk in looks:
+        u = ss.u(dt=lk.t - prev, v_new=1, v_done=lk.done is True, v_same=lk.same_frames,
+                 v_failing=(lk.done is False) or lk.failing)
+        prev = lk.t
+        if ss.step(u)["pass"][0]:
+            return lk.t
+    return None
+
+
+def test_matches_reference_done_rule_on_random_runs():
+    rng = random.Random(7)
+    for _ in range(3000):
+        looks = _looks(rng)
+        ref = decide.run_looks(looks)
+        got = _ss_pass_time(looks, Weights(min_gap_s=0.0))
+        assert got == ref, (looks, ref, got)
+
+
+def test_min_gap_merges_close_dones():
+    w = Weights(min_gap_s=3.0)
+    looks = [decide.Look(10.0, True), decide.Look(11.0, True), decide.Look(14.5, True)]
+    assert _ss_pass_time(looks, w) == 14.5          # 11.0 is too close to 10.0 to count
+    assert _ss_pass_time(looks, Weights(min_gap_s=0.0)) == 11.0
+
+
+def test_vectorised_over_weight_sets():
+    ws = [Weights(confirm=c, min_gap_s=0.0, window_s=0.0) for c in (1, 2, 3)]
+    ss = StateSpace(ws)
+    ss.start(1e9, None)
+    passes = []
+    for k in range(3):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=5, v_new=1, v_done=1))
+        passes.append(d["pass"].copy())
+    assert passes[0].tolist() == [True, False, False]
+    assert passes[1].tolist() == [True, True, False]
+    assert passes[2].tolist() == [True, True, True]
+
+
+def test_guards_and_fail_rule():
+    ss = StateSpace(Weights())
+    ss.start(timeout_s=40, stall_s=None)
+    for _ in range(2):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=3, l_guard_yes=1))
+    assert not d["guard"][0]
+    d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=0.1, l_guard_yes=1))
+    assert d["guard"][0]                             # 3 live frames in a row
+    ss.start(timeout_s=40, stall_s=None)
+    for _ in range(2):
+        d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=5, v_new=1, v_failing=1))
+    assert not d["fail"][0]                          # only 10 s of a 40 s stage: too early
+    d = ss.step(ss.u(dt=15, v_new=1, v_failing=1))
+    assert d["fail"][0]
+
+
+def test_observer_blends_fast_and_slow():
+    ob = Observer(Weights(l_fast=0.25, l_slow=0.8))
+    ob.reset()
+    for _ in range(4):
+        ob.fast({"at_goal": 1.0})
+    fast_only = ob.z[2]
+    ob.slow({"at_goal": 0.0})                        # Claude says no: it wins most of the way
+    assert fast_only > 0.6 and ob.z[2] < 0.3
+
+
+def test_tick_is_fast():
+    ss = StateSpace([Weights() for _ in range(1000)])
+    ss.start(60, 20)
+    u = ss.u(dt=0.05, v_new=1, v_done=1)
+    t = time.perf_counter()
+    for _ in range(200):
+        ss.step(u)
+    per_tick_ms = (time.perf_counter() - t) / 200 * 1000
+    assert per_tick_ms < 5.0                         # 1,000 weight sets per tick, well inside a 50 ms (20 Hz) budget
+
+
+def test_first_of_takes_the_fastest_good_answer():
+    delays = iter([0.3, 0.05, 0.2])
+
+    def call():
+        d = next(delays)
+        time.sleep(d)
+        return d
+    res = first_of(call, 3)
+    assert res.value == 0.05 and res.latency_s < 0.2
+    bad = first_of(lambda: (_ for _ in ()).throw(RuntimeError("x")), 2, timeout_s=1)
+    assert res.winner is not None and bad.winner is None and bad.errors
+    assert np.isfinite(res.latency_s)

+"""Benchmark the real stage monitor. --dry-run validates requests without keys or network.
+
+Examples:
+    python tools/bench_judges.py --dry-run --no-sim
+    python tools/bench_judges.py --judge openrouter:google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite -n 3
+    python tools/bench_judges.py --judge openrouter:anthropic/claude-haiku-5.5,google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite --hedge 1 --hedge 2
+
+Live runs cost money. The spend threshold stops NEW trials after accounting for all
+copies of the current trial. One trial can overshoot it. Set a key credit limit too.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import base64
+import copy
+import csv
+import hashlib
+import json
+import math
+import os
+import sys
+import threading
+import time
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Any
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+if str(ROOT) not in sys.path:
+    sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+
+from harness.plan import Plan, load_plan  # noqa: E402
+from harness.verifiers import (  # noqa: E402
+    MonitorVerifier, anthropic_transport, claude_cli_transport, frames_digest,
+    openrouter_request_body, openrouter_transport, valid_jpeg,
+)
+from tools.check_monitor import CASES, PHOTO, SIM_CASES, sim_frames  # noqa: E402
+
+# Public /api/v1/models, checked 2026-11-08. All accept images and return text.
+# Prices and availability change; this tool never fetches the catalog implicitly.
+DEFAULT_MODELS = (
+    "anthropic/claude-haiku-5.5",
+    "google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite",
+    "qwen/qwen3.8-flash",
+    "z-ai/glm-5.3-flash",
+    "meta-llama/llama-4-scout",
+    "openai/gpt-5-nano",
+    "mistralai/mistral-small-2603",
+    "google/gemma-4-26b-a4b-it",
+)
+BASELINE = DEFAULT_MODELS[0]
+SUMMARY_FIELDS = (
+    "judge", "trials", "guard_trials", "stage_done_accuracy", "accepted_done_accuracy", "guard_accuracy",
+    "parse_failure_rate", "transport_failure_rate", "dry_run_valid_rate", "latency_p50_s", "latency_p90_s",
+    "latency_max_s", "attempts_finished", "cost_complete", "known_cost_usd", "cost_usd", "cost_per_call_usd",
+    "cost_per_trial_usd",
+)
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Judge:
+    backend: str
+    models: tuple[str, ...]
+    hedge: int = 1
+
+    @property
+    def name(self) -> str:
+        return f"{self.backend}:{','.join(self.models)};hedge={self.hedge}"
+
+    @property
+    def attempts(self) -> int:
+        return self.hedge * len(self.models)
+
+
+def judge_spec(value: str) -> tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]]:
+    backend, sep, names = value.partition(":")
+    if not sep or backend not in ("openrouter", "api", "subscription"):
+        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("use BACKEND:MODEL[,MODEL], backend openrouter/api/subscription")
+    models = tuple(m.strip() for m in names.split(","))
+    if not all(models) or any(any(c.isspace() for c in m) for m in models):
+        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("model IDs must be nonempty and contain no whitespace")
+    if backend != "openrouter" and len(models) != 1:
+        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("model races require the openrouter backend")
+    return backend, models
+
+
+def positive_int(value: str) -> int:
+    n = int(value)
+    if n < 1:
+        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be at least 1")
+    return n
+
+
+def nonnegative_float(value: str) -> float:
+    n = float(value)
+    if not math.isfinite(n) or n < 0:
+        raise argparse.ArgumentTypeError("must be finite and nonnegative")
+    return n
+
+
+def validate_translation(payload: dict, body: dict) -> int:
+    """Check every translated block against its source, including image bytes and order."""
+    expected = []
+    system = payload.get("system")
+    if system:
+        text = system if isinstance(system, str) else " ".join(b.get("text", "") for b in system)
+        expected.append({"role": "system", "content": text})
+    images = 0
+    for message in payload["messages"]:
+        content = message["content"]
+        if isinstance(content, list):
+            parts = []
+            for block in content:
+                if block["type"] == "text":
+                    parts.append({"type": "text", "text": block["text"]})
+                elif block["type"] == "image":
+                    src = block["source"]
+                    data = base64.b64decode(src["data"], validate=True)
+                    if src["media_type"] != "image/jpeg" or not valid_jpeg(data):
+                        raise ValueError("monitor input must contain complete JPEG images")
+                    parts.append({"type": "image_url", "image_url": {
+                        "url": f"data:image/jpeg;base64,{src['data']}"}})
+                    images += 1
+                else:
+                    raise ValueError("unsupported monitor content block")
+            content = parts
+        expected.append({"role": message["role"], "content": content})
+    if body["messages"] != expected or body["max_tokens"] != payload["max_tokens"] or images == 0:
+        raise ValueError("monitor request translation mismatch")
+    if set(body) - {"reasoning"} != {"model", "messages", "max_tokens"}:
+        raise ValueError("unexpected wire fields")
+    json.dumps(body, allow_nan=False)
+    return images
+
+
+def numeric_cost(usage: Any) -> float | None:
+    value = usage.get("cost") if isinstance(usage, dict) else None
+    if isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)):
+        return None
+    return float(value) if math.isfinite(value) and value >= 0 else None
+
+
+class Accounting:
+    """One trial's attempts. No prompts, reply text, headers or exception messages are retained."""
+
+    def __init__(self) -> None:
+        self.condition = threading.Condition()
+        self.events: list[dict] = []
+
+    def record(self, event: dict) -> None:
+        usage = event.get("usage")
+        usage = usage if isinstance(usage, dict) else {}
+        # Retain only numeric usage fields, never provider-supplied strings.
+        safe_usage = {k: v for k, v in usage.items()
+                      if k in ("cost", "prompt_tokens", "completion_tokens", "total_tokens",
+                               "input_tokens", "output_tokens")
+                      and isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool) and math.isfinite(v)}
+        with self.condition:
+            self.events.append({"model": event["model"], "usage": safe_usage,
+                                "cost_usd": numeric_cost(usage),
+                                "latency_s": event.get("latency_s"),
+                                "error_type": event.get("error_type")})
+            self.condition.notify_all()
+
+    def drain(self, expected: int, timeout_s: float) -> tuple[list[dict], bool]:
+        with self.condition:
+            complete = self.condition.wait_for(lambda: len(self.events) >= expected, timeout=timeout_s)
+            return list(self.events), complete
+
+
+def transport_for(judge: Judge, accounting: Accounting, *, dry_run: bool, timeout_s: float):
+    if dry_run:
+        def dry(payload: dict) -> dict:
+            for model in judge.models:
+                started = time.monotonic()
+                error = None
+                try:
+                    validate_translation(payload, openrouter_request_body(model, payload))
+                except Exception as exc:
+                    error = type(exc).__name__
+                    raise
+                finally:
+                    accounting.record({"model": model, "latency_s": time.monotonic() - started,
+                                       "error_type": error})
+            # A stub exercises the monitor parser and hedge only. It is never scored.
+            return {"content": [{"type": "text", "text": '<json>{"stage_done": false, '
+                                 '"guard": false, "confidence": 1, "reason": "dry-run stub"}</json>'}]}
+        return dry
+    if judge.backend == "openrouter":
+        return openrouter_transport(list(judge.models), timeout_s=timeout_s, on_attempt=accounting.record)
+    if judge.backend == "api":
+        key = os.environ.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
+        if not key:
+            raise ValueError("api backend needs ANTHROPIC_API_KEY")
+        raw = anthropic_transport(key, timeout_s)
+    else:
+        raw = claude_cli_transport(timeout_s)
+
+    def observed(payload: dict) -> dict:
+        started, reply, error = time.monotonic(), {}, None
+        try:
+            reply = raw(payload)
+            return reply
+        except Exception as exc:
+            error = type(exc).__name__
+            raise
+        finally:
+            accounting.record({"model": judge.models[0], "usage": reply.get("usage"),
+                               "latency_s": time.monotonic() - started, "error_type": error})
+    return observed
+
+
+def run_trial(plan: Plan, judge: Judge, case: tuple, frames: dict[str, bytes], repeat: int,
+              *, dry_run: bool, timeout_s: float) -> dict:
+    source, stage_name, want_done, want_guard = case
+    trial_plan = copy.deepcopy(plan)
+    trial_plan.claude_user.update(hedge=judge.hedge, timeout_s=timeout_s)
+    accounting = Accounting()
+    transport = transport_for(judge, accounting, dry_run=dry_run, timeout_s=timeout_s)
+    mon = MonitorVerifier(trial_plan, model=judge.models[0], transport=transport, timeout_s=timeout_s)
+    stage = next(s for s in trial_plan.stages if s.name == stage_name)
+    started = time.monotonic()
+    verdict = mon.check_now(stage, frames, 5.0)
+    latency = time.monotonic() - started
+    events, drained = accounting.drain(judge.attempts, timeout_s + 5)
+    transport_failed = any(e["error_type"] for e in events)
+    parse_failed = verdict.stage_done is None and any(not e["error_type"] for e in events)
+    cost_complete = drained and all(e["cost_usd"] is not None for e in events)
+    return {
+        "judge": judge.name, "backend": judge.backend, "models": list(judge.models), "hedge": judge.hedge,
+        "source": source, "stage": stage_name, "repeat": repeat, "frames_sha1": frames_digest(frames),
+        "expected_stage_done": want_done, "expected_guard": want_guard,
+        "stage_done": None if dry_run else verdict.stage_done,
+        "accepted_done": None if dry_run else verdict.done is True,
+        "guard": None if dry_run else verdict.guard,
+        "stage_correct": None if dry_run else verdict.stage_done is want_done,
+        "accepted_done_correct": None if dry_run else verdict.stage_done is not None and (verdict.done is True) == want_done,
+        "guard_correct": None if dry_run or want_guard is None else verdict.guard is want_guard,
+        "parse_failure": None if dry_run else parse_failed,
+        "transport_failure": None if dry_run else transport_failed,
+        "dry_run_valid": drained and not transport_failed if dry_run else None,
+        "latency_s": None if dry_run else latency,
+        "attempts_expected": judge.attempts, "attempts_finished": len(events), "drained": drained,
+        "known_cost_usd": sum(e["cost_usd"] or 0 for e in events),
+        "cost_usd": sum(e["cost_usd"] for e in events) if cost_complete and not dry_run else None,
+        "cost_complete": cost_complete and not dry_run, "attempts": events,
+    }
+
+
+def percentile(values: list[float], fraction: float) -> float | None:
+    if not values:
+        return None
+    xs = sorted(values)
+    pos = (len(xs) - 1) * fraction
+    lo, hi = math.floor(pos), math.ceil(pos)
+    return xs[lo] + (xs[hi] - xs[lo]) * (pos - lo)
+
+
+def summaries(rows: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:
+    out = []
+    for name in dict.fromkeys(r["judge"] for r in rows):
+        group = [r for r in rows if r["judge"] == name]
+        def mean(field: str):
+            values = [r[field] for r in group if r[field] is not None]
+            return sum(values) / len(values) if values else None
+        times = [r["latency_s"] for r in group if r["latency_s"] is not None]
+        finished = sum(r["attempts_finished"] for r in group)
+        complete = all(r["cost_complete"] for r in group)
+        known = sum(r["known_cost_usd"] for r in group)
+        out.append({"judge": name, "trials": len(group), "guard_trials": sum(r["expected_guard"] is not None for r in group),
+                    "stage_done_accuracy": mean("stage_correct"), "accepted_done_accuracy": mean("accepted_done_correct"),
+                    "guard_accuracy": mean("guard_correct"), "parse_failure_rate": mean("parse_failure"),
+                    "transport_failure_rate": mean("transport_failure"), "dry_run_valid_rate": mean("dry_run_valid"),
+                    "latency_p50_s": percentile(times, .5), "latency_p90_s": percentile(times, .9),
+                    "latency_max_s": max(times) if times else None,
+                    "attempts_finished": finished, "cost_complete": complete, "known_cost_usd": known,
+                    "cost_usd": known if complete else None,
+                    "cost_per_call_usd": known / finished if complete and finished else None,
+                    "cost_per_trial_usd": known / len(group) if complete else None})
+    return out
+
+
+def write_reports(out: Path, rows: list[dict], metadata: dict) -> list[dict]:
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    summary = summaries(rows)
+    (out / "results.json").write_text(json.dumps({**metadata, "summary": summary, "trials": rows},
+                                                indent=2, allow_nan=False) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    with (out / "summary.csv").open("w", newline="", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
+        writer = csv.DictWriter(fh, fieldnames=SUMMARY_FIELDS)
+        writer.writeheader()
+        writer.writerows(summary)
+    return summary
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
+    ap.add_argument("--judge", action="append", type=judge_spec, metavar="BACKEND:MODEL[,MODEL]",
+                    help="repeat for multiple configs; default: eight OpenRouter singles plus a Haiku/Gemini race")
+    ap.add_argument("--hedge", action="append", type=positive_int,
+                    help="claude.hedge copies, repeat to compare e.g. 1 and 2; default: 1")
+    ap.add_argument("-n", "--repeat", type=positive_int, default=1)
+    ap.add_argument("--dry-run", action="store_true")
+    ap.add_argument("--no-sim", action="store_true", help="photo cases only; default includes MuJoCo renders")
+    ap.add_argument("--plan", type=Path, default=ROOT / "plans/hill_climb_v4.yaml")
+    ap.add_argument("--max-cost-usd", type=nonnegative_float, default=1.0,
+                    help="stop new trials at reported spend; missing cost also stops live runs; default $1")
+    ap.add_argument("--timeout-s", type=nonnegative_float, default=90.0)
+    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=ROOT / "runs" / "bench_judges")
+    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
+    if args.timeout_s == 0:
+        ap.error("--timeout-s must be positive")
+    specs = args.judge or [("openrouter", (m,)) for m in DEFAULT_MODELS] + [
+        ("openrouter", (BASELINE, "google/gemini-3.1-flash-lite"))]
+    judges = list(dict.fromkeys(Judge(b, m, h) for b, m in specs for h in (args.hedge or [1])))
+    plan = load_plan(args.plan)
+    cases = list(CASES) + ([] if args.no_sim else list(SIM_CASES))
+    if not {c[1] for c in cases} <= {s.name for s in plan.stages}:
+        ap.error("plan is missing required known-answer stages")
+    if not plan.guard or not all(plan.guard.covers(c[1]) for c in cases if c[3] is not None):
+        ap.error("plan must ask the guard on every guard-labelled case")
+    metadata = {"dry_run": args.dry_run, "max_cost_usd": args.max_cost_usd,
+                "plan": str(args.plan), "plan_sha256": hashlib.sha256(args.plan.read_bytes()).hexdigest(),
+                "repeats": args.repeat, "sim": not args.no_sim,
+                "configs": [j.name for j in judges], "stopped": None}
+    rows: list[dict] = []
+    sources = {}
+    print(f"judge benchmark: {'DRY RUN, no network' if args.dry_run else 'LIVE, billable'}; {len(judges)} configs")
+    try:
+        if not args.dry_run and args.max_cost_usd == 0:
+            metadata["stopped"] = "max_cost_usd"
+        else:
+            sources["photo"] = {"overhead": PHOTO.read_bytes()}
+            if not args.no_sim:
+                sources["sim_empty"], sources["sim_eight_held"] = sim_frames(False), sim_frames(True)
+            spent = 0.0
+            for judge in judges:
+                for case in cases:
+                    for repeat in range(1, args.repeat + 1):
+                        if metadata["stopped"]:
+                            break
+                        row = run_trial(plan, judge, case, sources[case[0]], repeat,
+                                        dry_run=args.dry_run, timeout_s=args.timeout_s)
+                        rows.append(row)
+                        spent += row["known_cost_usd"]
+                        if args.dry_run:
+                            if not row["dry_run_valid"]:
+                                metadata["stopped"] = "dry_run_validation_failed"
+                        elif not row["cost_complete"]:
+                            metadata["stopped"] = "incomplete_cost_accounting"
+                        elif spent >= args.max_cost_usd:
+                            metadata["stopped"] = "max_cost_usd"
+                        write_reports(args.out, rows, metadata)
+                    if metadata["stopped"]:
+                        break
+                if metadata["stopped"]:
+                    break
+    except KeyboardInterrupt:
+        metadata["stopped"] = "interrupted"
+    except (Exception, SystemExit) as exc:
+        # Credentials can occur in upstream exception text. Report only the type.
+        metadata["stopped"] = f"setup_or_run_error:{type(exc).__name__}"
+    summary = write_reports(args.out, rows, metadata)
+    for item in summary:
+        print(json.dumps(item, allow_nan=False))
+    print(f"reports: {args.out / 'summary.csv'} and {args.out / 'results.json'}")
+    if metadata["stopped"]:
+        print(f"stopped: {metadata['stopped']}")
+        return 2
+    return 0 if args.dry_run or all(r["stage_correct"] and r["guard_correct"] is not False for r in rows) else 1
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())

+"""Procedural model of the station's orange USB-A to USB-C cable, rendered with domain randomization.
+
+    blender -b -P render_cable.py -- OUT_DIR N_IMAGES BG_DIR [SEED]
+
+Model (mm, from the station photos IMG_2702-2704 and the USB specs):
+- USB-A plug: steel shell 12.0 x 4.5 x 12.0, two latch windows on the top face, dark tongue inside.
+- USB-C plug: steel shell 8.25 x 2.4 x 6.65, stadium cross-section, dark slot.
+- Ferrules: orange anodised aluminium, rounded boxes; A end 13.0 x 7.0 x 19.0, C end 11.0 x 6.0 x 18.0.
+- Strain reliefs: black, 15 mm, ribbed, tapering 6.0 -> 4.6 mm, orange showing between the ribs.
+- Cable: orange rubber, 4.0 mm, 150-220 mm long, random smooth bend in 3D.
+Each image: random cable shape and pose on a plane textured with a random crop of a real station frame or photo,
+random lights (sun + 1-3 area lights, colour temperature, strength), random camera (wide wrist-like or narrow
+lens, 5-40 cm away) aimed at one of: the USB-C end, the USB-A end, the cable middle, a strain relief, empty table.
+Random material roughness/colour jitter, depth of field and motion-free noise. Writes OUT_DIR/<label>/<n>.png
+(224 x 224) and OUT_DIR/labels.jsonl with the projected ferrule length in pixels.
+"""
+
+import json
+import math
+import os
+import random
+import sys
+
+import bpy
+from mathutils import Vector
+
+argv = sys.argv[sys.argv.index("--") + 1:]
+OUT, N, BG = os.path.abspath(argv[0]), int(argv[1]), os.path.abspath(argv[2])
+SEED = int(argv[3]) if len(argv) > 3 else 0
+rng = random.Random(SEED)
+MM = 0.001
+LABELS = ("usb_c", "usb_a", "none")
+
+BGS = [os.path.join(BG, f) for f in sorted(os.listdir(BG)) if f.lower().endswith((".jpg", ".png"))]
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- scene setup
+def reset():
+    bpy.ops.wm.read_factory_settings(use_empty=True)
+    sc = bpy.context.scene
+    sc.render.engine = "CYCLES"
+    prefs = bpy.context.preferences.addons["cycles"].preferences
+    for kind in ("OPTIX", "CUDA"):
+        try:
+            prefs.compute_device_type = kind
+            prefs.get_devices()
+            for d in prefs.devices:
+                d.use = True
+            sc.cycles.device = "GPU"
+            break
+        except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001
+            continue
+    sc.cycles.samples = 48
+    sc.cycles.use_denoising = True
+    sc.render.resolution_x = sc.render.resolution_y = 224
+    sc.render.film_transparent = False
+    sc.view_settings.view_transform = "Standard"
+    sc.view_settings.exposure = rng.uniform(-1.8, 0.1)
+    return sc
+
+
+def mat(name, color, metallic=0.0, rough=0.5, spec=0.5):
+    m = bpy.data.materials.new(name)
+    m.use_nodes = True
+    b = m.node_tree.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
+    b.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*color, 1.0)
+    b.inputs["Metallic"].default_value = metallic
+    b.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rough
+    return m
+
+
+def jitter(c, amt):
+    return tuple(min(1.0, max(0.0, v * (1 + rng.uniform(-amt, amt)))) for v in c)
+
+
+def mix(a, b, t):
+    return tuple(x + (y - x) * t for x, y in zip(a, b))
+
+
+BRIGHT = (0.9, 0.16, 0.005)      # linear RGB: saturated anodised orange
+SALMON = (0.80, 0.22, 0.10)      # linear RGB: the station cable's copper / salmon look on the wrist cameras
+
+
+def striped(name, base, rough):
+    """Black / orange braid bands along the object's local axis (cable and relief core)."""
+    m = mat(name, base, rough=rough)
+    nt = m.node_tree
+    wave = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexWave")
+    wave.wave_type = "BANDS"
+    wave.bands_direction = rng.choice(["X", "Y", "Z", "DIAGONAL"])
+    wave.inputs["Scale"].default_value = rng.uniform(40, 160)
+    wave.inputs["Distortion"].default_value = rng.uniform(0, 2)
+    coord = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
+    nt.links.new(coord.outputs["Object"], wave.inputs["Vector"])
+    ramp = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeValToRGB")
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (0.02, 0.02, 0.02, 1)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (*base, 1)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.5)
+    ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position + 0.05
+    nt.links.new(wave.outputs["Fac"], ramp.inputs["Fac"])
+    nt.links.new(ramp.outputs["Color"], nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"].inputs["Base Color"])
+    return m
+
+
+def materials():
+    orange = jitter(mix(BRIGHT, SALMON, rng.random()), 0.1)
+    rubber_rough = rng.uniform(0.4, 0.8)
+    rubber = striped("rubber", orange, rubber_rough) if rng.random() < 0.5 else mat("rubber", orange, rough=rubber_rough)
+    return {
+        "orange_anod": mat("anod", jitter(orange, 0.06), metallic=rng.uniform(0.2, 0.9),
+                           rough=rng.uniform(0.2, 0.55)),
+        "orange_rubber": rubber,
+        "black": mat("black", jitter((0.02, 0.02, 0.02), 0.5), rough=rng.uniform(0.4, 0.8)),
+        "steel": mat("steel", jitter((0.75, 0.75, 0.76), 0.1), metallic=1.0, rough=rng.uniform(0.1, 0.35)),
+        "dark": mat("dark", (0.03, 0.03, 0.035), rough=0.6),
+        "strip": mat("strip", jitter((0.03, 0.03, 0.03), 0.5), rough=rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7)),
+        "ball": mat("ball", jitter(mix((0.9, 0.25, 0.08), SALMON, rng.random()), 0.1), rough=rng.uniform(0.3, 0.7)),
+        "skin": mat("skin", jitter(rng.choice([(0.6, 0.35, 0.25), (0.35, 0.17, 0.1), (0.15, 0.07, 0.04),
+                                              (0.8, 0.5, 0.4)]), 0.15), rough=0.5),
+        "blue": mat("blue", jitter((0.02, 0.25, 0.6), 0.15), rough=0.5),
+    }
+
+
+def power_strip(M, face_at, side):
+    """Black power strip whose long side face (the USB ports) is at face_at, facing `side` (horizontal unit).
+    Returns (along unit vector, strip height)."""
+    L, W, H = rng.uniform(0.18, 0.3), rng.uniform(0.045, 0.06), rng.uniform(0.03, 0.045)
+    box = rbox("strip", (L, W, H), 0.004, M["strip"])
+    c = face_at - side * (W / 2)
+    box.location = (c.x + side.y * rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3) * L, c.y - side.x * rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3) * L, H / 2)
+    box.rotation_euler = (0, 0, math.atan2(side.x, -side.y))
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    return Vector((-side.y, side.x, 0)), H
+
+
+def yaw_for(d):
+    """Root yaw so the end's local +Y (towards the tip) points along horizontal d."""
+    return math.atan2(-d.x, d.y)
+
+
+def distractors(M, centre, n):
+    """Golf ball, finger stand-ins, blue block near the cable: the orange-ish things that are not ends."""
+    for _ in range(n):
+        p = centre + Vector((rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), rng.uniform(-0.06, 0.06), 0))
+        k = rng.random()
+        if k < 0.4:
+            r = 0.02135
+            bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_uv_sphere_add(radius=r, location=(p.x, p.y, r))
+            o = bpy.context.object
+            o.data.materials.append(M["ball"])
+            bpy.ops.object.shade_smooth()
+        elif k < 0.75:
+            r = rng.uniform(0.007, 0.01)
+            bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=r, depth=rng.uniform(0.04, 0.08),
+                                                location=(p.x, p.y, r + rng.uniform(0, 0.04)))
+            o = bpy.context.object
+            o.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+            bev = o.modifiers.new("b", "BEVEL")
+            bev.width, bev.segments = r * 0.9, 6
+            o.data.materials.append(M["skin"])
+        else:
+            s = rng.uniform(0.025, 0.04)
+            o = rbox("block", (s, s, s), 0.002, M["blue"])
+            o.location = (p.x, p.y, s / 2)
+            o.rotation_euler = (0, 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+
+
+def rbox(name, size, bevel, m):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cube_add(size=1)
+    o = bpy.context.object
+    o.name = name
+    o.scale = size
+    bpy.ops.object.transform_apply(scale=True)
+    if bevel > 0:
+        mod = o.modifiers.new("bev", "BEVEL")
+        mod.width = bevel
+        mod.segments = 4
+    o.data.materials.append(m)
+    return o
+
+
+def stadium(name, w, h, length, m):
+    """Rounded-end slab along +Y (the USB-C shell)."""
+    o = rbox(name, (w, length, h), h * 0.49, m)
+    return o
+
+
+def build_end(kind, M):
+    """One plug + ferrule + strain relief, along +Y from the origin (relief at y<0, tip at y>0). Returns
+    (parent empty, tip centre local, ferrule centre local, ferrule length)."""
+    parts = []
+    if kind == "a":
+        fw, fh, fl = 13.0, 7.0, 19.0
+        tw, th, tl = 12.0, 4.5, 12.0
+    else:
+        fw, fh, fl = 11.0, 6.0, 18.0
+        tw, th, tl = 8.25, 2.4, 6.65
+    fw, fh, fl = (v * rng.uniform(0.95, 1.05) for v in (fw, fh, fl))
+    fer = rbox("ferrule", (fw * MM, fl * MM, fh * MM), min(fw, fh) * 0.25 * MM, M["orange_anod"])
+    fer.location = (0, fl / 2 * MM, 0)
+    parts.append(fer)
+    if kind == "a":
+        shell = rbox("shell", (tw * MM, tl * MM, th * MM), 0.3 * MM, M["steel"])
+        shell.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2) * MM, 0)
+        tongue = rbox("tongue", ((tw - 1.2) * MM, (tl - 0.5) * MM, (th - 1.6) * MM), 0, M["dark"])
+        tongue.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2 + 0.3) * MM, -0.3 * MM)
+        parts += [shell, tongue]
+        for sx in (-2.5, 2.5):   # latch windows on the top face
+            win = rbox("win", (2.0 * MM, 2.5 * MM, 0.3 * MM), 0, M["dark"])
+            win.location = (sx * MM, (fl + tl * 0.55) * MM, (th / 2 + 0.05) * MM)
+            parts.append(win)
+    else:
+        shell = stadium("shell", tw * MM, th * MM, tl * MM, M["steel"])
+        shell.location = (0, (fl + tl / 2) * MM, 0)
+        slot = rbox("slot", ((tw - 1.4) * MM, 0.3 * MM, (th - 1.0) * MM), (th - 1.0) * 0.45 * MM, M["dark"])
+        slot.location = (0, (fl + tl + 0.05) * MM, 0)
+        parts += [shell, slot]
+    # strain relief: tapering ribs
+    n_rib = 7
+    for k in range(n_rib):
+        r = (3.0 - 0.7 * k / n_rib) * MM
+        bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=r, depth=1.3 * MM, vertices=24)
+        rib = bpy.context.object
+        rib.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
+        rib.location = (0, -(1.0 + k * 2.1) * MM, 0)
+        rib.data.materials.append(M["black"])
+        parts.append(rib)
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_cylinder_add(radius=2.2 * MM, depth=15 * MM, vertices=24)
+    core = bpy.context.object
+    core.rotation_euler = (math.pi / 2, 0, 0)
+    core.location = (0, -7.5 * MM, 0)
+    core.data.materials.append(M["orange_rubber"])
+    parts.append(core)
+    root = bpy.data.objects.new(f"end_{kind}", None)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(root)
+    for p in parts:
+        p.parent = root
+    tip = Vector((0, (fl + tl * 0.5) * MM, 0))
+    return root, tip, Vector((0, fl / 2 * MM, 0)), fl * MM
+
+
+def cable_curve(p0, d0, p1, d1, M):
+    """Orange cable from the C relief end to the A relief end: a Bezier with tangents along each end's axis."""
+    cu = bpy.data.curves.new("cable", "CURVE")
+    cu.dimensions = "3D"
+    cu.bevel_depth = 2.0 * MM
+    cu.bevel_resolution = 4
+    sp = cu.splines.new("BEZIER")
+    sp.bezier_points.add(1)
+    k = (p1 - p0).length * rng.uniform(0.3, 0.6)
+    for bp, p, d, sign in ((sp.bezier_points[0], p0, d0, -1), (sp.bezier_points[1], p1, d1, -1)):
+        bp.co = p
+        bp.handle_left_type = bp.handle_right_type = "FREE"
+        bp.handle_right = p + d * sign * k * (1 if bp is sp.bezier_points[0] else -1)
+        bp.handle_left = p - (bp.handle_right - p)
+    o = bpy.data.objects.new("cable", cu)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(o)
+    o.data.materials.append(M["orange_rubber"])
+    return o
+
+
+def place(root, pos, yaw, pitch=0.0, roll=0.0):
+    root.location = pos
+    root.rotation_euler = (pitch, roll, yaw)
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+
+
+def world_pt(root, local):
+    return root.matrix_world @ local
+
+
+def table(sc):
+    bpy.ops.mesh.primitive_plane_add(size=1.2)
+    pl = bpy.context.object
+    m = bpy.data.materials.new("table")
+    m.use_nodes = True
+    nt = m.node_tree
+    bsdf = nt.nodes["Principled BSDF"]
+    bsdf.inputs["Roughness"].default_value = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.9)
+    if BGS and rng.random() < 0.85:
+        tex = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexImage")
+        tex.image = bpy.data.images.load(rng.choice(BGS))
+        mapn = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeMapping")
+        coord = nt.nodes.new("ShaderNodeTexCoord")
+        s = rng.uniform(0.6, 3.0)
+        mapn.inputs["Scale"].default_value = (s, s, 1)
+        mapn.inputs["Rotation"].default_value = (0, 0, rng.uniform(0, 6.28))
+        mapn.inputs["Location"].default_value = (rng.random(), rng.random(), 0)
+        nt.links.new(coord.outputs["UV"], mapn.inputs["Vector"])
+        nt.links.new(mapn.outputs["Vector"], tex.inputs["Vector"])
+        nt.links.new(tex.outputs["Color"], bsdf.inputs["Base Color"])
+    else:
+        g = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.9)
+        bsdf.inputs["Base Color"].default_value = (*jitter((g, g, g * rng.uniform(0.9, 1.05)), 0.1), 1)
+    pl.data.materials.append(m)
+    return pl
+
+
+def kelvin(t):
+    t = t / 100.0
+    r = 1.0 if t <= 66 else min(1.0, 1.29 * (t - 60) ** -0.133)
+    g = min(1.0, (0.39 * math.log(t) - 0.63)) if t <= 66 else min(1.0, 1.13 * (t - 60) ** -0.0755)
+    b = 1.0 if t >= 66 else (0.0 if t <= 19 else min(1.0, 0.543 * math.log(t - 10) - 1.19))
+    return (max(r, 0), max(g, 0), max(b, 0))
+
+
+def lights(sc):
+    w = bpy.data.worlds.new("w")
+    sc.world = w
+    w.use_nodes = True
+    w.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Color"].default_value = (*kelvin(rng.uniform(3000, 7500)), 1)
+    w.node_tree.nodes["Background"].inputs["Strength"].default_value = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.6)
+    bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="SUN", rotation=(rng.uniform(0, 1.2), rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rng.uniform(0, 6.28)))
+    sun = bpy.context.object
+    sun.data.energy = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.5)
+    sun.data.color = kelvin(rng.uniform(3500, 7000))
+    for _ in range(rng.randint(1, 3)):
+        bpy.ops.object.light_add(type="AREA", location=(rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6), rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6),
+                                                         rng.uniform(0.4, 2.5)))
+        a = bpy.context.object
+        a.data.size = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.5)
+        a.data.energy = rng.uniform(5, 70)
+        a.data.color = kelvin(rng.uniform(3000, 7500))
+        direction = Vector((0, 0, 0)) - a.location
+        a.rotation_euler = direction.to_track_quat("-Z", "Y").to_euler()
+
+
+def camera(sc, target, ferrule_len, kind):
+    cam_data = bpy.data.cameras.new("cam")
+    wide = rng.random() < 0.6
+    cam_data.lens = rng.uniform(10, 16) if wide else rng.uniform(24, 60)
+    cam_data.clip_start = 0.001   # the camera is 2-30 cm from the plug; the 10 cm default clips it away
+    cam = bpy.data.objects.new("cam", cam_data)
+    bpy.context.collection.objects.link(cam)
+    sc.camera = cam
+    # distance so the ferrule spans a random share of the frame (what the classifier crop will look like)
+    frac = rng.uniform(0.18, 0.55)
+    fov = 2 * math.atan(cam_data.sensor_width / 2 / cam_data.lens)
+    dist = ferrule_len / frac / (2 * math.tan(fov / 2))
+    el = rng.uniform(math.radians(20), math.radians(88))
+    az = rng.uniform(0, 2 * math.pi)
+    off = Vector((math.cos(az) * math.cos(el), math.sin(az) * math.cos(el), math.sin(el))) * dist
+    cam.location = target + off
+    q = (target - cam.location).to_track_quat("-Z", "Y")
+    cam.rotation_euler = q.to_euler()
+    cam.rotation_euler.rotate_axis("Z", rng.uniform(-math.pi, math.pi))
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:
+        cam_data.dof.use_dof = True
+        cam_data.dof.focus_distance = dist * rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
+        cam_data.dof.aperture_fstop = rng.uniform(2.0, 8.0)
+    return cam, frac
+
+
+def seen(sc, cam, tgt, root):
+    """Line of sight from the camera to the target: for an end, the first thing hit must be part of that end (or
+    nothing before it); for 'none', the camera must not be inside or right against an object."""
+    dg = bpy.context.evaluated_depsgraph_get()
+    o = cam.matrix_world.translation
+    if sc.ray_cast(dg, o, Vector((0, 0, 1)), distance=1.0)[0]:
+        return False     # something right above the camera: it is inside an object (the strip, the ball)
+    v = tgt - o
+    dist = v.length
+    hit, loc, _, _, obj, _ = sc.ray_cast(dg, o, v.normalized(), distance=dist * 1.2)
+    if not hit:
+        return root is None
+    if root is None:
+        return (loc - o).length > 0.4 * dist
+    return obj.parent == root or (loc - o).length > dist * 0.97
+
+
+def one(i):
+    sc = reset()
+    M = materials()
+    table(sc)
+    lights(sc)
+    c_root, c_tip, c_fer, c_fl = build_end("c", M)
+    a_root, a_tip, a_fer, a_fl = build_end("a", M)
+    length = rng.uniform(0.15, 0.22)
+    yaw = rng.uniform(0, 2 * math.pi)
+    lift = 3.5 * MM
+    place(c_root, Vector((0, 0, lift)), yaw + rng.uniform(-0.9, 0.9), rng.uniform(-0.15, 0.15),
+          rng.choice([0, math.pi / 2, math.pi, -math.pi / 2]) + rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2))
+    p_a = Vector((math.cos(yaw) * length * 0.7 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05),
+                  math.sin(yaw) * length * 0.7 + rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05), lift))
+    place(a_root, p_a, yaw + math.pi + rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2), rng.uniform(-0.15, 0.15),
+          rng.choice([0, math.pi / 2, math.pi, -math.pi / 2]) + rng.uniform(-0.2, 0.2))
+    if rng.random() < 0.15:      # held in the air: tilt the whole end up off the table
+        c_root.location.z += rng.uniform(0.01, 0.08)
+        c_root.rotation_euler.x += rng.uniform(-0.8, 0.8)
+    bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    rel = lambda root: (root.matrix_world @ Vector((0, -15 * MM, 0)),
+                        (root.matrix_world.to_3x3() @ Vector((0, -1, 0))).normalized())
+    label = rng.choices(LABELS, weights=(0.4, 0.3, 0.3))[0]
+    seated = rng.random() < (0.5 if label == "usb_a" else 0.25)
+    if seated:
+        # the A end seated in a power strip's USB port, often with other cables' A ends next to it
+        side = Vector((-p_a.x, -p_a.y, 0)).normalized()
+        along, H = power_strip(M, p_a, side)
+        z = H * rng.uniform(0.35, 0.65)
+        gap = rng.uniform(0, 1.5) * MM
+        a_root.location = p_a + side * (a_fl + gap) + Vector((0, 0, z))
+        a_root.rotation_euler = (0, rng.choice([0, math.pi]), yaw_for(-side))
+        for k in rng.sample([-2, -1, 1, 2], rng.randint(0, 3)):
+            n_root, _, _, n_fl = build_end("a", M)
+            n_root.location = (p_a + along * k * rng.uniform(0.022, 0.035) + side * (n_fl + rng.uniform(0, 1.5) * MM)
+                               + Vector((0, 0, z)))
+            n_root.rotation_euler = (0, rng.choice([0, math.pi]), yaw_for(-side))
+            bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+            pn, dn = rel(n_root)
+            far = pn + side * rng.uniform(0.08, 0.15) + along * rng.uniform(-0.05, 0.05)
+            far.z = 2 * MM
+            cable_curve(pn, dn, far, -side, M)
+        bpy.context.view_layer.update()
+    pc, dc = rel(c_root)
+    pa, da = rel(a_root)
+    cable_curve(pc, dc, pa, da, M)
+    spots = []
+    if rng.random() < (0.7 if label == "none" else 0.35):
+        centre = rng.choice([pc, pa, (pc + pa) / 2])
+        centre = Vector((centre.x, centre.y, 0))
+        before = set(bpy.data.objects)
+        distractors(M, centre, rng.randint(1, 3))
+        spots = [o.location.copy() for o in bpy.data.objects if o not in before]
+    if label == "usb_c":
+        tgt = (world_pt(c_root, c_fer) + world_pt(c_root, c_tip)) / 2
+        fl = c_fl
+    elif label == "usb_a":
+        tgt = world_pt(a_root, a_fer) if seated else (world_pt(a_root, a_fer) + world_pt(a_root, a_tip)) / 2
+        fl = a_fl
+    else:
+        what = rng.random()
+        if spots and rng.random() < 0.5:
+            tgt = rng.choice(spots).copy()
+        elif what < 0.4:
+            tgt = (pc + pa) / 2 + Vector((rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), rng.uniform(-0.03, 0.03), 0))
+        elif what < 0.7:
+            tgt = rng.choice([pc, pa]) + Vector((0, 0, 0))
+        else:
+            tgt = Vector((rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3), rng.uniform(-0.3, 0.3), 0))
+        fl = rng.uniform(15, 19) * MM
+    tgt += Vector((rng.uniform(-1, 1), rng.uniform(-1, 1), 0)) * fl * 0.25   # not always dead centre
+    want = {"usb_c": c_root, "usb_a": a_root}.get(label)
+    for _ in range(15):
+        cam, frac = camera(sc, tgt, fl, label)
+        if seen(sc, cam, tgt, want):
+            break
+        bpy.data.objects.remove(cam, do_unlink=True)
+    else:
+        return None      # no clear view of the end in this scene: skip it rather than mislabel it
+    d = os.path.join(OUT, label)
+    os.makedirs(d, exist_ok=True)
+    path = os.path.join(d, f"{SEED:03d}_{i:05d}.png")
+    sc.render.filepath = path
+    bpy.ops.render.render(write_still=True)
+    return {"file": os.path.relpath(path, OUT), "label": label, "ferrule_frac": round(frac, 3), "seed": SEED, "i": i}
+
+
+os.makedirs(OUT, exist_ok=True)
+with open(os.path.join(OUT, f"labels_{SEED:03d}.jsonl"), "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
+    for i in range(N):
+        rec = one(i)
+        if rec is None:
+            continue
+        f.write(json.dumps(rec) + "\n")
+        f.flush()
diff --git a/tools/cable_end/train.py b/tools/cable_end/train.py
