+"""Train the cable-end classifier (usb_c / usb_a / none) on Blender renders + real station crops; export ONNX.
+
+    python train.py [--epochs 12] [--real-train 0.5] [--out cable_end.onnx]
+
+Inputs: synth/<label>/*.png (render_cable.py), real/cands/*.png + real/labels.json + real/cands.json.
+Real crops are split by source video (crops of one video never sit on both sides); the high-resolution photos
+(table_photos/*) are always test. 'unsure' real crops are never used. Reports per-class precision/recall on the real
+test crops for a synthetic-only model and for synthetic + real-train, and checks the exported ONNX against torch
+with OpenCV's DNN module (what the harness runs).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import glob
+import json
+import os
+import random
+
+import cv2
+import numpy as np
+import torch
+import torch.nn as nn
+import torchvision
+
+LABELS = ["usb_c", "usb_a", "none"]
+SIZE = 96
+MEAN = np.array([0.485, 0.456, 0.406], np.float32)
+STD = np.array([0.229, 0.224, 0.225], np.float32)
+HERE = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
+
+
+def prep(bgr: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
+    """BGR uint8 (any size) -> CHW float32 normalised, SIZE x SIZE. The harness does the same in numpy."""
+    im = cv2.resize(bgr, (SIZE, SIZE), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)[:, :, ::-1].astype(np.float32) / 255.0
+    return ((im - MEAN) / STD).transpose(2, 0, 1).copy()
+
+
+def augment(bgr: np.ndarray, rng: random.Random, synth: bool) -> np.ndarray:
+    h, w = bgr.shape[:2]
+    # zoom/shift/rotate: the runtime crop is centred on a detected ferrule but not perfectly
+    z = rng.uniform(0.75, 1.35) if synth else rng.uniform(0.85, 1.15)
+    ang = rng.uniform(-180, 180)
+    M = cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((w / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.1) * w, h / 2 + rng.uniform(-0.1, 0.1) * h), ang, z)
+    bgr = cv2.warpAffine(bgr, M, (w, h), borderMode=cv2.BORDER_REFLECT)
+    if rng.random() < 0.5:
+        bgr = cv2.flip(bgr, 1)
+    hsv = cv2.cvtColor(bgr, cv2.COLOR_BGR2HSV).astype(np.float32)
+    hsv[..., 0] = (hsv[..., 0] + rng.uniform(-4, 4)) % 180
+    hsv[..., 1] *= rng.uniform(0.5, 1.3)
+    hsv[..., 2] *= rng.uniform(0.6, 1.4)
+    bgr = cv2.cvtColor(np.clip(hsv, 0, 255).astype(np.uint8), cv2.COLOR_HSV2BGR)
+    # the wrist cameras: small, soft, compressed. Shrink to a random low res and back, blur, JPEG.
+    if synth or rng.random() < 0.3:
+        lo = rng.randint(24, 96)
+        bgr = cv2.resize(cv2.resize(bgr, (lo, lo), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA), (w, h))
+    if rng.random() < 0.4:
+        k = rng.choice([3, 5])
+        bgr = cv2.GaussianBlur(bgr, (k, k), 0)
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:   # motion blur
+        k = rng.randint(3, 9)
+        ker = np.zeros((k, k), np.float32)
+        ker[k // 2, :] = 1.0 / k
+        ker = cv2.warpAffine(ker, cv2.getRotationMatrix2D((k / 2, k / 2), rng.uniform(0, 180), 1), (k, k))
+        bgr = cv2.filter2D(bgr, -1, ker / max(ker.sum(), 1e-6))
+    if rng.random() < 0.6:
+        ok, enc = cv2.imencode(".jpg", bgr, [cv2.IMWRITE_JPEG_QUALITY, rng.randint(30, 90)])
+        bgr = cv2.imdecode(enc, cv2.IMREAD_COLOR)
+    if rng.random() < 0.3:
+        bgr = np.clip(bgr.astype(np.float32) + np.random.normal(0, rng.uniform(2, 10), bgr.shape), 0, 255).astype(np.uint8)
+    return bgr
+
+
+class DS(torch.utils.data.Dataset):
+    def __init__(self, items, train: bool, seed=0):
+        self.items, self.train = items, train
+        self.rng = random.Random(seed)
+        self.cache: dict[str, np.ndarray] = {}
+
+    def __len__(self):
+        return len(self.items)
+
+    def __getitem__(self, i):
+        path, y, synth = self.items[i]
+        im = self.cache.get(path)
+        if im is None:
+            im = cv2.imread(path)
+            if synth:
+                im = cv2.resize(im, (128, 128), interpolation=cv2.INTER_AREA)
+            self.cache[path] = im
+        if self.train:
+            im = augment(im, self.rng, synth)
+        return torch.from_numpy(prep(im)), y
+
+
+def real_items():
+    labels = {int(k): v for k, v in json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "real/labels.json"))).items()}
+    cands = {c["id"]: c for c in json.load(open(os.path.join(HERE, "real/cands.json")))}
+    out = []
+    for i, lab in labels.items():
+        if lab not in LABELS:
+            continue
+        out.append((os.path.join(HERE, f"real/cands/{i:05d}.png"), LABELS.index(lab), cands[i]["src"]))
+    return out
+
+
+def synth_items():
+    out = []
+    for y, lab in enumerate(LABELS):
+        for p in glob.glob(os.path.join(HERE, "synth", lab, "*.png")):
+            out.append((p, y, True))
+    return out
+
+
+def model():
+    m = torchvision.models.mobilenet_v3_small(weights=torchvision.models.MobileNet_V3_Small_Weights.DEFAULT)
+    m.classifier[3] = nn.Linear(m.classifier[3].in_features, len(LABELS))
+    return m
+
+
+def train(items, epochs, dev, seed=0):
+    torch.manual_seed(seed)
+    m = model().to(dev)
+    counts = np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in items], minlength=len(LABELS)).astype(np.float32)
+    w = torch.tensor(counts.sum() / np.maximum(counts, 1) / len(LABELS), device=dev)
+    dl = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(DS(items, True, seed), batch_size=128, shuffle=True, num_workers=0)
+    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(m.parameters(), lr=1e-3, weight_decay=1e-4)
+    sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.OneCycleLR(opt, 1e-3, total_steps=epochs * len(dl))
+    lossf = nn.CrossEntropyLoss(weight=w, label_smoothing=0.05)
+    for ep in range(epochs):
+        m.train()
+        tot = n = 0
+        for x, y in dl:
+            x, y = x.to(dev), y.to(dev)
+            loss = lossf(m(x), y)
+            opt.zero_grad()
+            loss.backward()
+            opt.step()
+            sched.step()
+            tot += loss.item() * len(y)
+            n += len(y)
+        print(f"  epoch {ep + 1}/{epochs} loss {tot / n:.3f}", flush=True)
+    return m.eval()
+
+
+@torch.no_grad()
+def predict(m, items, dev):
+    ds = DS(items, False)
+    xs = torch.stack([ds[i][0] for i in range(len(ds))])
+    out = []
+    for k in range(0, len(xs), 256):
+        out.append(torch.softmax(m(xs[k:k + 256].to(dev)), 1).cpu().numpy())
+    return np.concatenate(out) if out else np.zeros((0, len(LABELS)))
+
+
+def report(name, probs, ys):
+    pred = probs.argmax(1)
+    print(f"{name}: n={len(ys)} accuracy {np.mean(pred == ys):.3f}")
+    cm = np.zeros((3, 3), int)
+    for t, p in zip(ys, pred):
+        cm[t, p] += 1
+    print("   true\\pred " + " ".join(f"{l:>6}" for l in LABELS))
+    for t in range(3):
+        print(f"   {LABELS[t]:>9} " + " ".join(f"{cm[t, p]:>6}" for p in range(3)))
+    for k, l in enumerate(LABELS):
+        tp = cm[k, k]
+        prec = tp / max(cm[:, k].sum(), 1)
+        rec = tp / max(cm[k].sum(), 1)
+        print(f"   {l}: precision {prec:.2f} recall {rec:.2f} (n={cm[k].sum()})")
+    return cm
+
+
+def at_threshold(probs, ys, t):
+    """What the harness does: report an end only when its class prob >= t (else nothing)."""
+    pred = probs.argmax(1)
+    conf = probs.max(1)
+    for k, l in enumerate(LABELS[:2]):
+        said = (pred == k) & (conf >= t)
+        tp = int((said & (ys == k)).sum())
+        print(f"   at p>={t}: {l} precision {tp / max(int(said.sum()), 1):.2f} recall {tp / max(int((ys == k).sum()), 1):.2f}"
+              f" ({int(said.sum())} reported)")
+
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("--epochs", type=int, default=12)
+    ap.add_argument("--real-train", type=float, default=0.5, help="share of real source videos used for training")
+    ap.add_argument("--out", default=os.path.join(HERE, "cable_end.onnx"))
+    ap.add_argument("--final", choices=["real", "synth+real"], default="real", help="data for the shipped model")
+    ap.add_argument("--final-only", action="store_true", help="skip the held-out comparisons")
+    args = ap.parse_args()
+    dev = "cuda" if torch.cuda.is_available() else "cpu"
+    print("device", dev)
+    synth = synth_items()
+    real = real_items()
+    print("synthetic", np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in synth], minlength=3), "real", np.bincount([y for _, y, _ in real], minlength=3))
+    srcs = sorted({s for _, _, s in real if "table_photos" not in s})
+    rng = random.Random(0)
+    rng.shuffle(srcs)
+    train_src = set(srcs[: int(len(srcs) * args.real_train)])
+    real_train = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if s in train_src]
+    real_test = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if s not in train_src]
+    ys_test = np.array([y for _, y, _ in real_test])
+    photo = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real if "table_photos" in s]
+    ys_photo = np.array([y for _, y, _ in photo])
+    print(f"real train {len(real_train)} test {len(real_test)} (of which photos {len(photo)})")
+
+    def show(name, m):
+        p = predict(m, real_test, dev)
+        report(f"{name} on held-out real videos", p, ys_test)
+        at_threshold(p, ys_test, 0.7)
+        report(f"{name} on held-out photos", predict(m, photo, dev), ys_photo)
+
+    if not args.final_only:
+        print("== synthetic only")
+        show("synthetic-only", train(synth, args.epochs, dev))
+        print("== real only (real oversampled x8)")
+        show("real-only", train(real_train * 8, args.epochs, dev))
+        print("== synthetic + real (real oversampled x8)")
+        show("synthetic+real", train(synth + real_train * 8, args.epochs, dev))
+
+    # The shipped model: every labelled crop (all videos and the photos). 7 Nov, 15 epochs, held-out videos:
+    # real-only 0.944 accuracy vs synthetic+real 0.918 (photos 0.73 vs 0.56), so the renders stay out by default.
+    everything = [(p, y, False) for p, y, s in real]
+    print(f"== final ({args.final}): all {len(everything)} real crops")
+    m1 = train((synth if args.final == "synth+real" else []) + everything * 8, args.epochs, dev)
+    m1 = m1.cpu().eval()
+    torch.onnx.export(m1, torch.zeros(1, 3, SIZE, SIZE), args.out, input_names=["x"], output_names=["logits"],
+                      opset_version=17, dynamo=False)
+    net = cv2.dnn.readNetFromONNX(args.out)
+    xs = np.stack([DS(real_test, False)[i][0].numpy() for i in range(min(64, len(real_test)))])
+    net.setInput(xs)
+    o_cv = net.forward()
+    with torch.no_grad():
+        o_t = m1(torch.from_numpy(xs)).numpy()
+    print(f"ONNX via OpenCV vs torch: max |diff| {np.abs(o_cv - o_t).max():.2e}, argmax agree {np.mean(o_cv.argmax(1) == o_t.argmax(1)):.3f}")
+    print("wrote", args.out, f"{os.path.getsize(args.out) / 1e6:.1f} MB")
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
diff --git a/tools/check_monitor.py b/tools/check_monitor.py

 def main() -> int:

diff --git a/tools/fit_stage_curves.py b/tools/fit_stage_curves.py
new file mode 100644

+"""Fit each stage's success-vs-time curve from run logs, for harness.decide's planner.
+
+    python tools/fit_stage_curves.py RUNS_DIR [RUNS_DIR ...] [--out plans/stage_curves.json] [--horizon 295]
+
+Reads every segments.jsonl under the given directories. One episode = one stage in one run: the time from its
+first attempt's start to the end of its first successful attempt (recoveries and retries included), or, if it
+never succeeded, the total time spent on it (censored: we only know it took longer than that).
+
+Model (harness.decide.StageModel): F(tau) = p_max * (1 - exp(-(tau - t_min) / tau_c)) for tau > t_min. Fitted by
+maximum likelihood on a grid, with censoring: a success at tau adds log f(tau), a censored episode at c adds
+log(1 - F(c)). Stage names are grouped by stripping trailing digits and underscores (ball_1..ball_8 -> ball).
+
+Caveat: most logs so far are from Dream's MuJoCo YAM sim, not the Metal station. Fit station logs separately
+before trusting the timeouts for a match.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import math
+import sys
+from collections import defaultdict
+from pathlib import Path
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+from harness.decide import StageModel, plan_timeouts  # noqa: E402
+
+GRID_P = [i / 50 for i in range(1, 51)]
+GRID_TAU = [2.0 * 1.12 ** k for k in range(45)]          # 2 s .. ~330 s
+
+
+def episodes(dirs: list[Path]) -> dict[str, list[tuple[float, bool]]]:
+    """stage group -> [(time, succeeded)]"""
+    out: dict[str, list[tuple[float, bool]]] = defaultdict(list)
+    for d in dirs:
+        for seg_file in d.rglob("segments.jsonl"):
+            runs: dict[str, list[dict]] = defaultdict(list)
+            for line in seg_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace").splitlines():
+                try:
+                    r = json.loads(line)
+                except json.JSONDecodeError:
+                    continue
+                if r.get("t_start") is None or r.get("t_end") is None:
+                    continue
+                runs[r["stage"]].append(r)
+            for name, segs in runs.items():
+                segs.sort(key=lambda r: r["t_start"])
+                t0 = segs[0]["t_start"]
+                ok = next((r for r in segs if r.get("kind") == "stage" and r.get("outcome") == "ok"), None)
+                if ok is not None:
+                    out[group(name)].append((ok["t_end"] - t0, True))
+                else:
+                    out[group(name)].append((max(r["t_end"] for r in segs) - t0, False))
+    return out
+
+
+def group(name: str) -> str:
+    return name.rstrip("0123456789").rstrip("_") or name
+
+
+def loglik(eps: list[tuple[float, bool]], p: float, tau: float, tmin: float) -> float:
+    ll = 0.0
+    for t, ok in eps:
+        if ok:
+            if t <= tmin:
+                return -math.inf
+            ll += math.log(p) - (t - tmin) / tau - math.log(tau)
+        else:
+            F = 0.0 if t <= tmin else p * (1 - math.exp(-(t - tmin) / tau))
+            ll += math.log(max(1e-12, 1 - F))
+    return ll
+
+
+def fit(eps: list[tuple[float, bool]]) -> tuple[float, float, float, float]:
+    """(p_max, tau_c, t_min, loglik)"""
+    succ = [t for t, ok in eps if ok]
+    tmins = [0.0] if not succ else sorted({0.0, *(round(min(succ) * f, 2) for f in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95))})
+    best = (0.0, GRID_TAU[0], 0.0, -math.inf)
+    for tmin in tmins:
+        for p in GRID_P:
+            for tau in GRID_TAU:
+                ll = loglik(eps, p, tau, tmin)
+                if ll > best[3]:
+                    best = (p, tau, tmin, ll)
+    return best
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__.splitlines()[0])
+    ap.add_argument("dirs", nargs="+", type=Path)
+    ap.add_argument("--out", type=Path, default=None)
+    ap.add_argument("--min-episodes", type=int, default=5)
+    a = ap.parse_args(argv)
+    eps = episodes(a.dirs)
+    fitted = {}
+    print(f"{'stage':<24}{'n':>5}{'ok':>5}{'p_max':>8}{'tau_c s':>9}{'t_min s':>9}  median ok s")
+    for name, e in sorted(eps.items()):
+        if len(e) < a.min_episodes:
+            continue
+        p, tau, tmin, _ = fit(e)
+        succ = sorted(t for t, ok in e if ok)
+        med = f"{succ[len(succ) // 2]:.1f}" if succ else "-"
+        print(f"{name:<24}{len(e):>5}{len(succ):>5}{p:>8.2f}{tau:>9.1f}{tmin:>9.1f}  {med}")
+        fitted[name] = {"p_max": p, "tau_c": round(tau, 2), "t_min": tmin, "episodes": len(e), "successes": len(succ)}
+    if a.out:
+        a.out.write_text(json.dumps(fitted, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+        print("wrote", a.out)
+    return 0
+
+
+def models(curves: dict, spec: list[tuple[str, str, float, bool]]) -> list[StageModel]:
+    """[(stage name, curve group, points, gate)] -> StageModels for plan_timeouts."""
+    out = []
+    for name, grp, value, gate in spec:
+        c = curves[grp]
+        out.append(StageModel(name, value, c["p_max"], c["tau_c"], c["t_min"], gate))
+    return out
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main())
diff --git a/tools/joint_fault_data.py b/tools/joint_fault_data.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..7f9a7fb
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/joint_fault_data.py
@@ -0,0 +1,217 @@
+"""Training data for the learned joint fault detector (harness.joint_fault_net).
+
+Base motion is real: every joints.jsonl the sim matches recorded (Dream driving both YAM arms, 20 Hz). Each clip is
+6 s of one joint, shifted to sit inside a limit set (the YAM's or the Metal arm's), with sensor noise at the CAN
+reply's resolution. Then one of these is injected, and labels come from the resulting signals, not from any rule:
+
+  normal       nothing injected
+  near_miss    a fast move toward a limit that stops 1-12 deg short (hard negative: no fault)
+  limit_run    a move that runs past the limit (fault from the sample the joint is past it)
+  blocked      the joint stops (or crawls) while its command keeps going; torque climbs (fault from the contact)
+  oscillation  a growing 1.5-6 Hz oscillation (fault once its amplitude is over 2 deg)
+  overload     torque climbs to the joint's torque limit while the joint sags (fault at >= 90% of the limit)
+  overspeed    a burst over half the motor's top speed (fault while over it)
+
+`now[t]` is the fault state at t; `soon[t]` is any fault in [t, t + HORIZON]. Clips are split by match file, so a test
+clip's motion was never seen in training.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import glob
+import json
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+
+from harness.joint_fault_net import DT, HORIZON, WINDOW, features
+from harness.joint_observer import METAL_LIMITS_DEG, YAM_LIMITS_DEG
+
+JOINTS = ("shoulder_pan", "shoulder_lift", "elbow_flex", "wrist_flex", "wrist_yaw", "wrist_roll")
+YAM_TAU_MAX = 10.0
+METAL_TAU_MAX = {"shoulder_pan": 27.0, "shoulder_lift": 27.0, "elbow_flex": 27.0, "wrist_flex": 10.0,
+                 "wrist_yaw": 10.0, "wrist_roll": 10.0}
+VMAX_DEG_S = 573.0
+CLIP = 120                       # 6 s at 20 Hz
+KINDS = ("normal", "near_miss", "limit_run", "blocked", "oscillation", "overload", "overspeed")
+KIND_P = (0.30, 0.16, 0.14, 0.12, 0.10, 0.09, 0.09)
+FIELDS = 5                       # q, q_cmd, dq, tau, tau_cmd
+
+
+def load_series(paths: list[str]) -> list[np.ndarray]:
+    """Per file, arm and joint: (T, 5) arrays on a 20 Hz grid, one per stretch of increasing sim time."""
+    out = []
+    for path in paths:
+        rows = [json.loads(line) for line in open(path, encoding="utf-8") if line.strip()]
+        for arm in ("left", "right"):
+            for name in JOINTS:
+                t_list, v_list = [], []
+
+                def flush():
+                    if len(t_list) * DT >= CLIP * DT + 1.0:
+                        t = np.array(t_list)
+                        v = np.array(v_list, dtype=np.float64)
+                        grid = np.arange(t[0], t[-1], DT)
+                        if len(grid) >= CLIP:
+                            out.append(np.stack([np.interp(grid, t, v[:, k]) for k in range(FIELDS)], axis=1))
+                    t_list.clear()
+                    v_list.clear()
+
+                for r in rows:
+                    j = (r.get("arms") or {}).get(arm, {}).get(name)
+                    if j is None or any(x is None for x in j):
+                        continue
+                    t = r["t"]
+                    if t_list and t < t_list[-1] - 0.5:
+                        flush()
+                    if t_list and t <= t_list[-1]:
+                        continue
+                    t_list.append(t)
+                    v_list.append(j[:FIELDS])
+                flush()
+    return out
+
+
+def match_files(root: str) -> list[str]:
+    return sorted(glob.glob(str(Path(root) / "**" / "joints.jsonl"), recursive=True))
+
+
+def _minjerk(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
+    s = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
+    return 10 * s ** 3 - 15 * s ** 4 + 6 * s ** 5
+
+
+@dataclass
+class Clip:
+    raw: np.ndarray        # (CLIP, 5)
+    now: np.ndarray        # (CLIP,) bool
+    kind: str
+    lo: float
+    hi: float
+    tau_max: float
+    onset: int             # first fault sample, -1 when none
+
+
+def make_clip(series: list[np.ndarray], rng: np.random.Generator, kind: str | None = None) -> Clip:
+    base = series[rng.integers(len(series))]
+    start = rng.integers(0, len(base) - CLIP + 1)
+    q, qc, dq, tau, tauc = (base[start:start + CLIP, k].copy() for k in range(FIELDS))
+    name = JOINTS[rng.integers(len(JOINTS))]
+    if rng.random() < 0.5:
+        (lo, hi), tmax = YAM_LIMITS_DEG[name], YAM_TAU_MAX
+    else:
+        (lo, hi), tmax = METAL_LIMITS_DEG[name], METAL_TAU_MAX[name]
+    tau, tauc = tau * tmax / YAM_TAU_MAX, tauc * tmax / YAM_TAU_MAX
+    kind = kind or KINDS[rng.choice(len(KINDS), p=KIND_P)]
+    t = np.arange(CLIP)
+    s = int(rng.integers(WINDOW + 8, CLIP - 30))         # injection sample
+    # Place the clip in the range: random spot, or near a limit for the limit kinds.
+    span_lo, span_hi = q.min(), q.max()
+    side = 1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1
+    bound = hi if side > 0 else lo
+    if kind in ("limit_run", "near_miss"):
+        d0 = rng.uniform(5.0, 60.0)
+        shift = (bound - side * d0) - q[s]
+    else:
+        room_lo, room_hi = lo + 5 - span_lo, hi - 5 - span_hi
+        shift = rng.uniform(room_lo, room_hi) if room_hi > room_lo else (lo + hi) / 2 - q.mean()
+    q += shift
+    qc += shift
+    mask = np.zeros(CLIP, dtype=bool)
+    after = t >= s
+    n_after = CLIP - s
+    if kind == "limit_run":
+        v = rng.uniform(15.0, 250.0)
+        a = rng.uniform(0.0, 300.0)
+        tt = (t[after] - s) * DT
+        overshoot = rng.uniform(3.0, 25.0)
+        dist = abs(bound - q[s]) + overshoot
+        move = np.minimum(v * tt + 0.5 * a * tt ** 2, dist)
+        vel = np.where(v * tt + 0.5 * a * tt ** 2 < dist, v + a * tt, 0.0)
+        q[after] += side * move
+        qc[after] += side * np.minimum(move + v * 0.1, dist)
+        dq[after] += side * vel
+    elif kind == "near_miss":
+        stop = rng.uniform(1.0, 12.0)
+        dist = abs(bound - q[s]) - stop
+        n = int(rng.integers(8, 40))
+        prof = np.concatenate([_minjerk(n), np.ones(max(0, n_after - n))])[:n_after]
+        q[after] += side * dist * prof
+        qc[after] += side * dist * np.concatenate([_minjerk(max(2, n - 2)), np.ones(n_after)])[:n_after]
+        dq[after] += side * dist * np.gradient(prof) / DT
+    elif kind == "blocked":
+        crawl = rng.uniform(0.0, 0.25) if rng.random() < 0.4 else 0.0
+        if np.ptp(qc[s:s + 20]) < 10.0:
+            v = rng.uniform(15.0, 80.0) * (1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1)
+            qc[after] += v * (t[after] - s) * DT
+        hold = q[s] + crawl * (qc[after] - qc[s])
+        q[after] = hold
+        dq[after] = np.gradient(hold) / DT
+        push = np.clip(0.5 * tmax / 10.0 * (qc[after] - q[after]), -tmax, tmax)
+        tauc[after] = tauc[s] + push
+        # The motor delivers what is commanded, or (an outside push or a current limit) measures less or more.
+        tau[after] = tau[s] + push * (1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else rng.uniform(0.5, 1.4))
+        mask[after] = True
+    elif kind == "oscillation":
+        f = rng.uniform(1.5, 6.0)
+        amp_max = rng.uniform(3.0, 15.0)
+        ramp = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
+        tt = (t[after] - s) * DT
+        amp = amp_max * np.minimum(1.0, tt / ramp)
+        x = amp * np.sin(2 * np.pi * f * tt)
+        q[after] += x
+        dq[after] += amp * 2 * np.pi * f * np.cos(2 * np.pi * f * tt)
+        tau[after] -= 0.3 * tmax / 10.0 * x
+        tauc[after] -= 0.3 * tmax / 10.0 * x
+        mask[after] = amp >= 2.0
+    elif kind == "overload":
+        target = rng.uniform(0.95, 1.3) * tmax * (1 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1)
+        n = int(rng.integers(5, 30))
+        ramp = np.minimum(1.0, (t[after] - s) / n)
+        load = np.clip(tau[s] + (target - tau[s]) * ramp, -tmax, tmax)
+        tau[after] = load
+        tauc[after] = load
+        sag = rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0) * ramp * -np.sign(target)
+        q[after] += sag
+        dq[after] += np.gradient(sag) / DT
+    elif kind == "overspeed":
+        peak = rng.uniform(0.55, 1.0) * VMAX_DEG_S
+        disp = rng.uniform(30.0, 90.0)
+        n = max(3, int(round(1.875 * disp / peak / DT)))
+        room_up, room_dn = hi - q[s:].max(), q[s:].min() - lo
+        sign = 1 if room_up > room_dn else -1
+        prof = np.concatenate([_minjerk(n), np.ones(max(0, n_after - n))])[:n_after]
+        q[after] += sign * disp * prof
+        qc[after] += sign * disp * prof
+        dq[after] += sign * disp * np.gradient(prof) / DT
+    # CAN-resolution noise (Damiao MIT reply: q 16 bit over +-12.5 rad, dq 12 bit over +-30 rad/s, tau 12 bit)
+    q += rng.normal(0, 0.02, CLIP)
+    dq += rng.normal(0, 0.8, CLIP)
+    tau += rng.normal(0, 0.003 * tmax, CLIP)
+    # In the sim the measured torque is the commanded one; on a motor it is a current reading with its own error.
+    tau += rng.normal(0, rng.uniform(0.0, 0.03) * tmax, CLIP)
+    # Labels from the signals.
+    now = mask | (q > hi) | (q < lo) | (np.abs(dq) > 0.5 * VMAX_DEG_S) | (np.abs(tau) >= 0.9 * tmax)
+    idx = np.flatnonzero(now)
+    return Clip(np.stack([q, qc, dq, tau, tauc], axis=1), now, kind, lo, hi, tmax, int(idx[0]) if len(idx) else -1)
+
+
+def soon_labels(now: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
+    out = now.copy()
+    for k in range(1, HORIZON + 1):
+        out[:-k] |= now[k:]
+    return out
+
+
+def clip_features(c: Clip) -> np.ndarray:
+    return features(c.raw[:, 0], c.raw[:, 1], c.raw[:, 2], c.raw[:, 3], c.raw[:, 4], c.lo, c.hi, c.tau_max)
+
+
+def build(series: list[np.ndarray], n: int, seed: int) -> tuple[list[Clip], np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
+    """n clips; returns them with X (n, CLIP, 8), now (n, CLIP) and soon (n, CLIP)."""
+    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
+    clips = [make_clip(series, rng) for _ in range(n)]
+    X = np.stack([clip_features(c) for c in clips])
+    now = np.stack([c.now for c in clips])
+    return clips, X, now, np.stack([soon_labels(c.now) for c in clips])
diff --git a/tools/label_rollout.py b/tools/label_rollout.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..fa3163c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/label_rollout.py
@@ -0,0 +1,97 @@
+"""Label Dream's own record of a harness run for fine-tuning.
+
+Dream keeps each run's record (the station's archiver copies it to ~/rollouts/zeroshot_<time>/): last-run.csv (20 Hz
+joints and commands) and last-run_inputs/inputs.jsonl (every model input: frames, state and the prompt it was given).
+The harness knows what those seconds were for. This joins the two:
+
+    python tools/label_rollout.py runs/<stamp> <rollout dir or its inputs.jsonl>
+
+and writes <rollout>/harness_labels.jsonl, one line per model input, plus harness_segments.json:
+  stage, attempt     the harness stage and attempt the input belongs to
+  prompt_sent        what Dream was told (stage prompt, a monitor rewrite, a recovery, an operator prompt)
+  source             stage | rewrite | recovery | operator | tower
+  label              the clean task label for training: the stage's own prompt (rewrites map back to it)
+  outcome            ok | failed for the attempt the input is in; usable = ok and not a recovery
+
+The clocks differ (Dream's t_s is monotonic, the harness logs epoch time). They are aligned on the first /start:
+its harness time is the first model input's time, to about a second.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import pathlib
+import sys
+
+
+def _load(path: pathlib.Path) -> list[dict]:
+    return [json.loads(line) for line in path.read_text().splitlines() if line.strip()]
+
+
+def _source(why: str) -> str:
+    why = why or ""
+    if why.startswith("monitor next_prompt"):
+        return "rewrite"
+    if why.startswith("recovery"):
+        return "recovery"
+    if why == "operator":
+        return "operator"
+    return "stage"
+
+
+def label(run: pathlib.Path, rollout: pathlib.Path) -> tuple[list[dict], list[dict]]:
+    inputs_path = rollout if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout / "last-run_inputs" / "inputs.jsonl"
+    inputs = [r for r in _load(inputs_path) if r.get("kind") == "inputs"]
+    events = _load(run / "events.jsonl")
+    start = next(e for e in events if e["event"] == "start")
+    offset = start["t"] - inputs[0]["t_s"]   # harness epoch = Dream t_s + offset
+
+    # Spans: [t, stage, attempt, prompt, source, label], each running until the next prompt change.
+    spans, stage, attempt, base = [], None, 1, ""
+    outcomes: dict[tuple[str, int], str] = {}
+    for e in events:
+        ev = e["event"]
+        if ev == "stage_start":
+            stage, attempt, base = e["stage"], 1, e.get("prompt") or ""
+        elif ev == "tower_prompt" and e.get("next"):
+            base = e.get("prompt") or base
+        elif ev == "attempt_end":   # `retry` is logged after the retry's prompt, so count from here
+            outcomes[(e["stage"], e["attempt"])] = "ok" if e.get("ok") else "failed"
+            attempt = e["attempt"] + 1
+        elif ev == "prompt_change" and stage is not None:
+            src = _source(e.get("why"))
+            spans.append({"t": e["t"], "stage": stage, "attempt": attempt, "prompt_sent": e.get("prompt") or "",
+                          "source": src, "label": base if src in ("stage", "rewrite") else e.get("prompt") or ""})
+    rows = []
+    for r in inputs:
+        t = r["t_s"] + offset
+        span = next((s for s in reversed(spans) if s["t"] <= t + 0.5), None)
+        if span is None:
+            continue
+        outcome = outcomes.get((span["stage"], span["attempt"]), "unknown")
+        rows.append({"t_s": r["t_s"], **{k: span[k] for k in ("stage", "attempt", "prompt_sent", "source", "label")},
+                     "prompt_seen": (r.get("state") or {}).get("prompt"), "outcome": outcome,
+                     "usable": outcome == "ok" and span["source"] != "recovery"})
+    segs: list[dict] = []
+    for row in rows:
+        key = (row["stage"], row["attempt"])
+        if segs and (segs[-1]["stage"], segs[-1]["attempt"]) == key:
+            segs[-1]["t_end"] = row["t_s"]
+            segs[-1]["inputs"] += 1
+        else:
+            segs.append({"stage": row["stage"], "attempt": row["attempt"], "t_start": row["t_s"], "t_end": row["t_s"],
+                         "inputs": 1, "label": row["label"], "outcome": row["outcome"]})
+    return rows, segs
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    run, rollout = pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]), pathlib.Path(sys.argv[2])
+    rows, segs = label(run, rollout)
+    out = rollout.parent if rollout.suffix == ".jsonl" else rollout
+    (out / "harness_labels.jsonl").write_text("".join(json.dumps(r) + "\n" for r in rows))
+    (out / "harness_segments.json").write_text(json.dumps(segs, indent=2))
+    mismatch = sum(1 for r in rows if r["prompt_seen"] and r["prompt_seen"] != r["prompt_sent"])
+    for s in segs:
+        print(f"{s['outcome']:<7} {s['stage']:<18} a{s['attempt']}  {s['t_end'] - s['t_start']:5.0f} s  "
+              f"{s['inputs']:3d} inputs  {s['label'][:70]}")
+    print(f"{len(rows)} inputs labelled, {sum(r['usable'] for r in rows)} usable; prompt mismatches "
+          f"(alignment check, want ~0): {mismatch}  -> {out}/harness_labels.jsonl")

+"""How long the sim's camera renders take here (3 cameras per capture), and which settings make software rendering cheap.
+
+    MUJOCO_GL=osmesa python tools/render_bench.py      # software (cloud VMs)
+    MUJOCO_GL=egl python tools/render_bench.py         # GPU through /dev/dri (docker --device /dev/dri)
+"""
+import os
+import statistics
+import sys
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]))
+from sim_station import load_example  # noqa: E402
+from sim_scenes import hill_climb_table  # noqa: E402
+
+import mujoco  # noqa: E402
+
+y, dc = load_example(None)
+hill_climb_table.patch(y)
+env = y.YamEnv()
+s = env.state()
+out = {"gl": os.environ.get("MUJOCO_GL"), "threads": os.environ.get("LP_NUM_THREADS")}
+base = {"shadowsize": int(env.model.vis.quality.shadowsize), "offsamples": int(env.model.vis.quality.offsamples)}
+out["model"] = base
+
+
+def bench(label, w, h, shadows=True, reflection=True, offsamples=None, shadowsize=None):
+    if offsamples is not None:
+        env.model.vis.quality.offsamples = offsamples
+    if shadowsize is not None:
+        env.model.vis.quality.shadowsize = shadowsize
+    env.renderer = mujoco.Renderer(env.model, height=h, width=w)
+    env.renderer.scene.flags[mujoco.mjtRndFlag.mjRND_SHADOW] = shadows
+    env.renderer.scene.flags[mujoco.mjtRndFlag.mjRND_REFLECTION] = reflection
+    original = env.renderer.update_scene
+
+    def update(data, camera=None, scene_option=None):        # keep the flags across update_scene calls
+        original(data, camera=camera, scene_option=scene_option)
+        env.renderer.scene.flags[mujoco.mjtRndFlag.mjRND_SHADOW] = shadows
+        env.renderer.scene.flags[mujoco.mjtRndFlag.mjRND_REFLECTION] = reflection
+    env.renderer.update_scene = update
+    env.images()
+    times = []
+    for _ in range(10):
+        env.step(s["left"], s["right"])
+        t = time.perf_counter()
+        env.images()
+        times.append(time.perf_counter() - t)
+    ms = round(1000 * statistics.median(times), 1)
+    out[label] = ms
+    env.model.vis.quality.offsamples, env.model.vis.quality.shadowsize = base["offsamples"], base["shadowsize"]
+
+
+bench("640x480", 640, 480)
+bench("640x480_noshadow", 640, 480, shadows=False)
+bench("640x480_noreflect", 640, 480, reflection=False)
+bench("640x480_nomsaa", 640, 480, offsamples=0)
+bench("640x480_shadow1024", 640, 480, shadowsize=1024)
+bench("640x480_all_off", 640, 480, shadows=False, reflection=False, offsamples=0)
+bench("320x176_all_off", 320, 176, shadows=False, reflection=False, offsamples=0)
+t = time.perf_counter()
+for _ in range(40):
+    env.step(s["left"], s["right"])
+step = 1000 * (time.perf_counter() - t) / 40
+out["step_ms"] = round(step, 2)
+out["sim_rate_max"] = {k: round(min(1.0, 200 / (4 * step + v)), 2) for k, v in out.items()
+                       if isinstance(v, float) and k.startswith(("640", "320"))}
+print(out, flush=True)
+os._exit(0)          # skip MuJoCo's osmesa context teardown, which raises at exit

+"""One table of every live match: cloud Batch (gs://), the home cluster (ssh inspiron) and this laptop (local folder).
+
+    python tools/review_runs.py r1-            # job prefix
+Per match: where, job, match, wall s, sim s, sim rate since the first snapshot, stage, points, Dream Hz, round trip,
+Dream errors. Reads only the live/status.json + timeline.jsonl files the matches publish.
+"""
+import json
+import os
+import shutil
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+PREFIX = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "r1-"
+GCLOUD = shutil.which("gcloud") or "gcloud"
+LAPTOP = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "laptop_runs"
+CLUSTER = "/cluster/shared/dev/darsh/projects/hill_climb_sims/results"
+
+
+def row(where, path, timeline_lines):
+    rows = [json.loads(x) for x in timeline_lines if x.strip()]
+    if not rows:
+        return None
+    a, b = rows[0], rows[-1]
+    d = b.get("dream") or {}
+    dw = b["wall_s"] - a["wall_s"]
+    rate = round((b["sim_t"] - a["sim_t"]) / dw, 2) if dw > 0 and b.get("sim_t") is not None else None
+    parts = Path(path).parts
+    return (where, parts[-5] if len(parts) >= 5 else "?", parts[-3], b["wall_s"], b.get("sim_t"), rate, b.get("stage"),
+            b.get("points"), d.get("call_hz"), d.get("rtt_p50_s"), d.get("errors"))
+
+
+out = []
+listing = subprocess.run([GCLOUD, "storage", "ls", f"gs://hill-climb-sims-results/runs/{PREFIX}*/**/live/timeline.jsonl"],
+                         capture_output=True, text=True)
+for url in listing.stdout.split():
+    text = subprocess.run([GCLOUD, "storage", "cat", url], capture_output=True, text=True).stdout
+    r = row("cloud", url.replace("gs://", "/"), text.splitlines())
+    if r:
+        out.append(r)
+ssh = subprocess.run(["ssh", "inspiron", f"for f in $(find {CLUSTER} -path '*{PREFIX}*/live/timeline.jsonl'); do "
+                      f"echo \"### $f\"; cat \"$f\"; done"], capture_output=True, text=True)
+for chunk in ssh.stdout.split("### ")[1:]:
+    path, _, body = chunk.partition("\n")
+    r = row("cluster", path, body.splitlines())
+    if r:
+        out.append(r)
+for f in LAPTOP.glob(f"{PREFIX}*/**/live/timeline.jsonl"):
+    r = row("laptop", str(f), f.read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines())
+    if r:
+        out.append(r)
+print(f"{'where':8}{'job':18}{'match':14}{'wall':>6}{'sim':>7}{'rate':>6}  {'stage':16}{'pts':>4}{'Hz':>6}{'rtt':>6}{'err':>4}")
+for r in sorted(out, key=lambda r: (r[0], r[1], r[2])):
+    print(f"{r[0]:8}{r[1][:17]:18}{r[2][:13]:14}{r[3]:>6}{str(r[4]):>7}{str(r[5]):>6}  {str(r[6])[:15]:16}{str(r[7]):>4}"
+          f"{str(r[8]):>6}{str(r[9]):>6}{str(r[10]):>4}")
diff --git a/tools/start_poses.py b/tools/start_poses.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..9913da2
--- /dev/null
+++ b/tools/start_poses.py
@@ -0,0 +1,56 @@
+"""Build the start-pose reference for harness.start_pose from LeRobot teleop datasets.
+
+    python tools/start_poses.py OUT.json DATASET_DIR [DATASET_DIR ...]
+
+For every episode, the first frame of each task segment (a change of task_index starts a segment) becomes one
+reference start under that task's exact text. observation.state is [left 6 joints, left gripper, right 6 joints,
+right gripper] (the Metal/YAM follower layout in meta/info.json); grippers are dropped. Needs pyarrow.
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import json
+import sys
+from pathlib import Path
+
+
+def segment_starts(dataset: Path) -> list[tuple[str, dict]]:
+    import pyarrow.parquet as pq  # noqa: PLC0415 - only this tool needs it
+
+    info = json.loads((dataset / "meta" / "info.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
+    names = info["features"]["observation.state"]["names"]
+    idx = {side: [names.index(f"{side}_joint_{j}") for j in range(6)] for side in ("left", "right")}
+    tasks = {}
+    for line in (dataset / "meta" / "tasks.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8").splitlines():
+        if line.strip():
+            t = json.loads(line)
+            tasks[t["task_index"]] = t["task"]
+    out = []
+    for f in sorted((dataset / "data").glob("chunk-*/episode_*.parquet")):
+        cols = pq.read_table(f, columns=["observation.state", "task_index"]).to_pydict()
+        prev, seg = None, 0
+        for state, ti in zip(cols["observation.state"], cols["task_index"]):
+            if ti != prev:
+                start = {side: [round(float(state[i]), 4) for i in ids] for side, ids in idx.items()}
+                start["episode"] = f"{dataset.name}/{f.stem}/seg{seg}"
+                out.append((tasks[ti], start))
+                prev, seg = ti, seg + 1
+    return out
+
+
+def main(argv: list[str]) -> int:
+    if len(argv) < 2:
+        print(__doc__)
+        return 2
+    by: dict[str, list[dict]] = {}
+    for d in argv[1:]:
+        for task, start in segment_starts(Path(d)):
+            by.setdefault(task, []).append(start)
+    Path(argv[0]).write_text(json.dumps({"by_prompt": by}, indent=1) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
+    for task, starts in by.items():
+        print(f"{len(starts):3d}  {task}")
+    return 0
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    raise SystemExit(main(sys.argv[1:]))

+"""Readable trace of harness runs: one page per run (what was asked, what the monitor saw, why each stage ended)
+and an index of all runs.
+
+    python tools/trace_report.py                 # every run under runs/, then opens runs/index.html
+    python tools/trace_report.py runs/<stamp>    # one run
+
+Each run page lists, in time order: every prompt sent to Dream (and why), every monitor verdict with its reason,
+confidence and the frames it judged, attempt ends, skips, recoveries and the stop. Frames are the run's own files.
+"""
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import html
+import json
+import pathlib
+import subprocess
+import sys
+from datetime import datetime
+
+RUNS = pathlib.Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "runs"
+
+CSS = """
+:root { --bg:#fbfbfa; --text:#1d1d1b; --muted:#6b6b66; --line:#e4e4df; --ok:#2f7d4f; --bad:#b3261e; --prompt:#1f5fa8;
+  --rewrite-bg:#fff4dc; --rewrite:#8a5a00; --op-bg:#eef1ff; --op:#3b3fa8; }
+@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) { :root { --bg:#161615; --text:#ecece8; --muted:#9b9b95; --line:#30302c;
+  --ok:#5cc28a; --bad:#ff6b5e; --prompt:#7fb0ff; --rewrite-bg:#2e2412; --rewrite:#f0b54a; --op-bg:#1d2033; --op:#a9adff; } }
+* { box-sizing:border-box; }
+body { margin:0; padding:24px 16px 64px; background:var(--bg); color:var(--text);
+  font:14px/1.5 Inter, system-ui, -apple-system, "Segoe UI", sans-serif; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; }
+main { max-width:1100px; margin:0 auto; }
+h1 { font-size:20px; margin:0 0 4px; } h2 { font-size:15px; margin:28px 0 8px; }
+.muted { color:var(--muted); } a { color:inherit; }
+table { border-collapse:collapse; width:100%; } td, th { text-align:left; padding:6px 8px; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line);
+  vertical-align:top; } th { color:var(--muted); font-weight:500; }
+.ok { color:var(--ok); font-weight:600; } .bad { color:var(--bad); font-weight:600; }
+.row { display:grid; grid-template-columns:64px 130px 1fr; gap:12px; padding:10px 0; border-bottom:1px solid var(--line); }
+.t { color:var(--muted); } .kind { font-weight:600; }
+.prompt { color:var(--prompt); } .stage { border-top:2px solid var(--text); margin-top:18px; }
+.thumbs { display:flex; gap:6px; margin-top:6px; flex-wrap:wrap; }
+.thumbs img { height:96px; border-radius:4px; display:block; }
+.filter { margin:8px 0 0; } .filter label { margin-right:14px; }
+body.hide-wait .waiting { display:none; }
+.rewrite { background:var(--rewrite-bg); } .rewrite .kind { color:var(--rewrite); }
+.op { background:var(--op-bg); } .op .kind { color:var(--op); }
+.was { color:var(--muted); text-decoration:line-through; }
+"""
+
+KINDS = {"stage_start": "stage", "start": "prompt", "set_prompt": None, "prompt_change": "prompt", "verdict": "verdict",
+         "attempt_end": "attempt end", "stage_skipped": "skipped", "recovery": "recovery", "retry": "retry",
+         "entry_check": "entry check", "stop": "stop", "run_end": "run end", "guard_stop": "guard stop",
+         "prompt_rewrite": "monitor rewrote prompt", "tower_prompt": "tower prompt", "reset": "reset",
+         "auto_reset": "auto reset", "station_restart": "station restart", "operator_input": "operator said",
+         "operator_reply": "claude replied", "operator_action": "operator action", "guard_tripped": "guard tripped"}
+
+
+def _when(stamp: str) -> str:
+    try:
+        return datetime.strptime(stamp, "%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ").strftime("%d %b %H:%M UTC")
+    except ValueError:
+        return stamp
+
+
+def render(run: pathlib.Path) -> dict:
+    events = [json.loads(l) for l in (run / "events.jsonl").read_text().splitlines() if l.strip()]
+    if not events:
+        return {}
+    t0 = events[0]["t"]
+    summary = json.loads((run / "summary.json").read_text()) if (run / "summary.json").exists() else {}
+    end = next((e for e in reversed(events) if e["event"] in ("run_end", "stop")), {})
+    task = next((e.get("task") for e in events if e["event"] == "run_start"), "")
+    rows = []
+    for e in events:
+        kind = KINDS.get(e["event"])
+        if kind is None:
+            continue
+        t = f"+{e['t'] - t0:.0f}s"
+        stage = e.get("stage") or ""
+        body = ""
+        cls = "row"
+        if e["event"] == "stage_start":
+            cls += " stage"
+            body = f"<b>{html.escape(stage)}</b> <span class='prompt'>{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}</span>"
+        elif e["event"] == "prompt_rewrite":
+            cls += " rewrite"
+            body = (f"<span class='was'>“{html.escape(e.get('previous') or '')}”</span><br>"
+                    f"<span class='prompt'>“{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}”</span>"
+                    f"<div class='muted'>because: {html.escape(str(e.get('reason') or ''))}</div>")
+        elif e["event"] in ("operator_input", "operator_reply", "operator_action"):
+            cls += " op"
+            if e["event"] == "operator_input":
+                body = f"“{html.escape(e.get('text') or '')}”"
+            elif e["event"] == "operator_reply":
+                acts = e.get("actions") or {}
+                body = html.escape(str(e.get("reply") or "")) + (
+                    f"<div class='muted'>{html.escape(json.dumps(acts))}</div>" if acts else "")
+            else:
+                body = html.escape(f"{e.get('action')}: {e.get('note') or e.get('stages') or e.get('stage') or ''}")
+        elif e["event"] == "prompt_change" and str(e.get("why") or "").startswith("monitor next_prompt"):
+            continue   # shown by its prompt_rewrite row
+        elif e["event"] in ("start", "prompt_change"):
+            why = e.get("why")
+            body = f"<span class='prompt'>“{html.escape(e.get('prompt') or '')}”</span>" + (
+                f" <span class='muted'>({html.escape(str(why))})</span>" if why else "")
+        elif e["event"] in ("verdict", "entry_check"):
+            done = e.get("done")
+            if e.get("reason") == "monitor call in flight":
+                cls += " waiting"
+            mark = "<span class='ok'>done</span>" if done else "<span class='muted'>not yet</span>" if done is None \
+                else "<span class='bad'>failing</span>"
+            conf = e.get("confidence")
+            body = f"{mark} <span class='muted'>{'' if conf is None else f'conf {conf:.2f}'}</span> " \
+                   f"{html.escape(str(e.get('reason') or ''))}"
+            frames = e.get("frames") or []
+            if frames:
+                body += "<div class='thumbs'>" + "".join(
+                    f"<a href='{html.escape(f)}' target='_blank'><img loading='lazy' src='{html.escape(f)}' "
+                    f"title='{html.escape(f)}'></a>" for f in frames) + "</div>"
+        elif e["event"] == "attempt_end":
+            ok = e.get("ok")
+            body = ("<span class='ok'>passed</span> " if ok else "<span class='bad'>ended</span> ") + \
+                html.escape(str(e.get("reason") or ""))
+        else:
+            body = html.escape(str(e.get("reason") or e.get("why") or e.get("prompt") or e.get("mode") or ""))
+        rows.append(f"<div class='{cls}'><div class='t'>{t}</div><div class='kind'>{html.escape(kind)}"
+                    f"<div class='muted'>{html.escape(stage)}</div></div><div>{body}</div></div>")
+    stages = summary.get("stages") or []
+    stage_rows = "".join(
+        f"<tr><td>{html.escape(s['name'])}</td><td class='{'ok' if s.get('ok') else 'bad'}'>"
+        f"{'passed' if s.get('ok') else 'no'}</td><td>{s.get('attempts') or 0}</td>"
+        f"<td>{round(s.get('seconds') or 0)} s</td><td class='muted'>{html.escape(str(s.get('reason') or ''))[:160]}</td></tr>"
+        for s in stages if s.get("attempts"))
+    secs = round(end.get("seconds") or (events[-1]["t"] - t0))
+    page = f"""<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><title>Run {run.name}</title><style>{CSS}</style></head>
+<body><main><p><a href="../index.html">All runs</a></p>
+<h1>Run {html.escape(run.name)}</h1>
+<div class="muted">{_when(run.name)} · {secs} s · ended: {html.escape(str(end.get('reason') or end.get('why') or '?'))}</div>
+<p class="muted">{html.escape(task or '')}</p>
+<h2>Stages</h2><table><tr><th>Stage</th><th>Passed</th><th>Attempts</th><th>Time</th><th>How it ended</th></tr>{stage_rows}</table>
+<h2>Timeline</h2>
+<div class="filter muted"><label><input type="checkbox" id="w" onchange="document.body.classList.toggle('hide-wait', this.checked)">
+hide "monitor call in flight"</label></div>
+{''.join(rows)}
+</main></body></html>"""
+    (run / "trace.html").write_text(page)
+    passed = [s["name"] for s in stages if s.get("ok")]
+    return {"run": run.name, "secs": secs, "end": str(end.get("reason") or end.get("why") or ""), "passed": passed,
+            "tried": [s["name"] for s in stages if s.get("attempts")]}
+
+
+def index(items: list[dict]) -> pathlib.Path:
+    rows = "".join(
+        f"<tr><td><a href='{i['run']}/trace.html'>{_when(i['run'])}</a></td><td>{i['secs']} s</td>"
+        f"<td class='ok'>{html.escape(', '.join(i['passed'])) or '<span class=muted>none</span>'}</td>"
+        f"<td class='muted'>{html.escape(i['end'])[:120]}</td></tr>" for i in items)
+    path = RUNS / "index.html"
+    path.write_text(f"""<!doctype html><html lang="en"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
+<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1"><title>Harness runs</title><style>{CSS}</style></head>
+<body><main><h1>Harness runs</h1><p class="muted">Newest first. Click a run for its timeline.</p>
+<table><tr><th>Run</th><th>Length</th><th>Stages passed</th><th>How it ended</th></tr>{rows}</table></main></body></html>""")
+    return path
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    targets = [pathlib.Path(a) for a in sys.argv[1:]] or sorted(
+        (p for p in RUNS.iterdir() if (p / "events.jsonl").exists()), reverse=True)
+    items = [i for i in (render(t) for t in targets) if i]
+    out = index(items) if not sys.argv[1:] else targets[0] / "trace.html"
+    print(out)
+    if sys.platform == "darwin":
+        subprocess.run(["open", str(out)], check=False)
diff --git a/tools/train_joint_fault.py b/tools/train_joint_fault.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..e5dc1ec

@@ -0,0 +1,390 @@
+"""Architecture search and training for the learned joint fault detector (harness.joint_fault_net).
+
+    python tools/train_joint_fault.py --traces ../laptop_runs            # search, then retrain the best, export
+    python tools/train_joint_fault.py --traces ../laptop_runs --quick    # small run to check the pipeline
+
+Data: tools/joint_fault_data.py (real sim motion + injected faults), split by match file into train / val / test.
+Every candidate (MLP, dilated causal TCN, GRU, transformer encoder; several widths and depths) trains the same number
+of steps on the same windows. Its alarm threshold is picked on VAL so false alarms stay within --fa-budget per joint-
+hour; the search ranks candidates on VAL by mean "timely recall" over the fault kinds (alarm no later than 0.25 s
+after the fault starts), ties to fewer weights. The top candidates are retrained with more seeds and steps; the winner
+is scored once on TEST next to the rule observer (harness.joint_observer) on the same clips, and exported to
+harness/models/joint_fault_net.json with `beats_baseline` set from that comparison. Needs torch (training only).
+"""
+
+from __future__ import annotations
+
+import argparse
+import json
+import math
+import sys
+import time
+from pathlib import Path
+
+import numpy as np
+import torch
+from torch import nn
+
+ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
+sys.path.insert(0, str(ROOT))
+
+from harness.joint_fault_net import DT, FEATURES, HORIZON, N_FEATURES, WINDOW, FaultModel  # noqa: E402
+from harness.joint_observer import JointObserver, ObserverConfig  # noqa: E402
+from tools import joint_fault_data as D  # noqa: E402
+
+FAULT_KINDS = ("limit_run", "blocked", "oscillation", "overload", "overspeed")
+PERSIST = 2
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ models
+
+class MLP(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, hidden):
+        super().__init__()
+        dims = [WINDOW * N_FEATURES] + list(hidden)
+        for i in range(len(hidden)):
+            setattr(self, f"l{i}", nn.Linear(dims[i], dims[i + 1]))
+        self.n = len(hidden)
+        self.head = nn.Linear(dims[-1], 2)
+
+    def forward(self, x):
+        h = x.flatten(1)
+        for i in range(self.n):
+            h = torch.relu(getattr(self, f"l{i}")(h))
+        return self.head(h)
+
+
+class TCN(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, channels, dilations, kernel=3):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.dilations, self.kernel = list(dilations), kernel
+        cin = N_FEATURES
+        for i, d in enumerate(dilations):
+            setattr(self, f"c{i}", nn.Conv1d(cin, channels, kernel, dilation=d))
+            if cin != channels:
+                setattr(self, f"r{i}", nn.Conv1d(cin, channels, 1))
+            cin = channels
+        self.head = nn.Linear(2 * channels, 2)
+
+    def forward(self, x):
+        h = x.transpose(1, 2)
+        for i, d in enumerate(self.dilations):
+            y = torch.relu(getattr(self, f"c{i}")(nn.functional.pad(h, ((self.kernel - 1) * d, 0))))
+            res = getattr(self, f"r{i}")(h) if hasattr(self, f"r{i}") else h
+            h = y + res
+        return self.head(torch.cat([h[:, :, -1], h.mean(2)], 1))
+
+
+class GRU(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, hidden, layers):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.gru = nn.GRU(N_FEATURES, hidden, layers, batch_first=True)
+        self.head = nn.Linear(hidden, 2)
+
+    def forward(self, x):
+        out, _ = self.gru(x)
+        return self.head(out[:, -1])
+
+
+class Encoder(nn.Module):
+    def __init__(self, d_model, heads, layers, ff):
+        super().__init__()
+        self.inp = nn.Linear(N_FEATURES, d_model)
+        self.pos = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(WINDOW, d_model))
+        layer = nn.TransformerEncoderLayer(d_model, heads, ff, dropout=0.0, batch_first=True, norm_first=True)
+        self.enc = nn.TransformerEncoder(layer, layers, enable_nested_tensor=False)
+        self.norm = nn.LayerNorm(d_model)
+        self.head = nn.Linear(d_model, 2)
+
+    def forward(self, x):
+        h = self.enc(self.inp(x) + self.pos)
+        return self.head(self.norm(h)[:, -1])
+
+
+def build_model(family, cfg):
+    if family == "mlp":
+        return MLP(cfg["hidden"])
+    if family == "tcn":
+        return TCN(cfg["channels"], cfg["dilations"], cfg["kernel"])
+    if family == "gru":
+        return GRU(cfg["hidden"], cfg["layers"])
+    return Encoder(cfg["d_model"], cfg["heads"], cfg["layers"], cfg["ff"])
+
+
+CANDIDATES = [
+    ("mlp", {"hidden": []}),
+    ("mlp", {"hidden": [32]}),
+    ("mlp", {"hidden": [64, 32]}),
+    ("mlp", {"hidden": [128, 64]}),
+    ("tcn", {"channels": 8, "dilations": [1, 2, 4, 8], "kernel": 3}),
+    ("tcn", {"channels": 16, "dilations": [1, 2, 4], "kernel": 3}),
+    ("tcn", {"channels": 16, "dilations": [1, 2, 4, 8], "kernel": 3}),
+    ("tcn", {"channels": 32, "dilations": [1, 2, 4, 8], "kernel": 3}),
+    ("gru", {"hidden": 8, "layers": 1}),
+    ("gru", {"hidden": 16, "layers": 1}),
+    ("gru", {"hidden": 32, "layers": 1}),
+    ("gru", {"hidden": 32, "layers": 2}),
+    ("transformer", {"d_model": 16, "heads": 2, "layers": 1, "ff": 32}),
+    ("transformer", {"d_model": 32, "heads": 4, "layers": 1, "ff": 64}),
+    ("transformer", {"d_model": 32, "heads": 4, "layers": 2, "ff": 64}),
+]
+
+
+def n_params(model):
+    return sum(p.numel() for p in model.parameters())
+
+
+def name_of(family, cfg):
+    if family == "mlp":
+        return "mlp[" + "-".join(map(str, cfg["hidden"])) + "]" if cfg["hidden"] else "logistic"
+    if family == "tcn":
+        return f"tcn c{cfg['channels']} L{len(cfg['dilations'])}"
+    if family == "gru":
+        return f"gru h{cfg['hidden']} L{cfg['layers']}"
+    return f"enc d{cfg['d_model']} L{cfg['layers']} h{cfg['heads']}"
+
+
+# -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- data and training
+
+class Split:
+    def __init__(self, clips, X, now, soon):
+        self.clips, self.now, self.soon = clips, now, soon
+        self.X = torch.from_numpy(X)
+        self.windows = self.X.unfold(1, WINDOW, 1).permute(0, 1, 3, 2)   # (N, CLIP-W+1, W, F), a view
+        self.y = torch.from_numpy(np.stack([now, soon], -1)[:, WINDOW - 1:].astype(np.float32))
+
+    def batch(self, gen, size):
+        c = torch.randint(0, self.windows.shape[0], (size,), generator=gen)
+        e = torch.randint(0, self.windows.shape[1], (size,), generator=gen)
+        return self.windows[c, e], self.y[c, e]
+
+
+def train(family, cfg, data, norm, steps, seed, lr=3e-3, batch=512):
+    torch.manual_seed(seed)
+    model = build_model(family, cfg)
+    opt = torch.optim.AdamW(model.parameters(), lr=lr, weight_decay=1e-4)
+    sched = torch.optim.lr_scheduler.OneCycleLR(opt, max_lr=lr, total_steps=steps, pct_start=0.15)
+    pos = data.y.mean((0, 1)).clamp(1e-3, 1 - 1e-3)
+    loss_fn = nn.BCEWithLogitsLoss(pos_weight=((1 - pos) / pos).sqrt())
+    mean, std = (torch.from_numpy(a) for a in norm)
+    gen = torch.Generator().manual_seed(seed)
+    model.train()
+    for _ in range(steps):
+        x, y = data.batch(gen, batch)
+        loss = loss_fn(model((x - mean) / std), y)
+        opt.zero_grad()
+        loss.backward()
+        nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(model.parameters(), 1.0)
+        opt.step()
+        sched.step()
+    model.eval()
+    return model
+
+
+@torch.no_grad()
+def predict(model, data, norm):
+    mean, std = (torch.from_numpy(a) for a in norm)
+    N, E = data.windows.shape[:2]
+    out = []
+    flat = data.windows.reshape(N * E, WINDOW, N_FEATURES)
+    for i in range(0, flat.shape[0], 8192):
+        out.append(torch.sigmoid(model((flat[i:i + 8192] - mean) / std)))
+    p = torch.cat(out).reshape(N, E, 2).numpy()
+    full = np.zeros((N, D.CLIP, 2), dtype=np.float32)
+    full[:, WINDOW - 1:] = p
+    return full
+
+
+# --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- metrics
+
+def alarms(flags: np.ndarray, persist: int) -> np.ndarray:
+    run = np.zeros_like(flags, dtype=np.int32)
+    for t in range(flags.shape[1]):
+        run[:, t] = np.where(flags[:, t], (run[:, t - 1] if t else 0) + 1, 0)
+    return run >= persist
+
+
+def evaluate(alarm: np.ndarray, data: Split) -> dict:
+    """Event metrics for (N, CLIP) alarms on a split."""
+    N = alarm.shape[0]
+    hours = N * (D.CLIP - WINDOW) * DT / 3600.0
+    rising = alarm & ~np.concatenate([np.zeros((N, 1), bool), alarm[:, :-1]], 1)
+    clear = ~data.soon.copy()
+    for k in range(1, 11):                       # an alarm up to 0.5 s before the soon window is still not false
+        clear[:, :-k] &= ~data.soon[:, k:]
+    fa = int((rising & clear)[:, WINDOW:].sum())
+    per_kind = {}
+    for kind in FAULT_KINDS:
+        lat = []
+        for i, c in enumerate(data.clips):
+            if c.kind != kind or c.onset < WINDOW:
+                continue
+            lo = c.onset - HORIZON
+            hits = np.flatnonzero(alarm[i, lo: c.onset + 40])
+            lat.append((hits[0] + lo - c.onset) * DT if len(hits) else math.inf)
+        lat = np.array(lat)
+        found = np.isfinite(lat)
+        per_kind[kind] = {"n": int(len(lat)), "recall": float(found.mean()) if len(lat) else None,
+                          "timely": float((lat <= 0.25 + 1e-9).mean()) if len(lat) else None,
+                          "early": float((lat < 0).mean()) if len(lat) else None,
+                          "latency_med_s": float(np.median(lat[found])) if found.any() else None}
+    timely = [v["timely"] for v in per_kind.values() if v["timely"] is not None]
+    return {"fa_per_joint_hour": fa / hours, "timely_mean": float(np.mean(timely)), "kinds": per_kind}
+
+
+def auroc(score, label):
+    order = np.argsort(score)
+    ranks = np.empty(len(score))
+    ranks[order] = np.arange(1, len(score) + 1)
+    pos = label.sum()
+    neg = len(label) - pos
+    return float((ranks[label].sum() - pos * (pos + 1) / 2) / max(pos * neg, 1))
+
+
+def pick_threshold(p_soon, data, budget):
+    best = None
+    for thr in np.unique(np.concatenate([np.linspace(0.05, 0.95, 19), 1.0 - np.logspace(-5, -1.3, 40)])):
+        m = evaluate(alarms(p_soon >= thr, PERSIST), data)
+        if m["fa_per_joint_hour"] <= budget and (best is None or m["timely_mean"] > best[1]["timely_mean"]):
+            best = (float(thr), m)
+    return best or (0.995, evaluate(alarms(p_soon >= 0.995, PERSIST), data))
+
+
+def score_model(model, data, norm, budget, thr=None):
+    p = predict(model, data, norm)
+    if thr is None:
+        thr, m = pick_threshold(p[..., 1], data, budget)
+    else:
+        m = evaluate(alarms(p[..., 1] >= thr, PERSIST), data)
+    sl = slice(WINDOW - 1, None)
+    m["auroc_now"] = auroc(p[:, sl, 0].ravel(), data.now[:, sl].ravel())
+    m["auroc_soon"] = auroc(p[:, sl, 1].ravel(), data.soon[:, sl].ravel())
+    return thr, m, p
+
+
+RULE_KINDS = ("limit_breach", "limit_approach", "overspeed", "torque_saturation", "tracking_divergence",
+              "oscillation")
+
+
+def rule_alarms(data: Split) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
+    """The rule observer's alarms on each clip: 'any' and one array per alert kind."""
+    out = {k: np.zeros((len(data.clips), D.CLIP), dtype=bool) for k in ("any",) + RULE_KINDS}
+    for i, c in enumerate(data.clips):
+        obs = JointObserver(ObserverConfig(limits_deg={"j": (c.lo, c.hi)}, vmax_deg_s={"j": D.VMAX_DEG_S}))
+        for t in range(D.CLIP):
+            q, qc, dq, tau, tauc = c.raw[t]
+            for a in obs.observe(t * DT, "a", {"j": {"q_deg": q, "q_cmd_deg": qc, "dq_deg_s": dq, "tau_nm": tau,
+                                                       "tau_cmd_nm": tauc, "tau_max_nm": c.tau_max}}):
+                out["any"][i, t] = True
+                if a["kind"] in out:
+                    out[a["kind"]][i, t] = True
+    return out
+
+
+def numpy_step_us(spec, joints=12, reps=200):
+    m = FaultModel(spec)
+    x = np.random.default_rng(0).normal(size=(joints, WINDOW, N_FEATURES)).astype(np.float32)
+    m.probs(x)
+    t0 = time.perf_counter()
+    for _ in range(reps):
+        m.probs(x)
+    return (time.perf_counter() - t0) / reps * 1e6
+
+
+def export(model, family, cfg, norm, thr, extra):
+    return {"family": family, "config": cfg, "window": WINDOW, "rate_hz": 1 / DT, "features": list(FEATURES),
+            "horizon": HORIZON, "feature_mean": norm[0].tolist(), "feature_std": norm[1].tolist(),
+            "threshold": {"now": 0.5, "soon": thr}, "persist": PERSIST,
+            "weights": {k: v.detach().double().numpy().tolist() for k, v in model.state_dict().items()}, **extra}
+
+
+# ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ main
+
+def main():
+    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
+    ap.add_argument("--traces", default=str(ROOT.parent / "laptop_runs"))
+    ap.add_argument("--steps", type=int, default=3000)
+    ap.add_argument("--final-steps", type=int, default=8000)
+    ap.add_argument("--top", type=int, default=3)
+    ap.add_argument("--seeds", type=int, default=3)
+    ap.add_argument("--fa-budget", type=float, default=2.0, help="false alarms per joint-hour on val")
+    ap.add_argument("--quick", action="store_true")
+    ap.add_argument("--out", default=str(ROOT / "harness" / "models"))
+    args = ap.parse_args()
+    torch.set_num_threads(max(1, torch.get_num_threads()))
+    n_train, n_val, n_test = (2000, 800, 800) if args.quick else (16000, 4000, 4000)
+    if args.quick:
+        args.steps, args.final_steps, args.top, args.seeds = 300, 600, 2, 1
+    files = D.match_files(args.traces)
+    if len(files) < 3:
+        sys.exit(f"need at least 3 joints.jsonl under {args.traces}, found {len(files)}")
+    n_test_files = max(1, len(files) // 6)
+    split_files = {"train": files[: -2 * n_test_files], "val": files[-2 * n_test_files: -n_test_files],
+                   "test": files[-n_test_files:]}
+    data = {}
+    for (name, paths), n, seed in zip(split_files.items(), (n_train, n_val, n_test), (1, 2, 3)):
+        data[name] = Split(*D.build(D.load_series(paths), n, seed))
+        print(f"{name}: {len(paths)} files, {n} clips", flush=True)
+    flatX = data["train"].X.reshape(-1, N_FEATURES).numpy()
+    norm = (flatX.mean(0).astype(np.float32), np.maximum(flatX.std(0), 0.05).astype(np.float32))
+
+    results = []
+    print(f"{'model':22}{'weights':>8}{'s':>6}{'thr':>6}{'FA/jh':>7}{'timely':>8}{'AUC now':>8}{'AUC soon':>9}",
+          flush=True)
+    for family, cfg in CANDIDATES:
+        t0 = time.time()
+        model = train(family, cfg, data["train"], norm, args.steps, seed=0)
+        thr, m, _ = score_model(model, data["val"], norm, args.fa_budget)
+        r = {"name": name_of(family, cfg), "family": family, "config": cfg, "weights": n_params(model),
+             "train_s": round(time.time() - t0, 1), "val": m, "thr": thr}
+        results.append(r)
+        print(f"{r['name']:22}{r['weights']:>8}{r['train_s']:>6.0f}{thr:>6.2f}{m['fa_per_joint_hour']:>7.1f}"
+              f"{m['timely_mean']:>8.3f}{m['auroc_now']:>8.3f}{m['auroc_soon']:>9.3f}", flush=True)
+
+    ranked = sorted(results, key=lambda r: (-round(r["val"]["timely_mean"], 2), r["weights"]))
+    finals = []
+    for r in ranked[: args.top]:
+        runs = []
+        for seed in range(args.seeds):
+            model = train(r["family"], r["config"], data["train"], norm, args.final_steps, seed=100 + seed)
+            thr, m, _ = score_model(model, data["val"], norm, args.fa_budget)
+            runs.append((m["timely_mean"], thr, model, m))
+        vals = [x[0] for x in runs]
+        best = max(runs, key=lambda x: x[0])
+        finals.append({"r": r, "mean": float(np.mean(vals)), "sd": float(np.std(vals)), "best": best})
+        print(f"final {r['name']:22} val timely {np.mean(vals):.3f} +- {np.std(vals):.3f} over {len(vals)} seeds",
+              flush=True)
+    win = max(finals, key=lambda f: (round(f["mean"], 2), -f["r"]["weights"]))
+    _, thr, model, val_m = win["best"]
+    _, test_m, _ = score_model(model, data["test"], norm, args.fa_budget, thr=thr)
+    rules = rule_alarms(data["test"])
+    rule_m = evaluate(rules["any"], data["test"])
+    rule_m["fa_by_kind"] = {k: round(evaluate(rules[k], data["test"])["fa_per_joint_hour"], 1) for k in RULE_KINDS}
+    print("rule false alarms per joint-hour by kind:", rule_m["fa_by_kind"], flush=True)
+    beats = test_m["timely_mean"] > rule_m["timely_mean"] and \
+        test_m["fa_per_joint_hour"] <= max(rule_m["fa_per_joint_hour"], args.fa_budget * 1.5)
+    spec = export(model, win["r"]["family"], win["r"]["config"], norm, thr, {
+        "name": win["r"]["name"], "n_weights": win["r"]["weights"], "beats_baseline": bool(beats),
+        "trained_on": {k: [Path(p).parent.name for p in v] for k, v in split_files.items()},
+        "test": test_m, "rule_test": rule_m, "val": val_m,
+        "created": time.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ", time.gmtime())})
+    spec["numpy_us_per_step_12_joints"] = round(numpy_step_us(spec), 1)
+    out = Path(args.out)
+    out.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
+    (out / "joint_fault_net.json").write_text(json.dumps(spec), encoding="utf-8")
+    (out / "joint_fault_search.json").write_text(json.dumps({
+        "candidates": results, "finals": [{"name": f["r"]["name"], "weights": f["r"]["weights"],
+                                           "val_timely_mean": f["mean"], "val_timely_sd": f["sd"]}
+                                          for f in finals],
+        "winner": win["r"]["name"], "test": test_m, "rule_test": rule_m, "beats_baseline": bool(beats),
+        "numpy_us_per_step_12_joints": spec["numpy_us_per_step_12_joints"]}, indent=1), encoding="utf-8")
+    print(f"\nwinner {win['r']['name']} ({win['r']['weights']} weights), threshold {thr:.2f}")
+    for label, m in (("learned", test_m), ("rules", rule_m)):
+        print(f"TEST {label:8} FA/joint-h {m['fa_per_joint_hour']:.1f}  timely {m['timely_mean']:.3f}  " +
+              "  ".join(f"{k} {v['timely']:.2f}/{v['early']:.2f}/{v['latency_med_s']}" for k, v in m["kinds"].items()
+                        if v["timely"] is not None))
+    print("beats_baseline", beats, " numpy us/step (12 joints)", spec["numpy_us_per_step_12_joints"])
+
+
+if __name__ == "__main__":
+    main()
